### [1] [Singlet-triplet superconductivity from repulsive interactions in a $p$-wave magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14609)
*Hanbit Oh,Laura Classen,Daniel F. Agterberg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用配分重整化群（pRG）研究p波磁体中范霍夫奇点附近电子在纯排斥相互作用下的不稳定性竞争，发现超导是主导不稳定性，并预言了单重态-三重态混合配对乃至有限动量PDW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期提出的p波磁体（宿主奇宇称自旋劈裂）启发，作者希望理解奇宇称自旋劈裂的对称性如何调控范霍夫奇点附近电子在局域排斥相互作用下的各类不稳定性竞争，并探索排斥相互作用驱动非常规超导（乃至拓扑超导）的可能性。

Method: 采用配分重整化群（pRG）方法。首先分析一个简单的单子格p波磁体模型；随后拓展到更现实的双子格模型，以考察子格自由度对配对通道竞争的影响。

Result: 在单子格模型中，整个相图内超导均为纯排斥相互作用下的主导不稳定性；超导态一般为自旋单重态与自旋三重态的混合，当Tc远小于范霍夫电子与其反演伙伴之间的能量偏移时，趋于等权重混合。在双子格模型中，超导仍为主导不稳定性，但子格自由度导致多种单重态-三重态配对通道之间的竞争，甚至出现有限动量的等自旋配对密度波（PDW）。

Conclusion: 该机制广泛适用于奇宇称磁体，为节点拓扑超导开辟了一条自然途径。

Abstract: Motivated by recent proposals of $p$-wave magnets, which host odd-parity spin splitting, we study electrons near Van Hove singularities with local repulsive interactions. We demonstrate how the symmetry of the $p$-wave spin splitting governs the competition among instabilities. Using a parquet renormalization group (pRG) analysis of a simple one-sublattice model of a $p$-wave magnet, we find that superconductivity is the leading instability over the entire phase diagram for purely repulsive interactions. The resulting superconducting state is generically a mixture of spin-singlet and spin-triplet pairing and approaches an equal-weight mixture when $T_c$ is small compared with the energy offset between each Van Hove electron and its inversion partner. Extending the pRG analysis to a more realistic two-sublattice model, we find that superconductivity remains the leading instability, while the sublattice degree of freedom leads to a competition among various singlet-triplet pairing channels and even a finite-momentum equal-spin pair-density wave. Our mechanism applies broadly to odd-parity magnets and opens a natural route to nodal topological superconductivity.

</details>


### [2] [Superlattice Stabilization of the Topological Quantum Hall Ferromagnet in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14679)
*Tiago Cecchi,Ahmed Khalifa,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，可调的超晶格势能重新组织石墨烯中自旋与谷自由度的竞争，在电荷中性点无需大面内磁场或强介电屏蔽即可稳定铁磁量子霍尔拓扑绝缘体（即螺旋量子霍尔态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯中自旋序与谷序之间的竞争使其成为在量子霍尔区域工程化关联拓扑相的多功能平台，但稳定铁磁量子霍尔拓扑绝缘体通常需要大面内磁场或强库仑屏蔽。作者希望寻找一种更可控的手段来调节这种竞争并稳定目标拓扑相。

Method: 首先通过一种能计入超晶格势的广义重整化群（RG）程序计算决定不同竞争态之间选择的相互作用各向异性；随后在重正化各向异性基础上进行自洽的动量空间Hartree-Fock计算，以确定绝缘自旋极化铁磁态的稳定区间；最后通过绝热连接验证该铁磁态与量子霍尔铁磁体的拓扑等价性。

Result: 超晶格势抑制了与自旋极化竞争的各向异性，同时引入的色散倾向于部分自旋极化的补偿半金属；自洽计算确立了绝缘自旋极化铁磁态的稳定参数区间，并证实其与量子霍尔铁磁体绝热连接、具有拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 超晶格势可作为控制相互作用石墨烯相图的一种手段，并可用于实现其螺旋量子霍尔区域，为工程化关联拓扑相提供了新途径。

Abstract: Competition between spin and valley orders makes graphene a versatile platform for engineering correlated topological phases in the quantum Hall regime. We show that a tunable superlattice potential reorganizes this competition, and stabilizes the ferromagnetic quantum Hall topological insulator at charge neutrality without requiring a large in-plane magnetic field or strong dielectric suppression of Coulomb interactions. The interaction anisotropies responsible for choosing between different competing states are first computed by a generalized renormalization group procedure that accounts for the superlattice potential. We find that the potential suppresses anisotropies that compete with spin-polarization. At the same time, the potential introduces dispersion which prefers a partly spin-polarized compensated semi-metal. Therefore, we carry out a self-consistent momentum-space Hartree-Fock computation with renormalized aniostropies to establish the regime of stability of the insulating spin-polarized ferromagnet. Finally, we verify that this ferromagnet is adiabatically connected to the quantum Hall ferromagnet, thereby confirming its topological character. Our results establish superlattice potentials as a means of controlling the phase diagram of interacting graphene and accessing its helical quantum Hall regime.

</details>


### [3] [Termination-selective Kondo hybridization of topological surface states in CeCuAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15022)
*Bo Chen,Chen Zhang,Hao Liu,Qi-Yi Wu,Zhong-Tuo Fu,Ying Zhou,Chun-Hui Lyu,Yang Luo,Zi-Jie Xu,Hai-Long Deng,Zheng-Tai Liu,Mao Ye,Zhen-Hua Chen,Yao-Bo Huang,Sheng-Tao Cui,Zhe Sun,Da-Wei Shen,Jun He,Yu-Xia Duan,Jian-Qiao Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用不同表面终结分辨的ARPES、Ce 4d→4f共振光电子能谱及表面电子结构计算，研究Kondo晶格化合物CeCuAs₂，发现在CeAs2终结面上Dirac表面态与近E_F的弱色散Ce 4f带共存，并观测到终结面选择性的f-Dirac杂化及拓扑表面态中4f特征的发展，表明表面终结可作为调控Kondo关联与拓扑边界电子相互作用的微观手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kondo晶格中的强关联拓扑态是凝聚态物理前沿，边界电子与局域f态之间的耦合如何重塑低能电子结构尚缺乏直接的谱学证据；作者旨在阐明表面终结方式是否能选择性地调控Kondo关联与拓扑边界电子的相互关系。

Method: 结合终结面分辨的角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）、Ce 4d→4f共振光电子能谱，以及表面电子结构计算；通过特征费米面几何与终结面特定计算，将两种不同表面电子结构分别归属于As1和CeAs2终结面；利用共振光电子能谱识别低能4f特征，并在变温条件下分析4f与Dirac区域的谱权重演化。

Result: CeAs2终结面上，与计算的非平庸体拓扑相关的Dirac表面态与近E_F的弱色散Ce 4f带共存。共振光电子能谱确认了低能4f特征，并在Dirac支穿越的能量区间内揭示了与晶体场相关的精细结构。降温过程中，4f与Dirac区域的谱权重均增强，而参考导带区域变化较小，表明存在终结面选择性的f-Dirac杂化以及拓扑表面态中4f特征的发展。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了Kondo晶格CeCuAs₂中终结面选择性f-Dirac杂化及拓扑表面态4f特征的谱学证据，确立了表面终结作为微观调控Kondo关联与拓扑边界电子相互作用的手段。

Abstract: Strongly correlated topological states in Kondo lattices represent a frontier in condensed matter physics, where the coupling between boundary electrons and localized f states can reshape the low-energy electronic structure. Here, we combine termination-resolved angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), Ce 4$d \rightarrow 4f$ resonant photoemission, and surface electronic structure calculations to investigate the Kondo lattice compound CeCuAs$_2$. Two distinct surface electronic structures are assigned to the As1 and CeAs2 terminations through their characteristic Fermi-surface geometries and termination-specific calculations. On CeAs2, a Dirac surface state associated with the calculated nontrivial bulk topology coexists with a weakly dispersive Ce 4$f$ band near $E_F$. Resonant photoemission identifies the low-energy 4$f$ character and reveals crystal-field-related fine structure in the energy range traversed by the Dirac branch. On cooling, the 4$f$ and Dirac regions both gain spectral weight, whereas a reference conduction-band region changes much less. Together, these observations provide spectroscopic evidence for termination-selective $f$-Dirac hybridization and the development of 4$f$ character in a topological surface state. Our results identify surface termination as a microscopic means of controlling the interplay between Kondo correlations and topological boundary electrons.

</details>


### [4] [Maximally Chiral Eigenstate from Inverse Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15081)
*Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种有序量子态，其波在一个方向上具有有限速度，而在相反方向上能量呈指数般极小；作者为自旋-1/2海森堡本征态指定该谱并重建出非局域哈密顿量，揭示了方向性动力学、奇异热力学及可调束缚对等反直觉特性，并提议用动量分辨自旋谱学、弱场磁强计和低温熵测量加以探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过设计与谱相匹配的相互作用，构造出具有方向性动力学（单向有限波速、反向指数抑制能量）和异常热力学响应的有序量子态，从而建立“相互作用设计↔方向性动力学↔非寻常热力学”之间的联系。

Method: 以自旋-1/2海森堡本征态为出发点，预先指定具有单向有限波速、反向指数小能量的能谱，反向重构非局域哈密顿量；利用热力学构造选出有序、携带电流的态作为基态；在此基础上计算磁矩、弱场响应、每自旋熵的温度标度，并进行自旋响应计算以研究束缚对。进一步加入交换作用与更平滑的模式选择，以恢复最低能量处的二次型激发。

Result: 结果显示：该有序载流态磁矩保持有限，但对弱场的响应斜率发散；每自旋熵仅随温度对数倒数消失（T→0），且精确系数含有黄金比；自旋响应揭示出一个能量可调而强度不变的束缚对。加入交换项与更平滑的模式选择后，最低能区可恢复二次型激发谱。

Conclusion: 相互作用的设计可以系统性地产生方向性动力学与非寻常热力学行为，这些反直觉特征可通过动量分辨自旋谱学、弱场磁强计及低温熵测量实验加以验证，为量子材料中定向输运与奇异热力学的工程化提供了新思路。

Abstract: We show that an ordered quantum state can support waves with a finite speed in one direction and exponentially small energies in the other. We prescribe this spectrum for a spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg eigenstate and reconstruct a nonlocal Hamiltonian. The thermodynamic construction selects the ordered, current-carrying state as a ground state. Its magnetic moment remains finite while its response to a weak field has a divergent slope. The entropy per spin vanishes only inversely with a logarithm of temperature, with an exact coefficient containing the golden ratio. Our spin-response calculations reveal a bound pair whose energy can be tuned without changing its intensity. Added exchange and smoother mode selection restore quadratic excitations at the lowest energies. These results connect the design of interactions to directional dynamics and unusual thermodynamics. We propose momentum-resolved spin spectroscopy, weak-field magnetometry, and low-temperature entropy measurements to probe these counterintuitive signatures.

</details>


### [5] [Higher-order topology induced by altermagnetism in quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15139)
*Xingmin Huo,Xingchuan Zhu,Xingyu Ma,Shiping Feng,Huaiming Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究在无平移对称性的准晶体（Thue-Morse 与 Ammann-Beenker）中，由 d 波交变磁序与二维拓扑绝缘体耦合诱导的高阶拓扑相，并发现 d(x²−y²) 波交变磁可打开边缘能隙并产生零能高阶角态，而传统反铁磁与 d(xy) 波交变磁则不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁性（AM）具有零净磁化和动量依赖的自旋劈裂，是探索奇异量子现象的重要平台。近期研究将 AM 概念推广到准晶体，但在缺乏平移对称性的系统中，动量依赖的自旋劈裂机制尚不清晰，亟需理解 AM 序在非周期结构中的独特作用。

Method: 作者研究 Thue-Morse 和 Ammann-Beenker 两类准晶体中，d 波准晶 AM 序与二维拓扑绝缘体耦合所驱动的高阶拓扑相（HOTPs）的出现，重点比较 d(x²−y²) 波、d(xy) 波 AM 与传统反铁磁构型在打开边缘能隙和诱导零能高阶角态方面的差异。

Result: 研究发现 d(x²−y²) 波 AM 表现出类似铁磁的行为，能够打开边缘能隙并诱导零能高阶拓扑角态；而传统反铁磁和 d(xy) 波 AM 构型均无法诱导高阶拓扑能隙。高阶拓扑相的出现取决于交变磁对称性及其与晶格角点的对齐方式。

Conclusion: 交变磁对称性及其与晶格角点的对齐是实现高阶拓扑相的关键，高阶拓扑相的出现可作为在非周期系统中识别交变磁序的特征信号，从而为无平移对称性体系中的 AM 研究提供了新的判据。

Abstract: Altermagnetism (AM), a novel magnetic phase featuring zero net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin splitting, provides a compelling platform for exploring exotic quantum phenomena. While recent studies have extended the concept of AM to quasicrystals, the momentum-dependent spin splitting in the absence of translational symmetry remains inadequately understood. Here, we investigate the emergence of higher-order topological phases (HOTPs) in Thue-Morse and Ammann-Beenker quasicrystals, driven by the coupling between $d$-wave quasicrystal AM orders and a two-dimensional topological insulator. We reveal that the $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave AM exhibits a ferromagnetism-like aspect in opening edge gaps and inducing zero-energy higher-order topological corner states. In contrast, conventional antiferromagnetic and $d_{xy}$-wave AM configurations are unable to induce the higher-order topological gaps. These findings highlight the pivotal role of altermagnetic symmetry and its alignment with lattice corners in the realization of HOTPs and establish that the emergence of HOTPs can serve as a signature for identifying altermagnetic orders in aperiodic systems.

</details>


### [6] [Magnetism on emergent molecular-orbital lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15141)
*Zhan Liu,Chenrui Wang,Chen Fang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究固体中强杂化形成分子轨道（MO）所构成的涌现晶格上的新型磁性，指出同一原子晶格、同一填充下可通过调节跃迁参数实现从三角到蜂窝分子轨道晶格的转变，从而出现受挫与不受挫的反铁磁性，并提出可通过低能动力学结构因子及分子形状因子（零点和跨布里渊区比值）来实验验证此类分子轨道磁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固体中，原子轨道间强杂化会形成覆盖多个原子团簇的分子轨道，其中心构成与原子晶格不同的涌现晶格。对于部分填充的分子轨道，电子相互作用可能在涌现晶格上诱导新型磁性，其局域磁矩来自分子轨道而非单个原子。作者旨在揭示这种涌现晶格对磁性（尤其是受挫性质）的调控机制，并提供可实验观测的判据。

Method: 采用红宝石晶格（ruby lattice）紧束缚 Hubbard 模型，通过改变跃迁参数使涌现分子轨道晶格在三角与蜂窝晶格之间变化，研究部分填充分子轨道上的磁性；并用随机相近似（RPA）计算低能动力学结构因子，分析分子形状因子（分子自旋密度的傅里叶变换）的零点及其在不同布里渊区间的比值关系。

Result: 研究发现，在同一原子晶格和同一填充下，随跃迁参数改变、涌现分子轨道晶格从三角变为蜂窝时，可分别出现受挫与不受挫的反铁磁性。低能动力学结构因子受分子形状因子调制：形状因子的零点会抑制低能谱权重（与分子磁矩形成的具体磁序无关）；对低能积分结构因子，其在分子形状因子零点附近呈现极小值，且在不同布里渊区中选取等价动量处的比值与形状因子平方的比值一致。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，固体中分子轨道的涌现晶格几何可通过跃迁参数调控，从而在同一原子晶格和填充下产生不同受挫性质的反铁磁序；低能动力学结构因子及其分子形状因子特征（零点抑制与跨布里渊区的平方比值关系）为实验验证分子轨道磁性提供了普适性判据。

Abstract: In solids, strong hybridization between nearby atomic orbitals forms molecular orbitals (MOs), the range of which covers a cluster of atoms. The centers of molecular orbitals form an emergent lattice generally different from the atomic lattice. For partially filled MOs, electron interaction may induce a new type of magnetism on this emergent lattice where the local moments come from the molecular orbitals instead of single atoms. In this work, we show in a ruby-lattice tight-binding Hubbard model that frustrated and unfrustrated antiferromagnetism may appear on the same atomic lattice and at the same filling, as the emergent molecular-orbital lattice changes from triangular to honeycomb upon changing the hopping parameters. Moreover, we show that general molecular orbital magnetism may be experimentally verified in the low-energy dynamic structure factor: the molecular form factors, as the Fourier transforms of the molecular spin-density, modulate the low-energy dynamic structure factor across different Brillouin zones. Zeros of these form factors suppress low energy spectral weight irrespective of the magnetic order formed by the molecular moments. This is exemplified by random phase approximation calculations for the ruby-lattice model: the structure factor integrated over low energies exhibits minima around molecular form-factor zeros, while its ratios between selected equivalent momenta in different Brillouin zones agree with ratios of squared form factors.

</details>


### [7] [Kitaev Exchange and Altermagnetism in the Double Perovskite Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$: An Ab Initio Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15152)
*Alexey V. Ushakov,Sergey V. Strelsov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理研究指出，Co基双钙钛矿Sr₂CoTeO₆具有中等强度的铁磁Kitaev交换（|K|≈J/3）并伴随强阻挫，同时被识别为具有显著非相对论自旋劈裂的交变磁体（altermagnet），可能产生横向压磁效应与非平庸磁光响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于Sr₂CoTeO₆展现出非平凡的磁学性质，作者旨在从第一性原理角度揭示其微观磁相互作用（特别是Kitaev交换）的本质，并阐明其磁对称性分类及其可能带来的新奇物理效应。

Method: 采用从头算密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，分析最近邻Co²⁺离子间的各向异性交换相互作用，提取铁磁Kitaev项K与Heisenberg项J的相对大小，并结合面心立方晶格几何讨论交换阻挫；同时通过磁对称性分析判别altermagnetic（交变磁）序，并计算非相对论自旋劈裂。

Result: 计算表明最近邻铁磁Kitaev交换强度约为|K|≈J/3，与面心立方晶格共同导致显著的交换阻挫，使Sr₂CoTeO₆归入具有中等Kitaev相互作用强度的Kitaev材料之列；此外该材料被确认是altermagnetic，具有可观的非相对论自旋劈裂，可诱导横向压磁效应和非平庸磁光响应。

Conclusion: Sr₂CoTeO₆是兼具中等强度Kitaev相互作用（铁磁型）与altermagnetic序的多功能磁性材料，其阻挫与交变磁自旋劈裂共同赋予其横向压磁及磁光等新效应，值得进一步实验与理论研究。

Abstract: The Co$^{2+}$-based double perovskite Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ attracts attention due to its nontrivial magnetic properties. \textit{Ab initio} density functional theory calculations reveal a substantial ferromagnetic Kitaev exchange $|K|\sim J/3$ between nearest neighbors that, combined with the face-centered cubic lattice, leads to pronounced exchange frustration and places Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ among Kitaev materials with moderate Kitaev interaction strength. In addition, Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ is found to be altermagnetic with a sizable non-relativistic spin splitting, which can give rise to a transverse piezomagnetic effect and a nontrivial magneto-optical response.

</details>


### [8] [Multifractal collective order : Application to multifractal superconductivity in dodecagonal quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15446)

Here, we demonstrate that deterministic aperiodic geometry can instead stabilize multifractal collective order. We formulate a general diagnostic applicable to superconducting, magnetic, and charge channels, combining direct scaling analysis of the order parameter with three criteria: its spatial hierarchy must be dictated by the underlying geometry, emerge in the leading ordering instability, and persist in the self-consistent ordered state below a single mean-field ordering onset. We demonstrate that superconductivity on the experimentally resolved Ti network of a dodecagonal oxide quasicrystal satisfies all three criteria. The resulting condensate exhibits multifractal scaling over the resolved range of spatial scales, retaining its hierarchical structure throughout the ordered phase and channeling flux-induced equilibrium currents along geometry-locked rings.

Abstract: The realization of integer and fractional Chern insulators in moiré systems provides a lattice setting for exploring how the structure of Bloch wave functions influences the interplay between topology and disorder. Using a realistic three-orbital model of twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, we compare Hall and bulk transport under onsite-potential, displacement-field, and twist-angle disorder. At equal bare disorder strength, onsite-potential disorder produces broad integer plateaus with nearly direct transitions, whereas displacement-field disorder yields narrow plateaus separated by conducting regions with pronounced Hall-resistance dips. These contrasting behaviors arise from the layer composition of the Bloch states: contributions from different layer under displacement-field disorder partially cancel, weakening the bulk localization. Abstract: We reformulate active-orbital selection as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), analogous to decision-making in autonomous driving and robot control, and couple it with quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) to emulate expert-like selection of active orbitals. QUBO constructs complete candidate spaces under fixed electron and orbital counts, while the POMDP selects successive calculations, updates its belief from each completed result, and thereby corrects and refines the active orbitals. This belief-aware sequential procedure enables continuous active-space selection rather than one-shot threshold-based filtering, so the space can adapt as the calculation proceeds. We demonstrate the approach for valence-space construction in small molecules, spectral calculation of metal compound TiF$_3$, and active-space refinement of 1,2-dioxetanone around conical intersections. Recent $^{55}$Mn nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) study suggested the emergence of a spin-singlet paired state below $\sim$10 K. From the detailed NMR/NQR spectrum measurements and the DFT calculations, a quadrupolar frequency of $ν_{\rm Q}$ = 4.6$\sim$4.8 MHz and an asymmetry parameter of $η$ = 0.45$\sim$0.48 as well as the principal axes of electric field gradient EFG for the Mn site were determined. The temperature dependence of Knight shift ($K$) shows a similar temperature dependence of the DC magnetic susceptibility $χ$ except for the low-temperature region below $\sim$ 50 K where $K$ is almost constant while $χ$ keeps increasing, which suggests that the increase in $χ$ at low temperatures is not intrinsic. $^{55}$Mn spin-lattice relaxation rate divided by temperature ($1/T_1T$) is found to be nearly constant, confirming the metallic state of Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$ from a microscopic point of view. Furthermore, no peak in $1/T_1T$ is observed near 10 K, and neither the Knight shift nor the linewidth shows any reductions at low temperatures. These results suggest no clear signature for the spin-singlet pairing around 10 K in Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$.

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) spin vorticity models (SVMs) have been proposed to realize an exotic 2-form U(1) classical spin liquid (CSL), characterized by a zero-curl constraint, string excitations, and deconfined fluctuating membranes. A previous numerical study [PRB 111, 064417 (2025)] uncovered a puzzle in the pyrochlore lattice SVM: despite exhibiting pinch points characteristic of a U(1) spin liquid, it also develops weak all-in--all-out (AIAO) order. Here, we show that the emergence of the 2-form CSL in 3D SVMs is preempted by an intrinsic instability towards entropy-driven height locking. We establish this by resolving the ground state constraints using a height mapping, which we further relate to a 3-state Potts antiferromagnet. This identifies the pyrochlore's weak AIAO order as permutationally symmetric sublattice (PSS) order, albeit with an exceptionally weak locking strength. We extend the SVM to lattices of corner-sharing octahedra and cubes, where we identify the alternative broken-sublattice-symmetry (BSS) order, a hidden order without magnetic Bragg peaks coexisting with collective paramagnetism. All three models undergo a 3D Ising transition consistent with a symmetry-allowed locking potential in the coarse-grained height theory. The spin liquid persists over intermediate length and temperature scales, but a critical Coulomb ground state ensemble fails to materialize, its correlations cut off by the finite locking length. These findings illustrate how microscopic constraints can determine the ultimate stability of emergent gauge theories in frustrated spin systems.

Abstract: One of the most important challenges in condensed matter physics is understanding the macroscopic properties of strange metals, particularly why their resistivity remains linear in temperature over several decades. We propose that a (2+1)d theory of fermions coupled to underdamped quantum-critical bosons through a spatially disordered interaction provides this long-sought-after theoretical explanation. The key novelty of our work is to establish the appropriate separation of scales so that the bosons are underdamped. Using this framework, we obtain two distinct forms for the fermionic self-energy: a conformal-limit solution, $Σ\sim\sqrtω$, and a marginal Fermi-liquid solution, $Σ\simω\log(1/ω)$, corresponding to regimes above and below the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit, respectively. These solutions produce Planckian scattering, exhibit exact $T$-linear resistivity, $ω/T$ scaling in the optical conductivity, as well as $\sim T\log(1/T)$ heat capacity at low temperatures. Moreover, our theory naturally unifies strange- and bad-metal phenomena within a single framework, suggesting that we are now approaching a new epoch in understanding the physics of strongly correlated matter.

Abstract: The last couple of decades have seen enormous theoretical and experimental research efforts aimed at finding quantum spin liquids (QSLs) in geometrically frustrated (GF) magnetic materials. Experimental demonstrations of QSLs are challenging due to the indirect character of possible QSL signatures and the presence of impurities in materials, which may destroy or obscure a QSL. At the same time, theoretical literature often lacks consensus about the ground states of quantum spins on even the simplest GF magnetic lattices. We summarize experimental evidence that in the impurity-free limit, available GF magnetic materials that do not develop magnetic order at low temperatures would form {\it a quantum singlet liquid}, i.e. a disordered state of non-magnetic excitations. We further confirm the existence of such a singlet liquid and its gapless character with theoretical arguments. Such a liquid manifests itself in the thermodynamics of GF magnets while having no magnetic features at low temperatures.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反常刻画大多依赖连续时空描述或涌现的洛伦兹不变性，难以在纯微观晶格层面系统处理“晶体对称性与内禀对称性相互交织”的反常（如调制对称性、SPT–LSM约束等）。作者旨在给出不假设连续描述或洛伦兹不变性的微观反常理论，从而严格刻画施加晶体对称性后哪些内禀对称性反常在晶格上仍可存活或被平凡化。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic characterization of crystalline symmetry anomalies in bosonic lattice systems in (1+1) and (2+1) dimensions with a combination of crystalline and finite unitary internal symmetry. These results establish a crystalline equivalence principle for bosonic anomalies at the microscopic level, without assuming a continuum description or emergent Lorentz invariance. We prove that a nontrivial total anomaly index obstructs any symmetric invertible phase. The framework incorporates modulated symmetries where spatial symmetries permute internal symmetries. Abstract: Identifying the microscopic interactions governing a quantum system is essential for developing predictive theories of materials and validating quantum technologies. Despite considerable progress, learning Hamiltonians from realistic experimental data remains a challenge. Abstract: Magnetic Raman spectroscopy is sensitive to quasiparticle quantum geometry, yet microscopic Raman vertices generally prevent a direct geometric interpretation. Here we establish a symmetry-based extraction principle for two-dimensional quantum magnets with two-band quasiparticle excitations. Opposite circular-polarization spectra separate vertex-weighted quantum-metric and Berry-curvature contractions. Near a $C_3$-symmetric massive Dirac valley, symmetry fixes the leading Raman and velocity doublets up to a common scale, allowing the unknown Raman strength and intensity normalization to cancel from their spectral ratio. For an inversion-related pair of massive Dirac cones, the normalized counter-circular Raman asymmetry at frequency threshold $Ω_{\rm th}$ is fixed by the Chern number and follows a universal near-threshold frequency scaling. This yields a local spectroscopic readout without absolute-intensity calibration or frequency integration. We demonstrate the principle for Majorana fermions in the field-induced honeycomb Kitaev spin liquid and for topological magnons in a honeycomb ferromagnet, and identify the conditions for resolving it experimentally.

Abstract: The triangular lattice compound Na$_2$BaNi(PO$_4$)$_2$ has been recently established as a strong candidate for realizing the spin-nematic physics. In this theoretical work, we study the finite-temperature properties and the dynamics of a spin-1 triangular lattice model, which reproduces the spin-nematic physics and provides insights relevant to the compound. We show that a small biquadratic interaction can be used for stabilizing the SN phases in the semiclassical approach with SU(3) coherent states, which allows for a mathematical parameterization of the spin-nematic wave functions. With a semiclassical Monte Carlo calculation, the finite temperature transitions into the spin-nematic phases are shown to be related to BKT transitions associated with the proliferation of half vortices in the original spin-1 model. Using quantum Monte Carlo calculations with a low-energy effective model, the phase boundaries are determined and found to agree qualitatively with the semiclassical analysis. Within the spin wave approach, the low-energy spin dynamics can be captured equally well by either the spin-1 model with a small biquadratic term, or the effective spin-$1/2$ model. The accidental degeneracy of the semiclassical approach at zero field is revealed by explicit analytical parameterization, and is shown to be associated with the pseudo-Goldstone mode observed in the spin dynamics.

Abstract: Driving lattice vibrations provides a direct route to manipulating electronic correlations through nonlinear electron-phonon coupling. Finding suitable driving fields, however, requires control over the coupled dynamics beyond a simple choice of pulse amplitude and frequency. For the dissipation-free model at zero Hubbard interaction, all the methods increase double occupation, with the B-spline raising the mean total double occupation of approximately 30%. The NMR spectra exhibit no additional splitting down to 2 K and remain fully described by one quartet and two doublets associated with the three types of molecular sites, A (=A$^{\prime}$), B and C sites, demonstrating the preservation of inversion symmetry. Furthermore, no anomalous line broadening or critical enhancement of the nuclear spin-lattice relaxation rate is observed, indicating the absence of magnetic ordering and time-reversal-symmetry breaking. Analysis of the Knight shifts yields site-specific spin susceptibilities satisfying $χ_{\mathrm{C}} > χ_{\mathrm{A}} > χ_{\mathrm{B}}$, a characteristic feature of tilted Dirac-cone electronic states as observed in $α$-(BEDT-TTF)$_2$I$_3$ under pressure. However, the faint field-induced ferrimagnetic-like spin excitations observed in $α$-(BEDT-TTF)$_2$I$_3$, which are ascribed to the gapless tilted Dirac cones, are absent in $α$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$. These results demonstrate that the insulating ground state of $α$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$ is a symmetry-preserving gapped Dirac state and provide microscopic constraints on proposed correlated topological phases in this molecular Dirac system.

Abstract: We investigate an unconstrained $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory coupled to fermions on the triangular lattice at half filling, using auxiliary field quantum Monte Carlo simulations. The triangular geometry gives rise to a global $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry that acts as an Ising symmetry on the plaquette fluxes. At weak transverse field $h$, this symmetry is spontaneously broken, stabilizing a vison crystal. We argue that this phase is deconfined: fermions, charged under an emergent $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge field, propagate in an ordered $π$-flux background and form a Dirac semimetal. Increasing the transverse field triggers a first-order quantum phase transition to a confined $s$-wave superfluid, at which confinement and global symmetry breaking occur simultaneously. This contrasts with the square lattice, where the corresponding transition is continuous. We extend our analysis to finite temperature and determine the thermal phase boundaries of both ordered phases. The superfluid melts through a Kosterlitz--Thouless transition, whereas the vison crystal undergoes a thermal Ising transition. Together, our results establish the $T \times h$ phase diagram and demonstrate how the triangular geometry leads to qualitative changes in both the phases and the criticality of the model.

Abstract: $O(N)$ models provide a versatile framework for describing continuous phase transitions in a wide variety of physical systems. We investigate an extension of these models arising from order parameters that transform under interlocked internal and spatial symmetries which describe transitions towards non-polar structural order. Such order occurs, for instance, in dipolar antiferromagnets, spin-orbit-coupled magnets, non-polar ferroics, and certain nematic systems. The resulting effective theory is an $O(d)$ model with an additional symmetry-allowed kinetic term $h(\partial_iφ_i)^2$, which modifies the relative stiffness of longitudinal and transverse fluctuations. We study both thermal and quantum phase transitions within this $O(d)$ model using the functional renormalization group (FRG). In contrast to conventional $O(N)$ models, the interlocking of internal and spatial symmetries distinguishes spatial and imaginary-time derivatives, such that the quantum model is not simply obtained from the thermal one by a dimensional extension $d\rightarrow d+1$. We derive and analyze the corresponding FRG flow equations, paying particular attention to the tensor structure of the two-point function, which is essential for consistently extracting the flow of the kinetic terms. Our analysis shows that the additional kinetic coupling is irrelevant at the $O(d)$ Wilson-Fisher fixed point in both thermal and quantum cases, where quantum-to-classical correspondence thus re-emerges. However, its small but sizable correction-to-scaling exponent gives rise to substantial corrections to scaling in the vicinity of the critical point.

This two-magnon signal decays with a time constant of $28\pm11$ ps, exceeding the previously reported two-magnon dephasing time by more than two orders of magnitude. Result: 1. 弱、强耦合下相互作用边界附近出现无能隙模式，中间耦合进入完全有能隙的SMG相；超过临界相互作用强度后出现Z₂对称性自发破缺。2. 在全部三个边界相中，均发现量子化电荷泵浦，且每磁通量子的泵浦电荷相同。3. 泵浦电荷的空间分布随相的不同而变化。4. SMG相中，有能隙边界端点附近出现相互作用诱导的味道混合流，而弱、强耦合下均无此流，揭示了SMG相特有的电荷再分布机制。5. 提出与(3+1)d标准模型拓扑轻子生成的类比：3-4-5-0模型的无反常手性U(1)对称性类似于标准模型的重子数减轻子数(B−L)对称性。

Conclusion: 量子化电荷泵浦在包括SMG有能隙相在内的所有三个边界相中均得以保持，每磁通量子泵浦电荷相同，表明泵浦的量子化不受边界相互作用能隙化的影响，尽管泵浦电荷的空间分布会随相改变。SMG相中端点出现的味道混合流揭示了该相特有的电荷再分布机制。此外，该模型与(3+1)d标准模型拓扑轻子生成之间的类比表明，电荷泵浦可关联于沿经典定义但量子反常方向的连续形变，而离散子群保持为严格的量子对称性。

Abstract: Quantized charge pumping in quantum Hall systems is a bulk topological property, typically manifesting on gapless boundaries as spectral flow through chiral edge modes upon flux insertion. When the boundary is anomaly-free, it can be symmetrically gapped by symmetric mass generation (SMG) interactions. However, the effects on quantized charge pumping at an interacting gapped boundary are unclear. We address this using density-matrix renormalization group simulations of a four-leg ladder quasi-(2+1)d realization of the (1+1)d 3-4-5-0 lattice chiral fermion model with six-fermion interactions at one edge. We find gapless modes near the interacting edge for both weak and strong couplings, but the edge can be in a fully gapped SMG phase at intermediate coupling. We also identify spontaneous breaking of the model's $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry above a critical interaction strength. Across all three boundary phases, we find quantized charge pumping with the same pumped charge per flux quantum. The spatial distribution of the pumped charge varies between phases. Besides charge pumping, we study how a single injected fermion propagates under open boundary conditions. In the SMG phase, an interaction-induced flavor-mixing current develops at the endpoints of the gapped edge, which is absent in the weak- and strong-coupling limits. This current reveals a charge-redistribution mechanism unique to the SMG phase. Additionally, we propose an analogy between charge pumping in this model and a topological leptogenesis scenario in the (3+1)d Standard Model. The anomaly-free chiral U(1) symmetries of the 3-4-5-0 model play a role analogous to the baryon-minus-lepton symmetry of the Standard Model. In both settings, charge pumping can be associated with a continuous deformation along a classically defined but quantum-anomalous direction, while a discrete subgroup remains an exact quantum symmetry.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 O(N) 模型在临界温度及其以下时，局部自旋坐标的极限分布是否可由高斯场逼近，并精确刻画逼近误差随 N、M 与坐标个数 p 的衰减阶，从而严格化"大 N 极限下局部自旋近似独立高斯"这一统计物理直觉。

Method: 作者利用平均球模型（spherical model）的谱分析与有限体积谱位移 s_M，结合高斯自由场的各向异性缩放，对 O(N) 模型局部自旋坐标构造高斯逼近目标：坐标场相互独立，每个分量由缩放后的各向异性高斯自由场（临界以下另加独立的空间常数高斯项）构成；随后以 1-Wasserstein 度量估计误差，并针对含最近邻星形（一个格点及其 d 个正方向近邻）的窗口、周期无穷体积累积态等不同情形给出最优阶。

Result: 对含最近邻星形的窗口，当且仅当 p=o(N) 且 √p s_M→0 时误差趋于零；对任意周期无穷体积累积态，任意非空固定窗口上当且仅当 p=o(N)。当 d≥5 且逆温度超过临界值至少一个固定正量时，估计改进为 C(p/N+√p M^{-d})。对固定 p，星形窗口上的精确阶为 N^{-1}+M^{-d}，而周期累积态中每个非空固定窗口上则为 N^{-1}。

Conclusion: 本文严格证明了 O(N) 模型的局部自旋坐标在大体积极限下可由独立分量的各向异性高斯自由场逼近，并给出了误差关于 (N,M,p) 的精确率，包括临界及临界以下情形、星形窗口与周期累积态，以及 d≥5 高维下的改进界，从而完整刻画了局部高斯逼近的适用范围与收敛速率。

Abstract: We prove a volume-uniform Gaussian approximation for local spin coordinates in the zero-field nearest-neighbour $O(N)$ model on periodic boxes of side $M$ in $d\ge3$, at and below the critical temperature of the limiting spherical model. On a fixed spatial window, for sufficiently large $M,N$, the unnormalized $1$-Wasserstein error for the first $p\le N$ coordinates is at most $C\sqrt p (N^{-1/2}+s_M)$, where $s_M\to0$ is the spectral shift of the finite-volume mean spherical model. The estimate is uniform on compact ranges of positive directional couplings and inverse temperatures. The target has independent coordinate fields, each a scaled anisotropic Gaussian free field with an independent spatially constant Gaussian term below criticality.
  On a window containing a nearest-neighbour star (a site and its $d$ positive-direction neighbours), the error tends to zero as $M,N\to\infty$ exactly when $p=o(N)$ and $\sqrt p s_M\to0$. For any choice of periodic infinite-volume accumulation states, the error on any nonempty fixed window tends to zero as $N\to\infty$ exactly when $p=o(N)$. In $d\ge5$, when the inverse temperature exceeds its critical value by at least a fixed positive amount, the bound improves to $C(p/N+\sqrt p M^{-d})$. For fixed $p$, the sharp order is $N^{-1}+M^{-d}$ on star-containing windows and $N^{-1}$ on every nonempty fixed window in periodic accumulation states.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低温展开按壁面积排序伊辛滴（droplets），但同一阶会出现不同横向跨度（transverse spans），破坏了对结构的精细分类。作者希望改按"给定两个跨度下、超出最小壁面积的量"来分层，以便获得更清晰、可精确计数的组合结构。

Result: 每个固定超出层的二元生成函数均为有理分式，分母因子仅限 1-x、1-y 与 1-x-y；文中显式给出超出零至三全宽度的完整层，其几何组成可归因于已知的多联骨牌枚举。精确格点校验印证了公式。经认证的低温对比显示在一个采样点上取得适度的混合精度增益，但评估代价更高。

Conclusion: 结果确立了精确的层结构；但对无界超出的求和（summation over unbounded excess）仍是独立待解问题。

Abstract: A low-temperature expansion orders Ising droplets by wall area, but droplets with different transverse spans can occur at the same order. We instead organize the zero-field simple-cubic model by the excess above the minimum wall area compatible with two prescribed spans. We prove that every fixed excess layer, including all logarithmic cover corrections and Euler inversion, has a rational bivariate generating function with denominator factors restricted to 1 - x, 1 - y and 1 - x - y. The proof compresses repeated coordinate cuts into bounded decorations and assigns each unbounded interval to one monotone chain. A finite controller then enforces induced contacts and arbitrary cover-color reuse. We explicitly assemble the complete layers of excess zero through three over all widths, attributing their known geometric constituents to earlier polyomino enumeration. Exact lattice checks corroborate the formulas. A certified low-temperature comparison shows a modest hybrid accuracy gain at one sampling point and higher evaluation cost. The result establishes an exact layer structure; summation over unbounded excess remains a separate problem.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心极限定理只适用于弱相关随机变量，而临界点处的长程关联使其失效；临界涨落的典型行为（标度不变性）虽已广为人知，但稀有涨落（概率极小的极端事件）因传统数值方法难以触及而长期缺乏研究。核心问题是：临界点上是否仍存在一个普适的大偏差极限定理？

Method: 作者采用大规模 Tethered（拴系）蒙特卡洛模拟，将序参量涨落的探测概率降至 10^{-200} 量级，从而进入常规方法无法到达的极远尾部；同时利用共形场论推导出猜想的精确普适振幅，并对不同微观模型的归一化尾部进行塌缩验证。

Result: 数值结果显示，归一化后的序参量涨落极远尾部塌缩到同一条渐近曲线上，该曲线被二维伊辛普适类中不同微观模型共享；从数值中提取的普适振幅与共形场论推导的猜想精确结果相容。

Conclusion: 临界涨落的稀有事件遵循一个普适渐近定律，指向一种适用于临界系统的"广义中心极限定理"；这填补了大偏差在临界点处缺乏直接数值证据的空白，并验证了理论猜想的正确性。

Abstract: The central limit theorem is among the most fundamental principles in the natural sciences, governing the fluctuations of sums of weakly correlated random variables through Gaussian statistics. At critical points, however, long-range correlations invalidate this paradigm, raising a fundamental question: does any general limit theorem survive at criticality? Although scale invariance has long been recognised as the defining feature of typical critical fluctuations, the behaviour of rare fluctuations has remained largely unexplored, as their extraordinarily small probabilities place them beyond the reach of conventional numerical methods. Recent theoretical advances suggest that universal asymptotic laws {may govern large deviations in systems at criticality}, yet direct numerical evidence has been lacking. Here we provide the first such evidence in the two-dimensional Ising universality class. Using large-scale Tethered Monte Carlo simulations, we probe order-parameter fluctuations with probabilities as small as $10^{-200}$ and show that, once normalised by their characteristic width, the far tails collapse onto a single asymptotic law shared by distinct microscopic models in the same universality class. This collapse is governed by a universal amplitude, whose numerical extraction is compatible with the conjectured exact result we derive from conformal field theory, and points towards a generalised central limit theorem for critical fluctuations.

</details>

Abstract: Manipulating polymer chains by means of experimental instruments such as optical tweezers has helped understand better the effects of molecular forces in macromolecular structures. The recently introduced microcanonical inflection-point analysis method is employed to identify and classify structural phases of the system. Specific order parameters help characterize the transition behavior in response to the present pulling force. We show that, upon increasing the pulling strength, the coil-globule transition turns from second to first order, then merges with the liquid-solid transition. An intermediate phase, separating the coil and globule phases, is identified for certain force strengths. Clear evidence of the Gough-Joule effect is found as well. Transitions do not occur at all if the pulling force exceeds a certain threshold value.

### [41] [Optimal Driving between Nonequilibrium Steady States under a Dissipation Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14891)
*Keisuke Sugie,Dimitri Loutchko,Tetsuya J. Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了在给定“housekeeping EP 预算”约束下，通过同时优化驱动协议及其持续时间来最小化总熵产生（EP）的方法，建立了资源化几何框架并给出总 EP 的下界，且在钙泵模型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡稳态之间的驱动需同时付出两类热力学代价：由改变控制参数导致的超额熵产生（excess EP）和维持稳态电流所需的 housekeeping EP。传统固定时长优化只最小化前者而忽略后者，因此作者希望同时优化协议与时长，使总 EP 在给定 housekeeping EP 预算下最小化，以刻画两者之间的内在权衡。

Method: 作者在慢驱动（slow-driving）近似下，将累积的 housekeeping EP 视为“熵时间”，把问题映射为在由稳态 EP 率加权的摩擦度量下的测地线优化问题。借助这一基于资源的几何（resource-based geometry），确定最优路径与时间调度，并由此导出总 EP 的几何下界；在近平衡时，该下界在由修正热力学长度定义的最优预算下以能量均分（equipartition）方式达到。

Result: 研究表明，该框架确立了 housekeeping EP 与 excess EP 之间的内在权衡，给出总 EP 的几何下界；与固定时长驱动相比，最优协议明显不同。对 ATP 驱动钙泵的三态模型进行数值模拟，验证了总 EP 下界，并展示了最优预算下能量均分的实现。

Conclusion: 该工作为持续耗散系统提供了考虑 housekeeping EP 的最优驱动方案，揭示了资源化几何结构及 housekeeping/excess EP 间的权衡关系，拓展了传统固定时长优化框架，并与模拟结果一致。

Abstract: Driving between nonequilibrium steady states incurs two thermodynamic costs: excess entropy production (EP) resulting from changing control parameters, and housekeeping EP required to sustain steady currents. Conventional fixed-duration optimization minimizes the former while neglecting the latter. Here, we instead optimize excess EP over both the protocol and its duration subject to a fixed leading order of housekeeping-EP budget, thereby minimizing total EP for that budget constraint. In the slow-driving regime, accumulated housekeeping EP acts as an entropic time, mapping the problem to a geodesic optimization under a friction metric weighted by the steady-state EP rate. This resource-based geometry determines optimal paths and schedules for systems that continue to dissipate even in steady states, establishing an intrinsic trade-off between housekeeping and excess EPs. Near equilibrium, this framework further yields geometric lower bounds on total EP, attained with equipartition at the optimal budget given by a modified thermodynamic length. Numerical simulations of a three-state model for an ATP-driven calcium pump reveal distinct optimal protocols compared to fixed-duration driving, confirming the total-EP bounds.

</details>


### [42] [Going beyond the standard quasiparticle picture: Entanglement spreading after quenching from highly entangled states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15409)
*Elisa Sabadini,Vincenzo Alba*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究 XXZ 自旋链中从 n-rainbow 态淬灭后的纠缠增长。发现非相互作用 XX 链中产生 2n 种准粒子的纠缠多重态，虽种间关联按 1/n 衰减，却强烈抑制纠缠熵增长率（n→∞ 时趋于零），可用广义准粒子图像描述；而在相互作用的 XXZ 链中，tDMRG 模拟显示纠缠熵按热力学熵跨截面交换的速率增长，多重态抑制效应在可达时间尺度内无迹可寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准粒子图像能定量描述可积系统量子淬灭后的纠缠扩散，但标准图像依赖"淬灭产生纠缠准粒子对"的假设，该假设仅对所谓可积初态成立。作者旨在超越此限制，研究由越来越长程的 Bell 对构成的宏观均匀 n-rainbow 态（n>1 时不可积），从而检验并扩展准粒子图像在更一般初态下的适用性。

Method: 以 XXZ 自旋链（含非相互作用的 XX 极限）为模型，取 n-rainbow 态为初态进行量子淬灭。解析证明局部稳态随 n→∞ 趋近无限温度态，修正为 1/n 量级。在 XX 链中解析推导出 2n 种准粒子构成的纠缠多重态结构，并建立广义准粒子图像刻画其纠缠熵增长。在相互作用 XXZ 链中，采用时间相关密度矩阵重整化群（tDMRG）进行数值模拟，包括均匀淬灭与 n-rainbow 态和无限温度态的接合两类情形，并猜想 von Neumann 熵公式。

Abstract: The quasiparticle picture provides a quantitative description of entanglement spreading after quantum quenches in integrable systems. In interacting models, however, it relies on the assumption that the quench creates entangled pairs of quasiparticles, which is justified only for the so-called integrable initial states. Here we go beyond this setting by studying quenches in the XXZ spin chain from the $n$-rainbow states, a family of macroscopically homogeneous states formed by increasingly long-range Bell pairs, which are not integrable for $n>1$. The local stationary state after the quench approaches the infinite-temperature state as $n\to\infty$, with corrections of order $1/n$, as we show analytically. Nevertheless, in the noninteracting XX chain the quench produces entangled multiplets of $2n$ species of quasiparticles. Although interspecies correlations vanish as $1/n$, they strongly suppress the growth of the entanglement entropy, whose rate vanishes as $n\to\infty$. We capture this behaviour within a generalized quasiparticle picture. In the interacting XXZ chain, instead, time-dependent density matrix renormalization group (tDMRG) simulations show that the entanglement entropy grows at the rate at which thermodynamic entropy is exchanged across the cut. Within the time scales accessible to tDMRG, the data show no trace of the multiplet suppression. This holds both after homogeneous quenches and after joining the $n$-rainbow state with an infinite-temperature state, for which we conjecture a formula for the von Neumann entropy.

Abstract: Passive probes in active baths can undergo spontaneous motion driven by nonequilibrium forces that depend on probe velocity and interaction geometry. Using trajectory-response relations that express the bath response to a probe in terms of free-particle displacement statistics, we investigate the dynamics of freely moving probes weakly coupled to ideal active baths. By analyzing how the mean force depends on the probe velocity, we identify stable resting and moving states and determine the parameter regime in which they coexist. For elliptical interactions, the resting state becomes unstable first along the narrow axis, resulting in direction selection already at the linear-response level. For fourfold interactions, linear friction remains isotropic, while the nonlinear dependence of the mean force on probe velocity selects axial or diagonal motion and produces directional multistability. We further show that the trajectory-response relations extend to higher orders in the probe strength, where multi-interval free-particle trajectory statistics become relevant. For a Gaussian probe, these higher-order terms shift the speed of the stable moving state.

Phys. 160, 134505 (2024)]. \textcolor{black}{The substrate induces a fluctuating network of subcritical ice-like clusters, from which a spherical-cap-shaped supercritical nucleus eventually emerges stochastically.} Using Classical Nucleation Theory, combine previously reported homogeneous nucleation rates with heterogeneous nucleation rates computed in this work to estimate key heterogeneous nucleation parameters, including the nucleation barrier, the kinetic pre-factor, and the contact angle. Abstract: The Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) model provides an analytically controlled setting for strongly interacting quantum dynamics and holography, but its fermionic formulation leads to nonlocal encoding overheads on qubit-based quantum processors. We investigate the finite-$N$, finite-$q$ regime using exact diagonalization. We further construct coupled-cluster chains and obtain domain-wall dynamics consistent with diffusive magnetization transport in a small-cluster regime accessible to matrix-product-state simulations. These results establish analytical benchmarks and finite-size signatures of ordering, quantum chaos, and magnetization transport in U(1)-symmetric spin-SYK models, and they motivate quantum simulation of the low-temperature, large-system regime, which remains difficult for classical methods.

Abstract: We study the recovery of a rank-one signal embedded in a symmetric Euclidean random matrix, whose dense and geometrically correlated entries generally lack rotational invariance. We show that signal recovery through the leading eigenvector is governed by a competition between the principal directions of the noise and the signal. In particular, the signal may already be encoded in a subleading eigendirection, while remaining undetected by the leading principal component. As the signal strength increases, a crossing between competing eigenvalues can change their ordering, causing the leading eigenvector to switch abruptly from a noise-dominated to a signal-bearing direction. This mechanism gives rise to a discontinuous BBP-type transition, in which the overlap with the signal jumps from zero to a finite value. Using the replica method, we reduce the problem to a Fredholm eigenvalue equation and derive general criteria for the existence of a positive recovery threshold, expressed in terms of the unperturbed spectral edge, the spike mean and the spatial averages of the principal eigenfunctions. We obtain analytically the limiting largest eigenvalue, eigenvector-component distribution and signal overlap, and illustrate the results with explicit solutions for quadratic distance and rank-two kernels, finding excellent agreement with numerical diagonalisation.

Abstract: When a system is driven by a prescribed protocol, the entropy production can be decomposed into the housekeeping and non-autonomous contributions, each satisfying a fluctuation theorem. Abstract: Spatial heterogeneity in antibiotic environments drives the evolution of bacterial resistance. Abstract: Although information thermodynamics explains how the second law is modified due to the information acquired by Maxwell's demon, its formulation in many-body systems remains largely open. By additionally assuming that each local feedback term depends only on nearby measurement outcomes, we rigorously prove that the extractable work coincides in the thermodynamic limit with that extractable from the corresponding canonical ensemble. We further express this extractable work in terms of quantum-information quantities, including the quantum-classical mutual information and the Holevo information for the corresponding canonical ensemble. We also show that feedback based on distant measurement outcomes can exploit long-range correlations hidden in MITE, yielding an extensive increase in extracted work relative to the canonical ensemble.

Abstract: We investigate the transient dynamics of a one-dimensional ballistic random walker under threshold resetting (TR). Whenever the mean first-passage time to the threshold is finite, the walker reaches a steady state that is uniform on $[0,x_0]$ and independent of $φ(v)$. The relaxation towards it, in contrast, is highly sensitive to the behavior of $φ(v)$. For a large class of $φ(v)$-s, we find, rather remarkably, that the relaxation rate depends explicitly on the spatial coordinate, in contrast to conventional relaxation phenomena, where the rate is typically position-independent. Furthermore, this space-dependent relaxation rate eventually freezes to a constant, position-independent value beyond a critical position, a phenomenon we identify as \textit{freezing transition}. Additionally, for a power-law velocity distribution, the system exhibits an anomalously slow, algebraic relaxation towards the steady state. We also showed that a sharp cutoff in $φ(v)$ generates a shock-like discontinuity in the particle propagator that travels towards the threshold, with a jump height that decays with time. These results demonstrate how velocity fluctuations, despite leaving the steady state unchanged, can profoundly influence the transient spatial structures and showcase rich non-equilibrium behavior. Our analytical results are corroborated by Monte Carlo simulations, showing an excellent agreement.

Conclusion: 通过引入以信息熵定义正则温度的工作装配体，负温度热浴中的仅功提取被重新表述为普通的热流过程，从而与第二定律达成一致；该信息论与统计力学框架还自然解释了"驴行为"，并延伸到分子尺度机器。

Abstract: Work dissipation into a thermal bath is standard, whereas the reverse process -- sole work extraction from a thermal bath without a net state change -- contradicts the Kelvin-Planck formulation of the second law of thermodynamics. However, this prohibition is bypassed for baths at negative absolute temperature, sparking ongoing debate regarding their thermodynamic stability. Here, we reconcile the second law with sole work extraction from a negative-temperature bath by considering a large virtual assembly of weakly coupled working systems rather than an isolated machine. By assigning this assembly a canonical temperature derived from its Shannon/information entropy, the framework naturally extends to molecular-scale machines. We demonstrate paradigmatic examples where the assembly's canonical temperature is cooler than the negative-temperature bath. Consequently, sole work extraction represents standard heat flow from a hot system (the negative-temperature bath) to a cooler system (the working assembly), rendering the process fully consistent with the second law. This informational and statistical-mechanical framework also naturally explains the counter-intuitive phenomenon of "donkey behavior", where increasing the workload in a negative-temperature bath leads to enhanced work extraction.

Abstract: Finite-$N$ fluctuations govern the slow relaxation of long-range interacting systems, yet accessing their full statistics demands huge ensembles of costly simulations. We show that, in homogeneous systems without collective effects, this statistical problem collapses onto just three deterministic kinetic equations. Starting from the Landau-Dean-Kawasaki equation, i.e. the stochastic counterpart of the large deviation principle for the Landau equation, we derive the infinite hierarchy of $n$-point moments of the empirical distribution function at leading order in $1/N$. We prove that it is solved exactly by a quasi-Gaussian closure. The resulting minimal Landau fluctuation hierarchy is formed by the Landau equation, a Lyapunov equation for the two-point moment, and a kinetic equation for the three-point moment. From these three equations, all higher-order moments follow algebraically. The hierarchy exactly preserves the collisional invariants and their fluctuations, and predicts the full probability distribution of observables in the central fluctuation regime. Benchmarked against $N$-body realizations, it reproduces the mean, variance and higher-order moments with an accuracy that improves systematically with $N$. </details>

TL;DR: 本文综述并拓展了利用碳材料构造常负高斯曲率曲面（Lobachevsky 平面的实验室实现）的方法，核心思路是“平衡高斯-博内定理”：由拓扑决定的曲率在向错缺陷、弹性变形的六边形和边界之间分配；并基于 SL(2,R) 的单参数子群构造三类赝球面上的缺陷排布。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 围绕“平衡高斯-博内定理”这一单一原理进行综述与拓展：将拓扑所固定的总曲率在向错缺陷、弹性变形的六边形和边界之间分配；通过显式多面体构造证明仅由六边形构成的球面是“穿孔球面”；利用 SL(2,R) 的椭圆、抛物、双曲三类单参数子群分别构造椭圆族赝球面的缺陷排布，恢复已知的贝尔特拉米赝球面排布，并提出双曲赝球面的排布方案；最后将理想排布与设计所得及模拟纳米挤压所得的碳结构对比。

Abstract: Carbon-made surfaces of constant negative Gaussian curvature realize portions of the Lobachevsky plane in the laboratory, with motivations ranging from analogue gravity, where the elliptic, Beltrami and hyperbolic pseudospheres correspond to the three static phases of the Bañados--Teitelboim--Zanelli black hole, to water desalination. We review and extend their construction around a single principle, the balanced Gauss--Bonnet theorem: the curvature fixed by the topology is shared among disclination defects, elastically deformed hexagons and boundaries. Curvature without defects cannot change the topology: a sphere made of hexagons only is a punctured sphere, as an explicit polyhedral construction shows. The arrangements of the defects on the three pseudospheres stem from the elliptic, parabolic and hyperbolic one-parameter subgroups of $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$: we construct them for the elliptic family, recover the known one of the Beltrami pseudosphere, and propose one for the hyperbolic pseudosphere. We compare these ideal arrangements with carbon structures obtained by design and by simulated nano-extrusion, and suggest that the infinitely many Fuchsian groups may correspond to the infinitely many Lobachevsky surfaces.

Abstract: Finite measurements at a chain end can certify local quantum dynamics without determining bulk topology. Under explicit device assumptions, these sets yield state-independent lower bounds on symmetrized autocorrelations with a prescribed failure probability, valid for all compatible continuations. With equal hopping and pairing on the first bond and no additional boundary terms, an exact identity enables inference from the end qubit alone, even with arbitrary time-independent interactions elsewhere. Synthetic benchmarks near a boundary sweet spot certify persistence for up to about 60 times the measurement window. Three- and six-qubit IBM records strengthen conditional guarantees for the programmed coherent Hamiltonian. Applications to quantum-dot models relate tuning precision to persistence times and detuning-induced splitting.

</details>

Abstract: The low-frequency (LF) mode observed in the THz spectrum of liquid water near 10 meV has been known for decades, yet its microscopic origin remains debated. Leading interpretations assign it to a transverse acoustic-like excitation, a transverse optic-like mode, or a mode-mode coupling mechanism. We address this question by probing the collective dynamics of H$_2$O and D$_2$O at room temperature and pressures from 0.1 to 1 GPa using high-resolution (1.6 meV) inelastic X-ray scattering (IXS). The LF mode remains heavily damped, close to critical damping, throughout the explored range, while its frequency hardens markedly with pressure, much more than expected for a transverse acoustic mode. Comparison with IXS measurements and \textit{ab initio} simulations of crystalline Ice VI reveals a cluster of weakly dispersive modes in the same energy range, suggesting that the low-frequency mode in liquid water reflects a mixed character arising from the superposition of several branches rather than a single well-defined excitation, providing a coherent microscopic picture of water's anomalous THz dynamics under compression.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡传播依赖自由态与弱扰动平衡态之间的位移来实现物理系统中的学习，但现有研究尚未阐明底层振荡器动力学如何决定学习信号在振荡器伊辛机中的传输。作者希望揭示是哪些物理因素（如推动力、敏感度、耦合形式、谐波注入等）控制了这一传输过程，从而解释为何“实现相同的伊辛哈密顿量”并不自动等价于“具备有效学习能力”。

Method: 作者采用二分 OIM，令其相互作用在相位差耦合与相位和耦合之间插值，并确保所有耦合形式在二值锁相态下都实现相同的伊辛哈密顿量。通过将隐藏层响应分解为推动力（输出端施加）与敏感度（控制其向隐藏层传输）两个因子，分别考察二次谐波注入如何通过相位二值化影响推动力，以及耦合形式如何通过能量景观的混合曲率影响跨层敏感度。进一步用数值模拟验证这些机制，并评估其对分类性能的影响。

Result: 研究表明：二次谐波注入主要通过相位二值化影响推动力；耦合形式则通过能量景观的混合曲率控制跨层敏感度。当两种耦合等量混合时，混合曲率在二值流形上消失，从而强烈抑制隐藏层学习信号。数值模拟验证了这些机制及其对分类性能的后果。

Conclusion: 实现目标能量景观只是物理学习的必要条件而非充分条件；系统还必须能够支持学习所需扰动的传输。这一发现为设计具备有效学习能力的物理系统（如振荡器伊辛机）提供了新的准则。

Abstract: Equilibrium propagation (EP) enables learning in physical systems by exploiting the displacement between free and weakly perturbed equilibrium states. Here, we investigate how the underlying oscillator dynamics govern the transmission of learning signals in oscillator Ising machines (OIMs). We show that the hidden-layer response can be decomposed into two distinct factors: the nudging force applied at the output and the susceptibility governing its transmission to the hidden layer. Using a bipartite OIM with interactions interpolating between phase-difference and phase-sum coupling, we investigate these factors while ensuring that all coupling forms realize the same Ising Hamiltonian at binary phase-locked states. We demonstrate that second-harmonic injection primarily influences the nudging force through phase binarization, whereas the coupling form controls the cross-layer susceptibility through the mixed curvature of the energy landscape. At equal mixing, the mixed curvature vanishes on the binary manifold, strongly suppressing hidden-layer learning signals. Numerical simulations validate these mechanisms and reveal their consequences for classification performance. Our findings establish that realizing a desired energy landscape alone is insufficient to ensure effective physical learning; the system must also support the transmission of perturbations required for learning.

</details>

Abstract: Probabilistic-bit (p-bit) invertible logic encodes a Boolean relation as an energy whose ground states are its legal assignments, computing in any direction by sampling. Traditional constructions target ground-state membership and a wide minimum energy gap, yet finite-time samplers traverse excited states. We cast Hamiltonian design as inverse statistical mechanics: fit an energy with interactions up to order four whose Gibbs law approaches uniformity on each clamp case's legal states, under a gap floor keeping every legal state below every illegal one. Constrained maximum likelihood with pruning and refitting fits terms from a fixed pool, and every delivered energy is audited exactly on all cases. Each energy is scored by time to a verified solution under restarts with its best of nine tuned samplers. In underdetermined modes, learned multipliers factor in 0.55, 0.44, 0.52 and 0.46 [0.38, 0.56] of the hand-written encodings' updates from 4x4 to 7x7, faster within every family below 7x7 at no resolved work penalty; learned adders solve the reverse mode in 0.71 of the updates at 8 bits and about a quarter at 24 and 32 bits, at 0.55 of the work at 32 bits. At 5x5 under equal pool, budget and floor, a gap-maximizing fit reaches 0.79 [0.73, 0.88] in updates and 1.26 in work against the hand-written encoding, while likelihood fitting takes 0.55 [0.46, 0.58] and 0.58 [0.48, 0.61] of the gap fit's updates and work. Rounding coefficients to the narrowest exact integer words tested, we run both energies at each pair's common word on one FPGA color-sweep SSA core, bit-exact with software: learned multipliers take 0.41 of the hand-written encoding's cycles at 4x4 and 0.057 [0.045, 0.068] at 7x7, at up to 2.4 times its lookup tables. The method requires only a circuit's legal assignments; in determined modes, hand-written encodings take less work, and from 12 bits on the adder fewer updates.

</details>

Abstract: The antiferromagnetic Ising chain in transverse and longitudinal fields exhibits a continuous critical point, a mixed-order transition line and, with quenched disorder, an infinite-disorder fixed point and reentrant order. Using exact free-fermion solutions and the density-matrix renormalization group, we examine what the zeroth persistent homology of staggered ground-state correlations adds to conventional responses. Its barcode records the merge scales of the single-linkage hierarchy, and the maximum of its persistence-landscape velocity defines a threshold-free pseudocritical point. This point locates the clean critical point and the mixed-order line with an accuracy comparable to conventional responses. In the reentrant regime, the global velocity misplaces the entry, which is recovered only by restricting the filtration to the ordering scale, and none of the tested estimators locates the exit. A domain wall forced by the boundary conditions suppresses the disorder-averaged reentrant lobe. For individual disordered realizations, the labelled merge tree recovers most strong-disorder renormalization-group clusters, including noncontiguous ones missed by lattice-restricted hierarchies, with increasing agreement at stronger disorder. This information resides in the full correlation matrix, while zeroth homology provides a stable, threshold-free hierarchy.

Abstract: We report a reconfigurable macroscopic mechanical realization of the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model: a suspended chain of alternating masses coupled by identical compression springs. The relevant SSH degree of freedom is the relative longitudinal displacement between neighboring masses. In this bond-coordinate basis, applying a constant shift and rescaling of the mechanical dynamical matrix maps a chain of $N$ blocks onto an (N-1)-site SSH chain. Using video tracking, we reconstruct bond-resolved spectra and mode profiles and compare them with predictions for an ideal finite SSH chain. The topological termination supports mid-gap modes localized near the boundaries, with the alternating-sublattice structure expected for an effective even-length SSH chain. Mass variations split the edge-mode frequencies and produce asymmetric mode profiles relative to the non-disordered predictions. This directly imageable platform enables studies of topological normal modes and disorder effects in a classical mechanical system.

</details>

Conclusion: 关联无序模型为自旋玻璃研究提供了新的视角，并已被证明是产生关于自旋玻璃问题高度非平凡结果的沃土。

Abstract: Ising spin-glass models with a specific type of correlated bond disorder have recently been used to relate properties of spin-glass phases, such as temperature chaos and magnetic-field chaos, to ferromagnetic order. This correlated disorder has a simple physical interpretation, which underlies the sampling procedure introduced by Oshima, Arai, and Hukushima for numerical simulations: the bond configuration is obtained from the Edwards-Anderson model with the symmetric $\pm J$ distribution by drawing one of its equilibrium spin configurations and writing the model in the gauge in which this reference configuration becomes the all-up state. The construction is analogous to the Mattis model, with the roles of the ferromagnet and the spin glass reversed: whereas the Mattis model is a ferromagnet in disguise, the ferromagnetic phase of the correlated model is essentially a spin-glass phase in disguise. The frustration statistics of the correlated-disorder model are exactly those of the symmetric $\pm J$ model, and, in a generalization with an additional parameter $γ$, the probabilities of the gauge equivalence classes are determined by $γ$ alone, whereas the inverse temperature at which the reference configuration is drawn determines only the distribution within each class. The magnetization of this model is the overlap with the reference configuration. Through this correspondence, studies of its ferromagnetic phase have led to unexpected results on temperature chaos and magnetic-field chaos in the spin-glass phase of the Edwards-Anderson model. Models with correlated disorder have thus provided a new perspective on spin glasses and have proved fertile ground for highly nontrivial results on the spin-glass problem.

</details>

Abstract: The boson peak is an excess of low-frequency vibrational states over the Debye prediction and is commonly associated with structural disorder. Similar heat-capacity anomalies also occur in ordered molecular crystals, but their dependence on molecular structure and their relation to heat transport remain incompletely understood. Here we compare six polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon (PAH) crystals -- pyrene, tetracene, perylene, pentacene, coronene and corannulene -- using low-temperature heat capacity, low-frequency Raman spectroscopy and thermal conductivity measurements. For compounds with well-resolved maxima, the rising side of the heat-capacity excess is consistent with a raised-cosine scaling function with no adjustable shape parameter, whereas the decay is compound-dependent. The number and widths of resolved lattice-mode Raman bands vary across the series, although their assignment is constrained by spectral background and resolution. Thermal conductivity ranges from crystal-like to glass-like behaviour. An activated contribution is resolved in the five planar compounds but is not required to describe the corannulene data within the sensitivity of the present measurements. These results show that glass-like thermal and vibrational signatures are not unique to amorphous materials and are associated with differences in molecular structure and crystal packing; they do not, by themselves, isolate packing as the sole causal variable.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Higher-order node-based synchronization provides distinct mechanisms for discontinuous synchronization transitions. However, a comprehensive theoretical framework capable of encompassing existing models and revealing the role of higher-order interactions beyond triangles is still missing. Here, combining algebraic topology and nonlinear dynamics, we introduce Cup and Cap Synchronization, a unifying theoretical framework for higher-order node-based dynamics that naturally incorporates the topological organization of the underlying higher-order network. We show that Cup and Cap Synchronization can display critical behavior belonging to the universality classes of two existing higher-order Kuramoto-like models, whose distinct critical behaviors were thought to originate from different mechanisms. Remarkably, we demonstrate that Cup and Cap Synchronization displays additional phenomenology not captured by the previously proposed models of higher-order node-based synchronization. Most relevantly, the inclusion of four-body interactions can fundamentally alter the critical properties of the synchronization transition, giving rise to collective phenomena that cannot be observed in models including only interactions mediated by edges and triangles. These findings reveal the importance of going beyond the commonly studied three-body terms and establish Cup and Cap Synchronization as a unifying theoretical framework for understanding collective dynamics in higher-order networks.

</details>

Abstract: The presence of long-ranged, many-body resonances in the many-body localised phase of disordered quantum systems is manifest in strong inhomogeneities of eigenstate amplitudes on the Fock space. Exact diagonalisation of the disordered tilted-field Ising chain uncovers a stark contrast: typical correlations decay perfectly exponentially, whereas average correlations exhibit subexponential decay with systematic system-size dependence driven by rare resonant nodes. Crucially, the interplay of this distribution with the high-dimensional geometry of the hypercubic Fock-space graph, leads to the qualitative difference between the typical and average behaviours. The theory also allows us to derive the singularity spectrum of the many-body localised eigenstates, and to establish theoretically the anomalous symmetry violation of quantum multifractality as an inherent consequence of the Fock-space geometry.

Abstract: The Poisson $σ$-model (PSM) over a closed worldsheet $Σ$ is reinterpreted as a theory of connections on a trivial principaloid bundle $P_0 = Σ\times G \to Σ$ with the symplectic structure groupoid $(G,ω)$ integrating the Poisson target $(M,Π)$, and that of sections of the shadow bundle $F_0 = Σ\times M \to Σ$ - as defined in 2503.09886 [math.DG]. A general anomaly-cancellation mechanism is proposed, which couples the bare model - rewritten as the holonomy of a connection-dependent trivial gerbe on $F_0$ - to a background gerbe over $M$ endowed with a $G$-equivariant structure. The coupling takes the form of the tensor product of the two gerbes and the extended PSM is defined as the holonomy of the product gerbe. An essential generalisation of the theory is then proposed - to shadows $F$ of principaloid bundles $P$ of arbitrary topology, in keeping with the interpretation of the gauging as descent to the orbistack $M//G$. The generalisation uses local product gerbes depending on local connections over trivialisations of $F$. The $G$-equivariant structure is proven to ensure a 2-stacky descent of the local gerbes to a global one over $F$. Its holonomy is taken as the definition of the generalised PSM. A cohomological discussion of obstructions against the descent is provided. Finally, a reformulation of the generalised PSM as a field theory with a topological gauge-symmetry defect is given, in which the $G$-equivariant structure is employed - alongside the transition cocycle of $P$ - as the datum of the defect.

The construction uses bounded heat-regularized fields and a Hilbert algebra, which gives a faithful normal semifinite trace without a path-integral argument. The weight restriction is sharp: elementary point fields remain unbounded after positive heat regularization when $0<d\leq1/2$. For one massless scalar, specified bounded observables and normal states have a high energy limit described by a hyperfinite continuous core. We explain why this limit does not preserve the nonhyperfiniteness of the exact algebra.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微生物群落等生态系统中普遍观测到 Gamma 分布、无相关性的丰度波动,传统上这被解读为物种间缺乏相互作用的证据。作者旨在揭示这一解读的谬误,展示大量存在相互作用的系统也能产生相同的独立 Gamma 平稳分布;同时为一般 Lotka–Volterra 系统(尤其是非对称、捕食-被捕食情形)建立严格的随机热力学/metriplectic 框架,以刻画噪声、平稳态与熵产生之间的关系。

Method: 作者首先构造 Lotka–Volterra 系统的 metriplectic 结构:以 Volterra 函数 V_C 为能量、环境熵变量为熵,当且仅当 Volterra 耗散条件 CA+AᵀC≤0 时成立,并用对数-正则(log-canonical)泊松双向量显式构造。通过随机 GENERIC 的涨落-耗散关系引入乘法环境噪声,其协方差由 AC⁻¹ 的对称部分确定,得到显式吉布斯测度(独立 Gamma 分布乘积)。进而证明平稳定律只确定平衡态与 C/k,存在维度 n² 的开锥中的大量相互作用矩阵产生同一平稳定律。通过零滞后 log-丰度协方差导数等于 A(C/k)⁻¹ 揭示相互作用在时间上的体现,并闭式算出稳态熵产生。最后对一般噪声证明精确 Lyapunov 型恒等式 AK+KᵀAᵀ+Q=0(其中 K=Cov(x,log x)),由此导出物种独立当且仅当噪声为 metriplectic 兼容,并给出互协方差下界。

Result: 主要结果包括:(1) metriplectic 结构存在性的充要条件为 Volterra 耗散条件;(2) 随机系统平稳分布为独立 Gamma 分布乘积,对非对称相互作用与捕食-被捕食均成立;(3) 平稳定律只确定平衡态与 C/k,任意 n² 维开锥内的相互作用矩阵配合其兼容噪声都可产生与无耦合随机 logistic 方程相同的定律,故 Gamma 无相关丰度波动不能作为无相互作用的证据;(4) 零滞后 log-丰度协方差导数等于 A(C/k)⁻¹,其反对称部分即泊松部分承载全部稳态熵产生;(5) 一般噪声下精确 Lyapunov 恒等式 AK+KᵀAᵀ+Q=0 成立,给出互协方差的显式下界,捕食-被捕食模型模拟显示该下界近乎紧确。

Conclusion: 本文建立了 Lotka–Volterra 系统在 Volterra 耗散条件下的完备 metriplectic/随机 GENERIC 框架,显式构造了产生独立 Gamma 平稳分布的乘法噪声。核心启示是:观测到的 Gamma 分布、无相关丰度波动不能作为物种间无相互作用的证据,因为大量具有真实相互作用的系统可产生相同的统计定律;相互作用需通过时间相关性(协方差的时间导数)来识别。所得到的普适 Lyapunov 恒等式将物种独立性与噪声的 metriplectic 兼容性等价联系起来,并为物种间互协方差提供了几乎紧确的下界,为生态数据的相互作用推断提供了新的理论判据。

Abstract: We show that a Lotka--Volterra system with an interior equilibrium admits a metriplectic structure, with a Volterra function $V_C$ as energy and an entropy variable for the environment, if and only if Volterra's dissipativity condition $CA+A^{\top}C\le0$ holds, and we construct it explicitly with a log-canonical Poisson bivector. Adding noise through the fluctuation--dissipation relation of stochastic GENERIC yields multiplicative environmental noise, with covariance fixed by the symmetric part of $AC^{-1}$, whose stationary law is an explicit Gibbs measure: a product of independent Gamma distributions. Unlike known Gibbs measures for stochastic Lotka--Volterra systems, this holds for nonsymmetric interactions, predator--prey included. The stationary law determines only the equilibrium and $C/k$: every interaction matrix in an open cone of dimension $n^2$, in particular any Poisson part, produces with its compatible noise the same law as uncoupled stochastic logistic equations. Gamma-distributed, uncorrelated abundance fluctuations, as observed in microbial communities, are therefore no evidence of absence of interactions. The interactions are, however, visible in time: the zero-lag derivative of the log-abundance covariance equals $A(C/k)^{-1}$, and its antisymmetric part is the Poisson part, which carries all the stationary entropy production, computed in closed form. Finally, for general noise we prove an exact Lyapunov-type identity $AK+K^\top A^\top+Q=0$ for $K=\mathrm{Cov}(x,\log x)$ at any stationary solution. It implies that the populations are independent if and only if the noise is metriplectically compatible, and gives an explicit lower bound on interspecies cross-covariances in terms of the distance of $Q$ to the compatible family. Simulations of a predator--prey model show that the bound is nearly sharp.

</details>

Abstract: We construct two-parameter Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-operators for the transfer matrices of noncompact open $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ BC-type spin chains with general boundary interactions, treating both one-boundary and two-boundary models. Abstract: Data-driven constitutive model discovery is recast as causal discovery of mechanism parent sets using time-unrolled structural causal models. An adaptive lasso and a NOTEARS-type constraint ensure graph acyclicity and an explicit evaluation order, treating climate-chamber protocols as interventions. Automatic differentiation yields the score's Gauss--Newton matrix as a local identifiability certificate, driving greedy D-optimal test design. In a synthetic study on a hygro-thermo-viscoelastic damaging plate (8 true edges among 13 candidates), interventional data recover the exact true graph in 7 of 10 runs (0.01px displacement noise) with zero attribution error. Conversely, observational data with a temperature--moisture correlation of $-0.998$ fail entirely because confounded scores are 250--380 times flatter. Observationally discovered models reproduce training climates (3.6% error) but mispredict hot and humid climates by up to 41.5% (16.9% average), whereas interventionally discovered models stay within 3.2%. A correlation sweep shows the certificate successfully predicts attribution errors, though weak-form score noise bias remains a primary limitation. Computed without knowledge of the truth, the D-optimal design selects test sets ranked second or third out of 126 candidates.

Method: 作者限制在带有唯一遍历测度的系统上，把符号解释为粒子，考虑最小化粒子相互作用能的基态构型。核心工具是引入“严格边界性质”（Strict Boundary Property），即非周期构型中有限模式的涨落有界的性质，并以此作为判定非周期结构对相互作用扰动是否稳定的准则。

Result: 结果表明“严格边界性质”与非周期结构的稳定性密切相关，可作为衡量其“刚性”的有效条件；文章通过 Robinson 平铺、Thue-Morse 系统与 Sturmian 系统等具体例子展示了该性质的适用性与意义。

Abstract: We study various families of non-periodic configurations of symbols assigned to vertices of regular graphs. We restrict ourselves to such systems where considered configurations support a uniquely ergodic measure. Symbols can be interpreted as particles and we are concerned with configurations which minimize the interaction energy of particles, the so-called ground-state configurations. One of the main questions then is whether ground-state configurations are stable with respect to small perturbations of interactions. Such stability may be interpreted as a sort of rigidity of non-periodic structures such as tilings, substitution systems, Sturmian systems, and other uniquely ergodic non-periodic systems. We introduce a property of bounded fluctuations of finite patterns of non-periodic configurations, the so-called Strict Boundary Property. We discuss how relevant this property is for stability of non-periodic structures. Examples of Robinson's tilings, Thue-Morse, and Sturmian systems are presented. Some open problems are formulated.

</details>

Abstract: Norton's dome illustrates how a Newtonian initial-value problem can admit both rest and delayed departure from the same initial state. Abstract: Following Bach, Lieb and Solovej, quadratic Hamiltonians can be defined as generators of unitary groups implementing one-parameter groups of Bogoliubov transformations. We develop an analogous dynamical definition of generalized spin--boson Hamiltonians. For a single spin level, we review results of Guichardet and Dereziński: the van Hove Hamiltonians, including the renormalized ones, are exactly the generators of affine Bogoliubov dynamics that do not mix creation and annihilation operators. For several spin levels, there is no analogue of this group, and we introduce spin-dressed implementations, that is to say the free flow of the fields is implemented up to bounded operator-valued displacements, which vanish at a rate measured by a so-called regularity scale and have a spin observable, the coupling map, as derivative at time zero. The one-particle Hamiltonian $Υ$ and the coupling map are determined by the Hamiltonian, and the field obeys a quantum Langevin equation. Spin-dressed implementations in Fock space, at the finest scale and for form factors in $D(Υ^{-1/2})$, are exactly the generalized spin--boson Hamiltonians, unique up to the spin Hamiltonian. At the roughest scale, the renormalized spin--boson Hamiltonian of a two-level system with ultraviolet-singular form factor and arbitrary tunnelling is a spin-dressed implementation, and the definition alone yields asymptotic Weyl operators on the test functions along which the coupling map is integrable, a dense set under standard assumptions.

</details>
