Abstract: We study quantum Hamiltonians whose separated angular component is expressed through a generic Painlevé VI transcendent. We construct generalized ladder operators for one-dimensional Schrödinger operators with a quadratic spectral variable. For the Painlevé VI angular Hamiltonian these operators satisfy $[N,J_\pm]=\pm2kJ_\pm$, where $N=\sqrt M$, and close through an eighth-degree norm polynomial. Combining the angular shifts with the radial shift operators of the oscillator, Coulomb and free Bessel systems yields higher-order integrals and deformed Racah-type polynomial algebras for the two-dimensional superintegrable models. This construction provides new families of Tremblay-Turbiner-Winternitz (TTW)- and Post-Winternitz (PW)-like Hamiltonians with integrals of arbitrarily high order and Painlevé VI in the potential. This framework also recovers models from the classification of third- and fourth-order integrals as particular cases. For a representative oscillator extension we compute the Casimir explicitly and recover the finite-dimensional energy spectrum from a deformed-oscillator realization. We then analyze the special-solution sector using the explicit families collected by Gromak, Laine and Shimomura. The resulting potentials are organized into a small number of spectral classes, and the distinction between formal zeros of the ladder norm and physical self-adjoint representations is made explicit. The regular nonclassical rational examples are rational Darboux deformations of the trigonometric Pöschl--Teller system and lead to strictly isospectral exceptional-Jacobi sectors or to state-adding representations with an isolated angular level. A singular branch produces an interior inverse-square barrier and a split-domain problem. For one regular exceptional sector and two state-adding sectors we display the polynomial algebras.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学家预言二维单组分等离子体在低温（高 β）区存在流体相，但严格的数学证明长期缺乏。本文旨在为流体相提供严格的数学基础，并研究无限体积极限的存在性及其与经典 Debye-Hückel 屏蔽理论的联系。

Method: 研究二维单组分等离子体（对数相互排斥的点电荷系统，处于均匀中和背景中）。在逆温度 β ≤ 2 的条件下，通过点过程涨落受控的框架，证明关联函数指数衰减与 Gibbs 态唯一性；进而构造无限体积极限点过程 Gin_β（推广 Ginibre 点过程），并在 Debye 长度尺度上分析其截断两点函数。

Result: 当 β ≤ 2 时，证明了关联函数指数衰减，且 Gibbs 态在涨落受控的点过程中唯一；得到了无限体积极限 Gin_β 的存在性（推广 Ginibre 点过程）；证明了 Gin_β 的截断两点函数在 Debye 长度尺度上，对 β 的一阶项由 Debye-Hückel 近似给出。

Conclusion: 该工作严格证明了二维单组分等离子体在 β ≤ 2 时处于物理学家预言的流体相：关联指数衰减、Gibbs 态唯一，并成功构造无限体积极限 Gin_β，验证了 Debye-Hückel 近似在一阶意义下的正确性。

Abstract: The two-dimensional one-component plasma is a system of point charges that repel each other logarithmically, in a uniform neutralizing background. When the inverse temperature $β$ is at most $2$, we prove that its correlations decay exponentially, and that its Gibbs state is unique among point processes whose fluctuations are controlled. This constitutes part of the fluid phase predicted by physicists. As a consequence, we obtain the existence of an infinite-volume limit, extending the Ginibre point process, which we call $\mathrm{Gin}_β$. We show that its truncated two-point function, at the scale of the Debye length, is given to first order in $β$ by the Debye-Hückel approximation.

</details>

Conclusion: 该类长程 Ising 链的临界行为可由缓变剖面的对数积分与相对修正项刻画，并明确不遵循标准 Kosterlitz–Thouless 定律，临界指数可随修正参数连续变化。

Abstract: For ferromagnetic Ising chains with $n^2J_n\to B>0$, the critical excess magnetization in a field inversely proportional to spin separation determines the connected correlation. Their slowly varying profile has finite logarithmic integral exactly when the ordered thermal slope is finite. For any finite nonnegative core, an exact tail gives inverse-logarithmic correlations and a square-root cusp. A relative correction $c/\log(n+e)$ with $c>1$ gives continuously varying logarithmic powers and a linear cusp. A lower bound on its disordered susceptibility excludes the usual Kosterlitz--Thouless law.

Abstract: Inspired by Carroll black holes, we consider the most general theory of Galilean dilaton gravity in two dimensions. We find generically that the solution space describes cosmological spacetimes. From a limiting perspective, these Galilean geometries connect the white and black hole singularities through the bifurcation surface. The latter turns into a Galilean extremal surface in the limit of infinite speed of light. We also discuss aspects of Galilean tachydynamics and find that the entropy is given by the dilaton evaluated at the Galilean extremal surface.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭玻色子弦场论作用量是非多项式的，三十多年来Sonoda和Zwiebach（1990）的无-go定理排除了用单个弦场、对称顶点和正则传播子写出三次形式。本文旨在规避该定理，给出三次闭弦场论的显式构造。

Result: 得到全阶三次闭弦场论作用量；论证辅助场在动能算子可逆的通用动量处不引入物理态。世界面上，Σ_2是纠缠的双弦态，通过算子乘积展开，四次型约化为两个非对称三次顶点的粘合（由局部坐标运动“装饰”，定义在无level matching的态上），并预期推广到更高阶（每个patch带k个刺孔）。弦顶点作为多输出乘积满足零亏格IBL_∞关系的扩展形式（含无输入或无输出操作），当且仅当弦乘积构成循环L_∞代数时成立。

Conclusion: 本文给出与Sonoda–Zwiebach无-go定理相容的三次闭弦场论显式构造，并将所得作用量与Chiaffrino、Mamade和Zwiebach的bar–cobar构造进行了比较。

Abstract: The action of closed bosonic string field theory is non-polynomial, and for more than thirty years a no-go theorem of Sonoda and Zwiebach (1990) has excluded a cubic formulation with a single string field, a symmetric vertex and the canonical propagator. We evade it with a Hubbard--Stratonovich transformation and provide an explicit construction of a cubic closed string field theory at all orders. The transformation introduces an infinite tower of auxiliary $k$-string fields $Σ_k$, entering at order $g_s^{2 k - 2}$, which turn the vertices with four or more legs into kinetic terms and cubic couplings. The action is cubic, at most quadratic in each auxiliary field, and triangular: eliminating the auxiliary fields by Gaussian integrals recovers the original action order by order at tree level, under invertibility and kernel conditions on the auxiliary kinetic operators. The original gauge symmetry lifts to the cubic action with no new parameter, and we argue that the auxiliary fields add no physical state at generic momentum, wherever their kinetic operators are invertible. On the worldsheet, $Σ_2$ is an entangled two-string state: by an operator product expansion, the quartic form reduces to a gluing of two asymmetric cubic vertices dressed by the motion of the local coordinates and defined on states without level matching. We expect this picture to generalize to higher orders, defining a gluing of patches carrying $k$ punctures each. The string vertices, read as products with several outputs, satisfy an extended form of the genus-zero $\mathrm{IBL}_\infty$ relations, including operations with no input or no output, exactly when the string products form a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra. Finally, we compare our action with the bar--cobar construction of Chiaffrino, Mamade and Zwiebach.

</details>

Abstract: We revisit the regularized on-shell actions of rotating electrically charged AdS black holes of gauged supergravity via holographic renormalization and background subtraction, with a particular emphasis on the frame in which the radial cutoff is placed. For holographic renormalization, the asymptotically global AdS frame, recently employed in seven dimensions, works equally well in five and six dimensions: the renormalized on-shell action of the AdS$_d$ black holes with $S^1\times S^{d-2}$ conformal boundaries satisfies the quantum statistical relation, up to a parameter-independent Casimir term in odd dimensions, and thereby places the holographic comparisons that so far relied on this relation on a first-principles footing. Background subtraction, in contrast, fails to reproduce this result in the same frame; the mismatch, which we identify exactly in terms of the Casimir term and the counterterms, demands a criterion for the cutoff surface yet to be established. Abstract: In this paper, we construct new self-gravitating solutions for the four-dimensional $SU(2)$ Einstein non-linear sigma model and the Einstein-Skyrme model, both minimally coupled to the Maxwell field, which describes spinning pulsed mesonic tubes. The gyratonic solutions are described by a smooth matter distribution, which is free of curvature singularities. We first consider the $SU(2)$ non-linear sigma model minimally coupled to General Relativity and the Maxwell field, constructing asymptotically flat, spinning, pulsed gyratonic solutions. Subsequently, we include the Skyrme term in the analysis. In this case, we show that in order to maintain the resolvability of the matter field equations, the spacetime must be asymptotically de Sitter. It is also shown that, despite the fact that the total topological charge is zero, these solutions possess a non-zero topological charge density.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XXX spin-chain 哈密顿量可由 $R$-矩阵形式导出，其中 $U$ 因子扮演核心角色。作者希望从代数、物理、几何三种视角统一理解 $U$，并厘清 Yang-Baxter 方程对物理隧穿振幅施加的细微约束，以及不同隧穿通道之间的耦合与 wall-crossing 行为。

Method: 作者利用 Yangian $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{g})$ 中 rational $R$-矩阵的 Gauss 分解 $R=LDU$，把 $U$ 视为类 molten crystal 链基底下非通用的伪余积 $\mathscr{D}$ 与真 Yangian 余积 $\Delta$ 之间的共轭因子（$\Delta=U^{-1}\mathscr{D}U$）；在物理上把 $U$ 表达为关联 Dynkin 图的 SUSY 淬火态 QFT 中隧穿振幅之和（Berry 相位），其平坦性由超对称保证从而导出可积性（YBE）；几何上把 $U$ 解释为定点处重整化的 Maulik-Okounkov 稳定包络。进一步构造递推关系，由基本振幅生成高阶集体振幅，并研究不同隧穿通道的耦合、抵消与参数相关的 wall-crossing 现象；具体计算集中于 $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 的高自旋及非 evaluation 表示，以及 $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{sl}_{n+1})$ 的基本表示。

Conclusion: $U$ 因子作为连接代数、物理与几何三种视角的枢纽，统一了 factorization twist、Berry 相位与重整化稳定包络；Yang-Baxter 方程对隧穿振幅给出精细约束，其根源在于超对称保证的 Berry 联络平坦性，而通道间的交织与 wall-crossing 现象揭示了集体振幅表示的非平凡结构。

Abstract: XXX spin-chain Hamiltonians can be derived within the $R$-matrix formalism. For a simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$, the rational $R$-matrix $R$ intertwines the coproduct $Δ$ in the Yangian $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{g})$. $R$ admits a particular Gauss decomposition $R=LDU$, where the upper-triangular matrix $U$ can be obtained from the lower-triangular matrix $L$. We treat $U$ in three ways. Algebraically, $U$ is known as a factorization twist. It plays the role of a ``universalizer'': it conjugates a non-universal pseudo-coproduct $\mathscr{D}$ in the molten crystal chain basis to the true Yangian coproduct $Δ=U^{-1}\mathscr{D} U$. Physically, $U$ can be obtained as a Berry phase in SUSY quiver QFT for a quiver associated with the Dynkin diagram of $\mathfrak{g}$. In this case, $U$ is calculated as a sum over tunneling amplitudes. Geometrically, $U$ can be interpreted as a renormalized Maulik-Okounkov stable envelope localized at a fixed point. In the physical setting, the Yang-Baxter equation (YBE), which ensures integrability, follows from the flatness of the Berry connection, which in turn follows from supersymmetry. However, the YBE imposes subtle constraints on the tunneling amplitudes, which we examine here. We construct recursion relations that allow higher collective amplitudes to be obtained from more elementary ones. We observe that different tunneling channels are in fact intertwined, leading to finer cancellations among amplitudes and to wall-crossing phenomena: depending on the parameters, the same amplitude is represented as a sum over different channels. We focus on $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ with higher-spin and non-evaluation representations, as well as on the fundamental representation of $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{sl}_{n+1})$.

</details>

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究固定单孤子Pöschl–Teller背景（sine-Gordon扭结）中两块δ–δ'板间的一圈Casimir相互作用，发现相互作用能和力的符号不一致，吸引力/排斥力必须由力判定而非能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在正弦-Gordon扭结这一拓扑孤子背景中，两块δ–δ'板之间的一圈Casimir相互作用性质，特别是背景如何重塑有效耦合强度以及吸引/排斥的判定依据。

Method: 横向涨落问题采用S² sigma模型的高斯截断（等价于带畴壁的易轴反铁磁体），板对畴壁无经典力。在v≥2|w|时涨落算子非负，严格稳定耦合（v>2|w|）进一步排除零模，使二次场论在固定sine-Gordon质量下自洽。背景修饰的相对行列式精确分解为两块板的有效梯度相关强度，每个频率通道的相互作用能符号由板的取向决定。

Result: 两块相同、同向、严格稳定的板在固定耦合与间距下做整体平移时，从扭结附近的吸引切换为远离扭结的排斥。相互作用能与间距共轭的力既不处处同号、也不在同一位置变号，存在负能量加排斥力的区域，故吸引/排斥须由力读出。对w≠0的此类板，力在短距离排斥、大距离吸引，且相互作用能在取决于板对位置的间距处取极小。对于v=1.05、w=0.5、d=1.5，能量在c≈-0.44和2.63处变号，力在c≈-0.30和2.00处变号。

Conclusion: 在sine-Gordon扭结背景下，两块δ–δ'板的Casimir相互作用呈现出背景修饰的有效强度与取向依赖的符号结构，相互作用能与力的符号并不一致，因此物理上吸引与排斥必须依据力而非能量来判定。

Abstract: We study the one-loop Casimir interaction between two $δ$--$δ'$ plates in the fixed one-soliton Pöschl--Teller background of the sine-Gordon kink. The transverse fluctuation problem is the Gaussian truncation of an $S^2$ sigma model, equivalently an easy-axis antiferromagnet with a domain wall, in which the plates exert no classical force on the wall. For $v\ge2|w|$ the fluctuation operators are non-negative, and strictly stable couplings, $v>2|w|$, also exclude zero modes, so the quadratic field theory is consistent at the fixed sine-Gordon mass. The background-dressed relative determinant factorises exactly into effective, gradient-dependent strengths of the two plates, and the sign of each frequency channel of the interaction energy is fixed by their orientation. Two identical, equally oriented, strictly stable plates switch from attraction near the kink to repulsion away from it under a rigid translation at fixed couplings and separation. The interaction energy and the force conjugate to the separation neither share their sign everywhere nor change sign at the same positions: there are regions of negative energy and repulsive force, so attraction and repulsion must be read from the force. For such plates with $w\neq0$ the force is repulsive at short and attractive at large separation, and the interaction energy has a minimum at a separation that depends on the position of the pair. For $v=1.05$, $w=0.5$ and $d=1.5$ the energy changes sign at $c\simeq-0.44$ and $2.63$, and the force at $c\simeq-0.30$ and $2.00$.

</details>


### [97] [Solving spectral flow in AdS$_3$: from covering maps to pencils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15070)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdS₃ 中纯 NS-NS 通量弦由可精确求解的 SL(2,ℝ) WZW 模型描述，但绕数弦（谱流算子）的关联函数此前只做到三点；解决任意插入点数与绕数下的绕数弦关联函数是悬而未决的问题。

Method: 核心工具是一族（pencil）从世界面到边界的有理映射，它在一般插入点处存在，能一次性将所有插入解绕（unwind）；当覆盖映射存在时，该铅笔退化为覆盖映射。

Result: 得到了任意数目插入与任意绕数下的全部绕数弦关联函数，并统一表示为未绕数（unflowed）关联函数的组合，从而证明了 Dei 与 Eberhardt 的四点猜想。

Conclusion: 通过铅笔有理映射的解绕机制，绕数弦的任意点关联函数可完全约化为未绕数关联函数，四点猜想由此得证，完整解决了该模型绕数弦关联函数问题。

Abstract: Strings in AdS$_3$ with pure NS-NS flux are described by the exactly solvable SL(2,$\mathbb{R}$) WZW model, yet correlators of winding strings, given by spectrally flowed operators, were so far known only up to three points. We obtain all of them, for any number of insertions and windings, in terms of unflowed correlators, proving the four-point conjecture of Dei and Eberhardt. The key ingredient is a pencil of rational maps from the worldsheet to the boundary which exists for generic insertion points, unwinds all insertions at once, and collapses to the covering map when the latter exists.

</details>


### [98] [Solitons of the intermediate long wave equation with residue source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15072)

TL;DR: 本文通过佐藤超函数框架重新审视中间长波（ILW）方程的双线性形式，将主值积分解释为超函数，并引入"残差指标"来刻画进入围道内部的tau函数零点，从而得到带自洽源的残差扩展ILW系统，并据此分类孤子扇区、构造精确解并分析其谱衰减行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ILW方程的双线性形式依赖主值积分，通常假设tau函数在相关围道内无零点，从而恢复到无源方程。但当零点进入围道时，其残差贡献不可忽略，因此需要一种统一的框架来处理这一情形，以揭示此前被忽略的孤子扇区及其物理意义。

Method: 作者在佐藤超函数的框架下重新解读ILW方程双线性形式中的主值积分；当tau函数零点落入围道时保留其残差贡献，导出带自洽源（由tau函数自身决定）的残差扩展ILW系统。引入"残差指标"统计每个解析带内被围道包围的零点数目，用以分类孤子扇区。构造了精确的单孤子与多孤子tau函数（含升-降混合构型及其分解的二体相互作用），并结合Fourier空间分析研究孤子谱的大波数衰减行为。

Conclusion: 将ILW方程的双线性结构纳入佐藤超函数框架，可以自然地统一处理无源情形与带源自洽情形，得到残差扩展ILW系统；残差指标为孤子扇区提供了清晰分类，并揭示衰减率在扇区边界处趋于零的谱特征。这一框架为理解ILW方程更丰富的孤子结构与动力学行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: We revisit the bilinear formulation of the intermediate long-wave (ILW) equation by interpreting its principal-value integrals in the framework of Sato hyperfunctions. When the tau-functions have no zeros inside the relevant contours, the conventional source-free ILW equation is recovered. When zeros enter the contours, their residue contributions must instead be retained, leading naturally to a residue-extended ILW system with self-consistent sources determined by the tau-functions themselves. We introduce a residue index that counts the zeros enclosed in each analytic strip and use it to classify the resulting soliton sectors. The conventional elevation-soliton belongs to the zero-residue sector, whereas depression-solitons and higher elevation branches generally carry non-zero residue indices. Exact one- and multisoliton tau-functions are constructed, including mixed elevation--depression configurations with factorised two-body interactions. Their residue indices are additive and remain invariant during collisions, provided that no zero crosses a contour boundary. A Fourier-space analysis shows that the soliton spectra decay exponentially at large wavenumbers, with a decay rate determined by the distance of the nearest complex singularity from the real axis. This rate vanishes as an odd-multiple boundary between adjacent residue sectors is approached, providing a direct spectral signature of the transition between them.

</details>


### [99] [$n$-Gon Correlators in $d$-dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15147)
*Yong-Xiang Su,Dongyu Yang*

TL;DR: 本文研究标量单圈宇宙学 n 角关联函数的解析结构，通过被积函数级约化与适配线性管能量分母的 Baikov 表示，在空间维数 d=n 时确定将局域首奇异归一化的代数因子，并构造任意 n 的显式 dlog 形式；对 d=n=4 的完整盒图在共线运动学族中计算外能间断，求和所有时间序后得到一个非零的第二类完全椭圆积分，并讨论了管切割与宇宙学 dressing 表示的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学关联函数的解析结构超出一股局域首奇异（leading singularities）的范畴，而积分域（时间排序/管能量）对解析结构的决定作用尚未被充分理解，需要发展适用于管能量分母的被积函数约化与表示工具来刻画其全局解析性质。

Method: 作者采用被积函数级约化（integrand-level reduction）与 Baikov 表示，并将其适配到线性的管能量（tube-energy）分母；在空间维数 d=n 下确定归一化局域首奇异的代数因子，并构造任意 n 的显式 dlog 形式。对 d=n=4 的完整盒图，在共线运动学族中计算外部能量间断，求和所有时间排序得到椭圆积分；同时比较 d=3 与 d=4 的逐次管能量间断，并将若干管切割与宇宙学 dressing 表示相联系。

Result: 在 d=n 时给出了归一化局域首奇异为 1 的代数因子以及任意 n 的显式 dlog 形式；d=n=4 完整盒图的外能间断在共线运动学族中求和所有时间排序后得到非零的第二类完全椭圆积分（K 型），说明解析结构并非仅有局域首奇异；比较了 d=3 与 d=4 的逐次管能量间断，并将部分管切割与宇宙学 dressing 表示关联起来。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，在超出局域首奇异的层面，积分域（尤其是时间排序所对应的管能量区域）对确定宇宙学关联函数的解析结构至关重要；被积函数约化与适配管能量分母的 Baikov 表示为系统刻画此类结构提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We study the analytic structure of scalar one-loop cosmological $n$-gon correlators using an integrand-level reduction and a Baikov representation adapted to linear tube-energy denominators. At spatial dimension $d=n$, we determine the algebraic factors that normalize their local leading singularities to unity and construct explicit $d\log$ forms for arbitrary $n$. For the complete box at $d=n=4$, we evaluate an external-energy discontinuity in a collinear kinematic family and find a nonvanishing complete elliptic integral of the second kind after summing all time orderings. We also compare successive tube-energy discontinuities in $d=3$ and $d=4$, and relate selected tube cuts to the cosmological dressing representation. These results illustrate the importance of integration domains in determining the analytic structure beyond local leading singularities.

</details>


### [100] [Canonical Commutation Relations and Poisson Brackets with Non-locality in Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15173)
*Yasu Gen,Hiroaki Matsunaga,Jojiro Totsuka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究一种受弦启发的模型，其动能项通过有限位移 σ 耦合不同时刻的场，并带有一个与固定背景的耦合 λ。作者从作用量变分的边界项构造守恒的辛形式，导出正时间泊松括号，并利用 Schwinger–Dyson 关系证明正时间间隔上的量子对易子等于该括号乘以 iℏ。对易子期望值在 s=σ 处出现 ℏ 的跳跃；在 0<s<σ 区间内对易子恒为零，且 λ→0 不能恢复自由对易子，而 σ→0 则能恢复局域对易子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非局域（时间位移）动能项下场的正则结构与量子化问题，特别是有限时间位移 σ 对泊松括号和对易子结构的影响，以及 λ→0 与 σ→0 两个极限的不可交换性。

Method: 从作用量变分的边界项出发构造守恒的辛形式，进而导出正时间泊松括号；借助 Schwinger–Dyson 关系，将量子对易子与该泊松括号联系起来，并分析对易子期望值随正时间间隔 s 的行为及两个参数极限。

Result: 正时间间隔 s 上的对易子等于 iℏ 乘以泊松括号；对易子期望值在 s=σ 处出现等于 ℏ 的跳跃，对应位移变量在等时意义下的局域动能项；在 0<s<σ 区间内，对任意 λ>0 对易子均为零；λ→0（固定 σ）在该区间不能恢复自由对易子，而 σ→0（固定 λ）在每个固定 s>0 处恢复局域对易子。

Conclusion: 非局域时间位移动能项会显著改变场的正则结构与量子对易子，导致 λ→0 与 σ→0 两个极限不可交换：先取 σ→0 方能恢复局域理论，而先取 λ→0 得不到自由对易子。这提示在处理带时间位移的非局域模型时必须谨慎处理极限次序。

Abstract: We study the canonical structure of a string-inspired model in which the kinetic term couples fields at times separated by a finite shift $σ$, with a coupling $λ$ to a fixed background. From the boundary term in the variation of the action, we construct a conserved symplectic form and derive the positive-time Poisson bracket. Using the Schwinger--Dyson relation, we show that at positive time separation $s$ the commutator equals $i\hbar$ times this bracket. The canonical normalization $\hbar$ appears as a jump of the commutator expectation value at $s=σ$, which is equal time for shifted variables with a local kinetic term; for $0<s<σ$ the commutator vanishes for every $λ>0$. Hence $λ\to0$ at fixed $σ$ does not reproduce the free commutator on this interval, whereas $σ\to0$ at fixed $λ$ recovers the local commutator at every fixed $s>0$.

</details>


### [101] [Simplicity of the Three-Loop Six-Point ABJM Amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15193)
*Hanyu Fang,Chia-Kai Kuo,Kai Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过负几何方法计算了平面 ABJM 理论中的三点图六点振幅，发现其仅由一个二分链贡献，积分结果恰为 $-π^2$ 乘以一圈振幅，从而证明共线反常维度（如同尖点）在三圈无修正，完整三圈振幅完全由低圈数据与 $-π^2$ 系数决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算平面 ABJM 理论中高圈散射振幅，特别是三圈六点振幅的圈次修正，以探究其红外结构与共线反常维度是否具有类似超对称规范理论中的简单性。

Method: 采用负几何（negative geometries）框架计算三圈六点振幅；在该阶仅单一二分链（bipartite chain）对振幅对数有贡献，构造其对偶共形不变的被积函数并证明其局部有限，随后完成积分。

Result: 积分所得二分链红外有限，且恰好等于 $-π^2$ 倍的一圈振幅；因此共线反常维度（以及尖点反常维度）在三圈无修正；完整三圈振幅由低圈乘积（承载全部红外发散）与系数 $-π^2$ 唯一确定。

Conclusion: 三圈六点 ABJM 振幅展现出意外的简单性，并暗示其奇、偶圈贡献之间存在迭代关系。

Abstract: We compute the three-loop six-point amplitude in planar ABJM theory using negative geometries. At this order, only a single bipartite chain contributes to the logarithm of the amplitude, and we find that its dual-conformally invariant integrand is locally finite. The integrated chain is therefore infrared finite and, remarkably, equals exactly $-π^2$ times the one-loop amplitude. Hence the collinear anomalous dimension, like the cusp, receives no three-loop correction. Restoring the disconnected products of lower-loop contributions, which carry all the infrared divergences, we obtain the full three-loop amplitude, fixed entirely by lower-loop data and the coefficient $-π^2$. These results reveal an unexpected simplicity of the six-point ABJM amplitude and suggest an iterative relation between its odd- and even-loop terms.

</details>


### [102] [D(-1)/D7 bound states in supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15423)
*Gongrui Cheng,Klaas Parmentier,Thomas Van Riet*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在欧氏IIB型超引力中构造了D(-1)/D7束缚态，并将其超对称场方程约化为两个受单个复约束的全纯函数，得到一类余维二解，其中最有趣的解具有常dilaton，并连接无穷远真空与几何变为R×S¹×T⁸的受控标度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Billò等人的弦论设定启发——该设定的开弦描述可实现八维超杨-米尔斯理论中的瞬子，但此前超引力构造要么在十维欧氏超对称问题上留有未解决问题，要么依赖部分涂抹到余维一。

Method: 在欧氏IIB型超引力中构造D(-1)/D7束缚态，分类局部余维二解，并证明超对称场方程可约化为两个受单一复约束的全纯函数；候选束缚态解需要互补的D3膜通量，这与此前D7世界体积分析一致。

Result: 得到一类局部余维二解；候选束缚态解需互补D3膜通量；特别有趣的解具有常数dilaton，并连接无穷远真空与受控标度极限（几何变为R×S¹×T⁸）。

Conclusion: 成功构造了欧氏IIB型超引力中D(-1)/D7束缚态，解决了此前超引力构造在十维欧氏超对称和余维问题上的遗留困难，并揭示了一个连接真空与T⁸标度极限的常数dilaton解。

Abstract: We construct D($-$1)/D7 bound states in Euclidean type IIB supergravity motivated by the string-theory setup of Billò et al., whose open-string description realizes instantons in eight-dimensional super Yang--Mills theory. Previous supergravity constructions either left unresolved issues concerning ten-dimensional Euclidean supersymmetry or relied on partial smearing to codimension one. We classify the local codimension-two solutions by showing that the supersymmetric field equations reduce to two holomorphic functions subject to a single complex constraint. The candidate bound-state solutions require complementary D3-brane fluxes, consistent with the D7 worldvolume analysis. A particularly interesting solution has constant dilaton and connects the vacuum at infinity to a controlled scaling limit in which the geometry becomes $\mathbb R\times S^1\times\mathbb T^8$.

</details>


### [103] [A ménage à quatre without strings attached](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15424)
*Iosif Bena,Gongrui Cheng,Klaas Parmentier,Dimitrios Toulikas,Thomas Van Riet*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究D膜交叉时的Hanany-Witten效应：通常两对D膜交叉会各自产生基本弦，但作者构造了一种D0-D8-D4-D4'四重束缚态，其中HW弦消失，并给出通量需满足的二次约束关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两对D膜（各自本可产生HW弦）能否结合为不带HW弦的四重束缚态，并理解其通量约束与超对称结构。

Method: 在超引力中显式构造D0-D8-D4-D4'四重态背景，要求无HW弦从而推出通量间的二次关系；并用D8膜的非阿贝尔DBI作用量的超对称解实现该束缚态，验证其Killing旋量与体超引力解匹配，将带通量的D8堆作为两个超对称背景间的畴壁。

Result: 成功构造无HW弦的D0-D8-D4-D4'四重束缚态；无弦条件给出通量的二次约束；该态可作为D8膜堆的非阿贝尔DBI超对称解；带通量D8堆充当两背景间的畴壁，通量跳变对应其携带的荷。

Conclusion: 两对会产生HW弦的膜对可结合为无弦的四重束缚态，其存在受限于通量的二次约束，并能在超引力与D膜有效作用量两个层面一致实现。

Abstract: When two D-branes with eight mixed Dirichlet-Neumann directions cross, a fundamental string is created. This is the Hanany-Witten (HW) effect. In supergravity, the string shows up as an electric NSNS B-field sourced by the RR fields of the two branes. We show that two brane pairs, each of which would create an HW string on its own, can combine into a quadruple bound state with no HW strings attached. We explicitly construct a background with the fluxes of such a D0-D8-D4-D4' quadruple. Requiring no HW strings imposes a quadratic relation among the fluxes. We also realize this bound state as a supersymmetric solution of the non-Abelian DBI action of the D8-branes, whose Killing spinors match those of the bulk supergravity solution. The fluxed D8 stack acts as a domain wall between two supersymmetric D0-D8-D4-D4' backgrounds. Each background satisfies the no-string constraint, and the jumps in the fluxes give the charges carried by the stack.

</details>


### [104] [Spectra and Central Charges of Gauged Curvature Squared Supergravities in Five Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15444)
*Erol Aksoy,Cagatay Okyay,Mehmet Ozkan,Cemal Berfu Senisik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究五维 N=2 离壳超引力在加入 Weyl 平方、Ricci 张量平方、Ricci 标量平方及其 Gauss-Bonnet 组合等高阶曲率不变量后的谱与中心荷，精确到耦合常数并保留全部辅助场，围绕极大超对称 AdS5 真空组织超多重态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶曲率项对五维 N=2 超引力的谱、幺正性/鬼场问题及中心荷($a,c$、味中心荷)的影响尚未被系统厘清，特别是各不变量单独与 Gauss-Bonnet 组合所引入的新模式及其物理后果。

Method: 在离壳五维 N=2 超引力中精确处理耦合并保留全部辅助场，将谱按极大超对称 AdS5 真空组织为超多重态；分别分析 Ricci 标量平方、Weyl 平方、Ricci 张量平方及 Gauss-Bonnet 组合的二次作用量；精确计算中心荷 a、c 与味中心荷，并考察真空方程与 a-最大化、c-极值化以及 AdS3×Σ2 背景上的二维中心荷与高 KK 能级谱行为。

Result: Ricci 标量平方不变量使谱增加一个长矢量多重态，且在耦合的有限范围内无鬼场；Weyl 平方或 Ricci 张量平方不变量使谱增加一个长自旋-2 多重态，且始终含鬼场或不稳定性；Gauss-Bonnet 组合不引入新模式，其超对称真空附近二次作用量是两导数理论作用量的重标度副本，从而确定 Ricci 张量平方扩展的谱；真空方程即 a-最大化，无质量引力子与矢量多重态的动能项分别正比于 c 和味中心荷，故大质量自旋-2 多重态与引力子多重态简并的临界点是 c 的零点；在 Gauss-Bonnet 扩展的超对称 AdS3×Σ2 背景上，二维中心荷由 c-极值化重现，而谱一般非幺正且含能量在大 KK 能级保持有界的态塔，在参数空间部分区域自旋-2 多重态塔有正范数，故谱无有限态密度。

Conclusion: 高阶曲率不变量对五维 N=2 超引力谱的影响显著且依赖具体不变量：Ricci 标量平方在有限耦合范围内保持无鬼场，Weyl/Ricci 张量平方型则必然破坏幺正性；Gauss-Bonnet 组合作为特殊情形仅重标度两导数理论并控制 Ricci 张量平方的谱，且中心荷(a、c、味)与真空、临界点及二维背景存在精确的相互关系（a-最大化、c-极值化、c 零点对应临界点）。

Abstract: We study the spectra and the central charges of off-shell gauged five-dimensional $\mathcal N=2$ supergravity extended by the Weyl squared, Ricci tensor squared and Ricci scalar squared invariants, and by their Gauss-Bonnet combination. Working exactly in the couplings and keeping all auxiliary fields, we organize the spectra around the maximally supersymmetric AdS$_5$ vacuum into supermultiplets. With the Ricci scalar squared invariant, the spectrum gains a long vector multiplet and is free of ghosts in a finite range of the coupling, while with the Weyl squared or Ricci tensor squared invariant, it gains a long spin-2 multiplet and always contains ghosts or instabilities. The Gauss-Bonnet combination adds no new modes: around its supersymmetric vacuum, the quadratic action is a rescaled copy of that of the two-derivative theory, and this fixes the spectrum of the Ricci tensor squared extension. We also compute the central charges $a$ and $c$ and the flavor central charge exactly in the couplings. The vacuum equation is $a$-maximization, and the kinetic terms of the massless graviton and vector multiplets are proportional to $c$ and to the flavor central charge, so that the critical points, where the massive spin-2 multiplet becomes degenerate with the graviton multiplet, are zeros of $c$. Finally, around supersymmetric AdS$_3\timesΣ_2$ backgrounds of the Gauss-Bonnet extension, the two-dimensional central charges are reproduced by $c$-extremization, while the spectrum is generically not unitary and contains towers of states whose energies stay bounded at large Kaluza-Klein levels. In part of the parameter space, the tower of spin-2 multiplets has positive norm, so that the spectrum has no finite density of states.

</details>


### [105] [Radiation from scattering on multiple shockwaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15468)
*Tim Adamo,Anton Ilderton,Hannah Kingham*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在多个零电磁冲击波背景下精确计算带电探针散射及单光子发射的振幅，进而在独立散射与干涉散射两种情形下计算辐射密度，复现并推广了LPM抑制效应，并发现脉冲/超短平面波情形下谱的抑制更为强烈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典LPM效应通常依赖近似处理（线性散射中心、高能探针、软光子等），作者希望在一个能够对多重散射及光子发射做精确计算的物理场景中研究该效应，从而验证并推广已知结论，同时探索源自由情形下的新现象。

Method: 考虑带电探针在多个零电磁冲击波（null electromagnetic shockwaves）上的散射，利用冲击波的横向剖面，对任意运动学精确求解弹性散射与单光子发射的振幅；随后对冲击波在横向平面及光前时间上的位置做双重平均，计算辐射密度，并区分独立散射与干涉散射两种机制。

Result: 对于横向径向对称的冲击剖面（包括超boost的库仑场），在更一般的运动学条件下复现了已知的LPM谱抑制；此外，对于脉冲/超短平面波上的多重散射（源自由情形），发现LPM区间内谱的抑制比常规情形强得多。

Conclusion: 通过精确可解的多重零冲击波散射模型，作者复现并推广了LPM效应，并揭示了源自由脉冲平面波情形下更强的谱抑制，为LPM抑制机制的精确研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: It is well known that radiation produced by scattering an electron off multiple sources depends on the length of the mean free path between these scatterings. When this is sufficiently small, the spectrum is suppressed due to destructive interference, a famous result known as the Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal (LPM) effect. This is usually modelled approximately, with scattering centres treated linearly (e.g., as screened Coulomb potentials), high-energy probes and soft emitted photons. In this paper, we consider a scenario where multiple scatterings can be computed exactly: charged probe scattering on multiple null electromagnetic shockwaves. Here, the amplitudes for elastic scattering and single photon emission can be determined exactly, for arbitrary kinematics, in terms of the shock profiles in the transverse plane. We use these amplitudes to compute the radiation density (doubly-averaged over shock locations in the transverse plane and lightfront time) in the regimes of independent and interfering scatterings. For shock profiles which are radially symmetric in the transverse plane (including the ultraboosted Coulomb field), we recover the known LPM suppression, albeit with more general kinematics. In addition, our setup allows us to consider the source-free case of multiple scattering on impulsive/ultra-short plane waves, where we find much stronger suppression of the spectrum in the LPM regime.

</details>


### [106] [Numerical Thermodynamics of AdS Black Holes with Higher-Order Mass Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15470)
*Saad Eddine Baddis,Adil Belhaj,Hajar Belmahi,Yassine Hassouni,Maryem Jemri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在非交换几何与精髓（quintessence）启发下，构造了一类新的渐近反德西特黑洞解，其特征是含有高阶质量形变项 $aM^{n-1}/r^n$（含参数 $a$ 与 $n$），并借助 CUDA 并行编程数值研究了其热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受非交换几何与 quintessence（精髓/暗能量）思想的启发，希望拓展渐近反德西特（AdS）黑洞解的种类，并研究由高阶质量形变项引入的复杂热力学行为。由于解析处理困难，转而采用数值模拟手段来刻画黑洞的热力学性质。

Method: 构造一类含高阶质量形变项 $\frac{aM^{n-1}}{r^n}$（参数为 $a$ 与 $n$）的渐近 AdS 黑洞解；因解析求解困难，采用 CUDA 代码进行并行编程数值模拟，针对 $n=3$、$n=4$、$n=5$ 三种模型，数值研究热力学性质，包括稳定性、$P$-$v$ 临界性以及相变行为。

Result: 通过数值模拟获得了 $n=3,4,5$ 情形下的热力学结果，刻画了黑洞的稳定性、$P$-$v$ 临界性以及相变特征（具体数值结果未在摘要中展开）。

Conclusion: 构建了新的含高阶质量形变项的渐近 AdS 黑洞解，并证明了利用 CUDA 并行数值模拟可以有效研究其热力学（稳定性、$P$-$v$ 临界性与相变），为解析难以处理的引力-热力学问题提供了可行的计算途径。

Abstract: Inspired by noncommutative geometry and quintessence, we construct a new class of asymptotically anti-de Sitter black hole solutions characterized by a higher-order mass deformation term $\frac{aM^{n-1}}{r^n}$ involving two parameters, $a$ and $n$. Due to analytical difficulties, we approach the underlying thermodynamics through parallel programming simulations using CUDA codes. By considering models with $n=3$, $n=4$, and $n=5$, we numerically study certain thermodynamic properties, including the stability, the $P$-$v$ criticality, and the phase transitions.

</details>


### [107] [BPS invariants from resurgent topological string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15480)
*Jie Gu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了拓扑弦自由能的复现（resurgence）结构与同一 Calabi-Yau 三流形上紧化的 II 型弦论 BPS 不变量之间猜想性关联的最新进展：Borel 奇点位于与稳定 D 膜束缚态中心荷对应的整周期处，而 Stokes 常数编码其 BPS 指标（广义 DT 不变量）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是系统梳理并归纳拓扑弦非微扰效应与 BPS 态计数之间对应关系的猜想及各类支撑性论证，为后续寻求一般性证明和非微扰完备化指明方向。

Method: 先引入复现理论与微扰拓扑弦，再通过全纯反常方程（holomorphic anomaly equations）的 trans-series 解确定非微扰振幅；在局部与紧致 Calabi-Yau 几何中展示该对应，并讨论其在不同极化与模参数变换下的行为；随后综述来自精确 WKB 分析、blowup 方程、平缓坐标与对偶配分函数的 Stokes 自同构、以及 Kontsevich-Soibelman 墙交叉（wall-crossing）等支撑论据。

Result: 所综述的图景将 Borel 奇点定位于与稳定 D 膜束缚态中心荷相关的整周期，Stokes 常数编码 BPS 指标/广义 DT 不变量；trans-series 解能确定非微扰振幅，且在局部与紧致几何中均得到印证，各类独立方法（精确 WKB、blowup 方程、Stokes 自同构、墙交叉）相互支持。

Conclusion: 尽管对应关系得到多方证据支持，仍存在若干开放问题：缺乏一般性证明、非微扰振幅的物理解释尚不明确，以及如何构造完整的非微扰化（non-perturbative completions）。

Abstract: We review recent progress on the conjectural relation between the resurgent structure of topological string free energies and BPS invariants of type II string theory compactified on the same Calabi-Yau threefold. In this picture, Borel singularities are located at integral periods associated with central charges of stable D-brane bound states, while Stokes constants encode their BPS indices, or generalized DT invariants. After introducing resurgence and perturbative topological strings, we explain how trans-series solutions of the holomorphic anomaly equations determine non-perturbative amplitudes and illustrate the proposed correspondence in local and compact geometries. We discuss the behavior under changes of polarization and moduli, and review supporting arguments based on exact WKB analysis, blowup equations, the Stokes automorphisms of flat coordinates and dual partition functions, and Kontsevich-Soibelman wall-crossing. We conclude with open questions concerning a general proof, the interpretation of non-perturbative amplitudes, and the construction of non-perturbative completions.

</details>


### [108] [Holographic dual of the 2d Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15484)
*Kazumi Okuyama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出用实心环上的 Turaev-Viro TQFT 作为环面上二维 Ising 模型的全息对偶，通过核心区制备缺陷态、三明治区计算态求和，并利用 2-骨架 P 离散化体时空来联系缺陷、融合图与床面高度变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为环面上的二维 Ising 模型构造一个显式的全息对偶描述，将边界临界理论与体态拓扑量子场论（TQFT）联系起来，并自然容纳缺陷、弦网融合图以及高度变量等结构。

Method: 以实心环上的 Turaev-Viro TQFT 作为体理论；将体时空划分为"核心区"与"三明治区"，核心区在拓扑边界 D 上制备缺陷态 |D⟩，三明治区计算 Turaev-Viro 态求和 ⟨D|Π|F⟩；物理边界 B 上的融合图 F 由 Aasen-Fendley-Mong 构造给出；D 与 B 之间引入"床面" S 承载高度变量（Ising 自旋）；体时空由插值 D 与 F 的 2-骨架 P 离散化。

Result: 当缺陷 D 缺失时，2-骨架 P 终止于床面 S，并可解释为弦网 F 的体世界面；整个构造给出了环面上 Ising 模型的 Turaev-Viro 全息对偶框架，将缺陷态、态求和、融合图与高度变量统一纳入体几何描述。

Conclusion: 通过把二维 Ising 模型的全息对偶实现为实心环上的 Turaev-Viro TQFT 态求和，成功建立了边界弦网、缺陷态与体 2-骨架之间的清晰对应，为理解二维共形场论/格点模型的拓扑全息提供了具体构造。

Abstract: We propose a holographic dual of the 2d Ising model on a torus as the Turaev-Viro TQFT on a solid torus. The bulk spacetime is divided into the ``core'' region and the ``sandwich'' region. The core region prepares a defect state $|\mathcal{D}\rangle$ on the topological boundary $\mathbb{D}$. The sandwich region computes the Turaev-Viro state sum $\langle\mathcal{D}|Π|\mathcal{F}\rangle$ between the state $|\mathcal{D}\rangle$ on $\mathbb{D}$ and the state $|\mathcal{F}\rangle$ on the physical boundary $\mathbb{B}$. The fusion diagram (or string-net) $\mathcal{F}$ on $\mathbb{B}$ is given by the Aasen-Fendley-Mong construction. There is also a ``bedding surface'' $\mathbb{S}$ between $\mathbb{D}$ and $\mathbb{B}$, and the height variables (or Ising spins inside/outside of the domain wall) are naturally living on $\mathbb{S}$. The bulk spacetime is discretized by the 2-skeleton $P$ which interpolates $\mathcal{D}$ and $\mathcal{F}$. When the defect $\mathcal{D}$ is absent, the 2-skeleton $P$ ends on the bedding surface $\mathbb{S}$ and $P$ is interpreted as the bulk worldsheet of the string-net $\mathcal{F}$.

</details>


### [109] [1-loop partition function of 11d supergravity on $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$: matching $A(k)$ in ABJM free energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15510)
*Stefan A. Kurlyand,Arkady A. Tseytlin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现，此前基于 S^7 标准 KK 谱计算的 11d 超引力在 AdS4×S^7/Zk 上的单圈配分函数无法重现 ABJM 理论自由能中的 A(k)。作者识别出缺失的贡献来自由欧氏 AdS4 上平方可积调和 2-形式（张量 S^7 模式）构造的 3-形式场涨落，这部分不在标准 KK 谱中。补入并做 Zk 投影、用 zeta 函数正规化后，成功重现 A(k)。类似方法应用于 3d ADHM 理论（其 M 理论对偶为 AdS4×S^7/Zp 轨道折叠，沿固定轨迹含 7d SU(p) 矢量多重态）同样取得成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前基于标准 KK 谱的 11d 超引力单圈计算无法重现由局域化精确得到的 ABJM 自由能中 A(k)，说明超引力一侧存在被遗漏的单圈贡献，需要找出并修正这一不一致。

Method: 作者识别出缺失贡献来自由欧氏 AdS4 上平方可积调和 2-形式与 S^7 模式张量积构成的 3-形式场涨落（标准 KK 谱未包含）。将其加入以超共形特征标表示的 KK 塔贡献，并施加 Zk 投影，经 zeta 函数正规化求值。对 3d ADHM 理论，加入沿固定轨迹 AdS4×S^3 上 7d SU(p) 矢量多重态的局域化 KK 模式，再与 Zp 投影后的超引力谱相加。

Result: 补入缺失的 3-形式场涨落、做 Zk 投影并 zeta 正规化后，总表达式精确重现 ABJM 理论的 A(k)；对 ADHM 情形，加入局域化 KK 模式后的单圈自由能重现其局域化对应量 A(k)。

Conclusion: 超引力单圈计算与场论局域化结果之间的不一致可归因于标准 KK 谱遗漏的（平方可积调和形式构造的）3-形式场涨落；补入该贡献后两端精确一致，且该方法可推广到含轨道折叠奇点（局域化矢量多重态）的 ADHM 情形。

Abstract: The function $A(k)$ appearing in the large $N$ expansion of free energy of ABJM theory on $S^3$ is known exactly from localization. An earlier calculation of the 1-loop partition function of 11d supergravity on $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb Z_k$, based on the standard KK spectrum on $S^7$, did not reproduce it. We identify a missing 1-loop supergravity contribution as that of the 3-form field fluctuations built from square-integrable harmonic 2-forms on Euclidean $\mathrm{AdS}_4$ tensored with $S^7$ modes, which are not captured by the KK spectrum. Adding it to the KK tower contribution expressed in terms of superconformal characters and applying the $\mathbb{Z}_k$ projection, the total expression simplifies, and its evaluation in a zeta-function prescription reproduces the expression for $A(k)$. We also apply a similar approach to the 3d ADHM theory whose M-theory dual is the orbifold $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_p$, which has singularity along the fixed locus $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^3$, supporting a 7d $SU(p)$ vector multiplet. Adding the localized KK modes of this multiplet to the $\mathbb{Z}_p$-projected supergravity spectrum gives a 1-loop free energy that reproduces the ADHM localization counterpart of $A(k)$.

</details>


### [110] [Schwinger-Unruh Interplay and Mode-Resolved Entanglement of Charged Scalar Fields in Rindler Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15623)
*Shagun Kaushal,Himanshu Parihar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究Rindler时空中带电标量场在平行电磁场背景下的成对及跨视界关联，将磁场量子化为朗道能级并计算四模高斯态的对数负性与量子互信息，发现跨楔反粒子-粒子对仅当q2<tanh r_U时纠缠，而整体右-左楔划分的纠缠由一对集体模承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平行电场与磁场共存时，横磁场产生朗道能级，纵模等效为有效质量M_n^2=m^2+(2n+1)|qB|的带电标量Rindler问题。作者希望厘清在选定全局真空（负频区）下，出射四模高斯态的模解析纠缠结构，尤其是跨楔与楔内关联的区别，并避免将真空依赖的混合参数直接等同于探测器温度。

Method: 在负频区指定全局真空，得到出射四模高斯态；对选定模对计算对数负性（logarithmic negativity）与量子互信息（quantum mutual information）；利用高斯施密特分解（Gaussian Schmidt decomposition）分析完整右-左楔划分的整体纠缠，并讨论朗道能级依赖及各模解析结论的适用范围。

Result: 跨楔反粒子-粒子对仅在q2<tanh r_U时纠缠，否则变为可分但其互信息仍非零；楔内粒子-反粒子对在非零Schwinger压缩下保持纠缠；整体右-左楔划分的纠缠由一对集体模承载，即使所选出射跨楔对已可分；结论受朗道能级影响且不将真空依赖混合参数识别为探测器温度。

Conclusion: 模解析的纠缠判断（单个跨楔对是否纠缠）与整体楔划分的纠缠并不完全对应：整体跨楔纠缠由集体模承载，因而在单个出射对可分时仍存在。该结果表明在平行电磁场与Rindler背景下需区分模式级与划分级的纠缠结构，并谨慎处理真空依赖参数与温度的关系。

Abstract: We study pairwise and cross-horizon correlations of a charged scalar field in Rindler spacetime in the presence of parallel electric and magnetic fields. The transverse magnetic field produces Landau levels, while the longitudinal mode problem follows the charged-scalar Rindler construction with the effective mass $M_n^2=m^2+(2n+1)|qB|$. For a specified global vacuum in the negative-frequency sector, we obtain the outgoing four-mode Gaussian state and calculate logarithmic negativity and quantum mutual information for selected mode pairs. We find that the cross-wedge antiparticle-particle pair is entangled only when $q_2<\tanh r_U$. It can therefore become separable although its mutual information remains nonzero. The intra-wedge particle-antiparticle pair remains entangled for nonzero Schwinger squeezing. Using the Gaussian Schmidt decomposition, we show that the entanglement across the complete right--left wedge partition is carried by a pair of collective modes, even when the selected outgoing cross-wedge pair becomes separable. Using the Gaussian Schmidt decomposition, we show that the entanglement across the complete right--left wedge partition is carried by a pair of collective modes, even when the selected outgoing cross-wedge pair becomes separable. We discuss the Landau-level dependence and the limits of these mode-resolved conclusions, without identifying the vacuum-dependent mixing parameter with a detector temperature.

</details>


### [111] [Entropy and charge transport limit Mpemba preparation in holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15654)
*Yu Shi,Sheng Long*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全反作用全息超导体中的Mpemba弛豫及其制备代价，发现在匹配末态能量与电荷下边界驱动可翻转源移除后剩余视界熵的排序；沿临界慢分支，视界位移抵消二次面积响应，仅留下O(ε⁴)的熵储备，并由此给出制备时间至少按ε⁻²增长的界，电荷输运进一步强化该时间下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究全息超导体中Mpemba现象的物理机制与可行性，并量化通过边界驱动实现快速弛豫（乃至超越平衡态）所需的制备代价，特别是理解临界慢标量场如何限制熵储备与制备时间的下限。

Method: 在带全反作用的全息超导框架下，固定末态能量与电荷，数值比较不同边界驱动协议在移除源后剩余视界熵的排序；考虑携带慢标量场与外部电荷的临界态，沿临界慢分支展开分析，利用视界正则性与径向均匀控制等条件建立熵储备的O(ε⁴)标度，并推导制备时间下界及电荷输运对协变标量通量与最小通量代价的约束。

Result: 匹配末态能量与电荷时，某些协议在计入驱动与等待时间后仍能更早达到给定容差；沿临界慢分支，视界位移抵消二次面积响应，熵储备为O(ε⁴)（ε为初始标量振幅），且同一系数将该储备与邻近超导平衡态的熵优势及正常侧慢尾的熵权重联系起来；压制主导视界场与外部电荷的释放模式下，制备时间至少按ε⁻²增长，电荷输运约束同一协变标量通量并提高最小通量代价，从而强化时间下界。

Conclusion: 全息超导体的Mpemba弛豫可由边界驱动实现，但有限的O(ε⁴)熵储备严格限制了已有慢场的移除速度，且电荷输运通过约束产生视界熵的同一标量通量进一步抬高代价并加强制备时间下界，揭示快速弛豫优势与制备成本之间的基本权衡。

Abstract: We study Mpemba relaxation and its preparation cost in a holographic superconductor with full backreaction. At matched final energy and charge, boundary drives can reverse the ordering of the remaining horizon entropy after source removal. In separate numerical comparisons, some protocols reach a given tolerance earlier even when driving and waiting times are included. To examine the limits of preparation, we consider critical states that already carry a slow scalar field and exterior charge. Along the critical slow branch at fixed energy and charge, horizon displacement cancels the quadratic area response, leaving an entropy reserve of $O(\varepsilon^4)$ for an initial scalar amplitude $\varepsilon$. The same coefficient connects this reserve to the entropy advantage of the nearby superconducting equilibrium and the entropy weight of the slow tail on the normal side. This limited reserve constrains how quickly the existing field can be removed. For releases that suppress the leading horizon field and exterior charge, the preparation time grows at least as $\varepsilon^{-2}$ under horizon regularity and uniform radial control. Charge transport constrains the same covariant scalar flux that produces horizon entropy, raising the minimum flux cost and strengthening the time bound.

</details>


### [112] [Triple-Massive-Exchange Bispectrum of Quasi-Single-Field Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15660)
*Xingang Chen,Zhong-Zhi Xianyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过将 Mellin-Barnes 表示直接应用于无质量与有质量模式之间的混合传播子，成功解析求解了拟单场暴胀模型的三交换双谱（triple-exchange bispectrum），将其表示为对一般质量与混合强度均收敛的双重级数，结果精确、非微扰且物理含义清晰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟单场暴胀模型的三交换双谱自提出以来，尽管宇宙学对撞机物理进展迅速，但一直无法在其完整参数空间内完成解析求解。这一解析空白阻碍了对该模型非高斯信号的理论理解与精确预言，因此需要一种能够覆盖一般质量和混合强度的精确解析方法。

Method: 作者将 Mellin-Barnes 表示直接施加于无质量模式与有质量模式之间的混合传播子上，从而把三交换双谱表达为一个对一般质量和混合强度均收敛的双重级数，实现了全参数空间的精确解析求解。

Result: 得到了拟单场暴胀三交换双谱的精确、非微扰的双重级数解析表达式，该级数在一般质量与混合强度参数空间内收敛，物理含义透明。

Conclusion: 本文成功填补了拟单场暴胀三交换双谱全参数空间解析求解的空白，所提出的 Mellin-Barnes 方法具有广泛适用性，为宇宙学对撞机物理及相关领域提供了新的有力计算工具。

Abstract: The triple-exchange bispectrum of the scalar quasi-single-field inflation model has resisted analytic evaluation across its full parameter space since its advent, despite rapid progress in cosmological collider physics. In this Letter, we solve it for general mass and mixing strength as a convergent double series, by applying the Mellin-Barnes representation directly to the mixed propagator between the massless and massive modes. The result is exact, non-perturbative, and physically transparent; the method is broadly applicable.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum and classical thermal equilibrium for probabilistic classical fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15749)
*C. Wetterich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证经典概率场的演化可用量子形式描述，自由经典场对应自由量子场论；经典热平衡（瑞利-金斯谱）与量子热平衡（普朗克谱）的选择不是基本概念问题，而是相互作用下的动力学选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示经典概率分布与量子场论之间的对应关系，并阐明在存在相互作用时，经典场体系的渐近动力学究竟会选择瑞利-金斯谱还是普朗克谱这一动力学选择问题。

Method: 利用量子形式描述经典场的概率分布时间演化；对自由经典场构造一般的“经典-量子映射”，将经典概率分布映射到量子场论的密度矩阵；通过比较经典与量子平衡态下能量和场关联的动量依赖关系，并借助含相互作用的概率经典场的数值模拟来判断所选平衡态。

Result: 自由经典场可自然嵌入为自由量子场论的一个子系统；不同经典概率分布分别对应瑞利-金斯发散的经典热平衡或普朗克谱的量子热平衡；谱的选择取决于含相互作用时的动力学选择，而非基本概念差异。

Conclusion: 瑞利-金斯谱与普朗克谱的取舍是由相互作用下的渐近动力学决定的，而非基础理论的概念分歧；通过经典-量子映射可统一描述自由经典概率场与量子场论，并可用数值模拟判定实际被选中的平衡态。

Abstract: The time evolution of the probability distribution for classical fields can be described by the quantum formalism. For free classical fields the free quantum field theory arises as a subsystem. Different probability distributions for classical fields correspond to the classical thermal equilibrium with the Rayleigh-Jeans divergence or the quantum thermal equilibrium with the Planck spectrum. The selection of the Rayleigh-Jeans or Planck spectrum is not a question of fundamental concepts. It becomes an issue of dynamical selection once interactions are present. We compare the momentum dependence of the classical energy and field correlations for the classical and quantum equilibrium. This permits to decide which equilibrium is selected as a result of asymptotic dynamics by numerical simulations of the probabilistic classical fields including interactions. For free probabilistic classical fields we construct a general ``classical-quantum map'' between the classical probability distribution and the density matrix of the quantum field theory.

</details>


### [114] [Extension of Poincare superalgebra via hidden symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15946)
*Ümit Ertem*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过引入特殊奇 Killing-Yano 形式和特殊偶闭共形 Killing-Yano 形式，将 Poincaré 超代数的奇部分从 Killing 矢量场及其旋量表示推广到高阶微分形式，并结合 Killing 旋量构造出 Poincaré 超代数的扩展结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流形的等距对称性（Killing 矢量场）与 Killing 旋量共同构成对应 Poincaré 超代数的对称超代数结构。作者希望把 Killing 矢量场向高阶形式的反对称推广（Killing-Yano 形式）纳入同一超代数框架，从而揭示隐藏对称性与 Killing 旋量之间的代数联系。

Method: 将 Poincaré 超代数的奇部分由 Killing 矢量场及其旋量表示所在空间，扩展为特殊奇 Killing-Yano 形式与特殊偶闭共形 Killing-Yano 形式所在空间；偶部分仍对应于 Killing 旋量空间。通过显式定义相应的括号（代数运算），证明这些对象共同构成 Poincaré 超代数的一个扩展。

Result: 证明了隐藏对称性（特殊形式的 Killing-Yano 与闭共形 Killing-Yano 形式）连同 Killing 旋量一起，在恰当定义的括号运算下封闭，构成 Poincaré 超代数的一个扩展。

Conclusion: Killing-Yano 形式所刻画的隐藏对称性与 Killing 旋量并非彼此独立，而是可以统一纳入一个扩展的 Poincaré 超代数结构中，从而为理解流形上高阶对称性与旋量几何的融合提供了代数框架。

Abstract: Isometries of a manifold and special types of spinors called Killing spinors which are related to Killing vector fields constitute a symmetry superalgebra structure corresponding to Poincare superalgebra. The antisymmetric generalization of Killing vector fields to higher degree forms which are called Killing-Yano forms are considered. The odd part of the Poincare superalgebra is extended from the space of Killing vector fields and their rotational representation to the space of special odd Killing-Yano forms and special even closed conformal Killing-Yano forms. The even part is again corresponds to the space of Killing spinors. By defining the relevant brackets, we show that hidden symmetries and Killing spinors together define an extension of the Poincare superalgebra.

</details>


### [115] [Harmonic superspace and Kaluza-Klein reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15782)
*Nikita Zaigraev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在谐波超空间框架下推广了Kaluza-Klein约化程序，通过对谐波导数施加质量项变形，构造了保持离壳超对称与Grassmann解析性的超场表述，并成功应用于5D Maxwell理论和线性化5D Poincaré超引力，得到4D、N=2巨矢量与巨超引力多重态的离壳作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为4D、N=2 BPS巨质量多重态（尤其是巨质量高自旋多重态）建立规范不变的离壳超场表述，并进一步推广到4D、N=1巨质量高自旋理论。

Method: 将Kaluza-Klein约化推广到谐波超空间：通过对谐波导数施加质量项进行变形，全程保持显式离壳超对称性与Grassmann解析性。将解析前势与谐波导数中自然编码的规范对称结构加以利用，应用于约化自由的5D、N=1 Maxwell理论及线性化5D、N=1 Poincaré超引力。

Result: 约化5D、N=1 Maxwell理论得到BPS 4D、N=2巨质量矢量超多重态的离壳超场描述，该表述自然包含解析的Stückelberg型规范对称性；约化线性化5D、N=1 Poincaré超引力得到BPS 4D、N=2巨质量超引力多重态的无约束谐波超空间作用量。

Conclusion: 该方法为4D、N=2巨质量高自旋BPS多重态的规范不变离壳超场表述开辟了直接路径，所得理论还可改写为4D、N=1超空间形式，从而为4D、N=1巨质量高自旋理论的规范不变离壳描述铺平道路。

Abstract: We extend the Kaluza-Klein reduction procedure to the framework of harmonic superspace and apply it to the construction of off-shell superfield formulations of supersymmetric gauge multiplets. The reduction is implemented by a deformation of the harmonic derivatives by mass terms, while ensuring that manifest off-shell supersymmetry and Grassmann analyticity are preserved at all stages. As a first application, we reduce the free \(5D,\mathcal{N}=1\) Maxwell theory and obtain an off-shell superfield description of the BPS \(4D,\mathcal{N}=2\) massive vector supermultiplet. The resulting formulation naturally incorporates an analytic Stückelberg-type gauge symmetry. We then reduce linearized \(5D,\mathcal{N}=1\) Poincaré supergravity and derive the unconstrained harmonic-superspace action for the BPS \(4D,\mathcal{N}=2\) massive supergravity multiplet. The technical simplicity of the procedure stems from the structure of gauge theories in harmonic superspace, where the gauge symmetry is naturally encoded in analytic prepotentials and harmonic derivatives. The approach presented in this paper opens a direct route to gauge-invariant off-shell superfield formulations of massive higher-spin $4D,\mathcal{N}=2$ BPS multiplets. Furthermore, the resulting theories can be reformulated in $4D,\mathcal{N}=1$ superspace, opening the way to gauge-invariant off-shell descriptions of $4D,\mathcal{N}=1$ massive higher-spin theories.

</details>


### [116] [Holographic Wilsonian RG of Yang-Mills theory in AdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16074)
*Soner Albayrak*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将随机量子化与威尔逊重整化之间的平衡-演化对偶性推广到反德西特空间中的非阿贝尔规范理论，其中 AdS 的径向坐标充当随机时间。作者证明，对于无显式径向依赖的体理论，任意场阶下的 Langevin 分布在时刻 t 等于紫外与红外径向波函数在截断 ε=t 处的乘积，从而将该 Langevin 过程识别为径向演化的基态（Doob）变换。此外，文中扩展了随机-重整化对偶的字典，并给出全息威尔逊作用量在有限截断处的三重迹与四重迹耦合的闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将既有的随机量子化与威尔逊重整化之间的对偶关系推广到更一般的时空背景（反德西特空间）与非阿贝尔规范理论，以深化对全息重整化群流、体理论径向演化与随机过程之间联系的理解。

Method: 作者将 AdS 径向坐标视为随机时间，利用 Langevin 演化与径向（重整化群）演化的对偶性，对无显式径向依赖的体理论证明到场的任意阶其 Langevin 分布等于紫外与红外径向波函数在截断 ε=t 处的乘积，从而将该过程识别为径向演化的基态（Doob）变换。通过显式计算，建立了若干词典对应关系，并在树级、AdS₄ 情形下推导出有限截断全息威尔逊作用量的三重迹与四重迹耦合的闭式表达式。

Result: 得到三点核心结果：(1) 到场的任意阶，Langevin 分布为紫外与红外径向波函数的乘积，相应过程是径向演化的基态（Doob）变换；(2) 词典扩展：体场径向分量对应 Zwanziger 随机规范固定函数，径向规范对应 Parisi-Wu 量子化，体规范 A_z=α⁻¹∂ᵢAᵢ 对应 Zwanziger 规范；(3) 给出有限截断全息威尔逊作用量三重迹与四重迹耦合的闭式公式；在红外它们退化为已知的胶子关联函数，在总能极点处退化为平直空间 Yang-Mills 振幅，符合预期。

Conclusion: 成功将平衡-演化对偶性推广至 AdS 中的非阿贝尔规范理论，并通过显式构造确认了随机量子化、全息重整化与规范固定之间的深层对应关系。虽然结果主要在树级、AdS₄ 下给出，但作者指出这些陈述可对应推广到德西特空间以及考虑圈修正的情形。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the equilibrium-evolution duality between stochastic quantization and Wilsonian renormalization to non-abelian gauge theories in anti-de Sitter space, for which the radial coordinate of AdS plays the role of the stochastic time. We show, to all orders in the fields for bulk theories without explicit radial dependence, that the Langevin distribution at time $t$ is the product of the ultraviolet and infrared radial wavefunctions at the cutoff $ε=t$. We therefore identify this Langevin process as the ground-state (Doob) transform of the radial evolution. By explicit calculation, we also extend the existing dictionary in the stochastic-renormalization duality: the radial component of the bulk field is Zwanziger's stochastic gauge-fixing function; radial gauge is Parisi-Wu quantization; and the bulk gauge $A_z=α^{-1}\partial_iA_i$ is Zwanziger's gauge. We also obtain the expressions for the triple-trace and quadruple-trace couplings of the holographic Wilsonian action at finite cutoff in closed form; in the infrared, they reduce to the known gluon correlators and, at the total-energy pole, to flat-space Yang-Mills amplitudes as expected. Although our explicit results are provided at tree-level and derived in AdS$_4$, we also comment on the de Sitter counterpart of these statements and on the loop corrections.

</details>


### [117] [How to find regular black-hole islands in the swamp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15795)
*Astrid Eichhorn,Pedro G. S. Fernandes,Verena Koeder*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提�出一套基于高阶曲率修正的单调性要求，用以在有效场论有效范围内追踪黑洞奇点被逐步“软化”的过程，从而约束有效场论系数，并在八阶有效场论中找到了一个满足正则性条件的“岛屿”，进而检验弦论与渐近安全引力是否落于其上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限的更高阶曲率修正预期能给出正则黑洞解，但正则行为应该随有效场论逐阶逐步建立而非突变。作者希望将这一假设转化为对度规函数偏离史瓦西解的单调性要求，以便在有效场论有效范围内追踪“越来越不那么奇异”的黑洞行为，并据此约束理论系数。

Method: 假定正则行为随有效场论各阶逐步累积，且有效场论仅含一个负责消解奇点的新物理尺度。将这两个假设转化为度规函数相对史瓦西解偏离量的单调性约束条件，并在黑洞视界内外、有效场论有效范围内用这些条件追踪“较不奇异”行为的增强，从而形成对有效场论系数的限制。具体构造了五种条件，并在八阶有效场论中寻找满足这些条件的系数区域（正则性“岛屿”）。

Result: 在八阶有效场论中找到了一个对应于黑洞正则性条件的系数“岛屿”，即同时满足所提五条单调性约束的系数区域；并探究了弦论与渐近安全引力是否位于该岛屿上。

Conclusion: 通过将“正则行为随高阶曲率逐阶累积、且新物理仅含单一尺度”的假设形式化为度规偏离的单调性条件，可系统约束有效场论系数，并识别出满足黑洞正则性的系数区域，为判断弦论与渐近安全引力等候选理论是否具备相应正则行为提供了判据。

Abstract: An infinite tower of higher-curvature corrections is expected to lead to regular black-hole solutions. We assume that this regular behavior does not abruptly arise, but gradually builds up with each successive order of the effective field theory of gravity. In addition, we assume that the effective field theory comes with a single scale of singularity-resolving new physics. We translate this assumption into a monotonicity requirement for deviations of the metric functions from a Schwarzschild black hole. Under these assumptions, conditions can be formulated which trace the increase of ``less singular'' black-hole behavior on and outside of the black-hole horizon within the regime of validity of the effective field theory. These conditions constrain the effective field theory coefficients. To exemplify this construction, we motivate five specific conditions and find a corresponding island of black hole regularity conditions at order eight in the effective field theory. We also explore whether string theory as well as asymptotically safe gravity lie on this island.

</details>


### [118] [Generalized Kosmann derivative and symmetry operators on Dirac structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16309)
*Ümit Ertem,Özgür Açık,Özgür Kelekçi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 H-扭曲精确 Courant 代数胚上建立度规狄拉克结构的旋量对称性框架，证明正旋量 Kosmann–Dorfman 作用与内在 Kosmann 导数在保持广义度规的截面上的精确交织关系，推导 Killing–Yano 及其共形版本的积分恒等式，构造 Killing 旋量保持算子，并以 S^2×S^1 和 S^3 上的具体例子展示该框架，揭示广义等距、隐旋量对称性与通量敏感的 Lie 代数胚几何之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将广义几何中的度规狄拉克结构与旋量对称性统一起来：广义度规赋予狄拉克结构内在的黎曼 Lie 代数胚几何，并使其等距同构于广义切丛的正子丛。作者希望在不依赖辅助相容广义联络的前提下，刻画 Killing–Yano 型张量的积分恒等式与 Killing 旋量的对称性算子，从而理解通量（H-flux）如何影响隐含的旋量对称性与等距结构。

Method: 方法上，作者构建旋量对称性框架：(1) 对保持广义度规的截面证明正旋量 Kosmann–Dorfman 作用与内在 Kosmann 导数的精确交织关系，且该比较独立于辅助相容广义联络的选取；(2) 在内在几何中推导 KY 与 CKY 可积性恒等式；(3) 由奇特殊 KY 形式和偶特殊闭 CKY 形式构造 Killing 旋量保持算子；(4) 从具有虚 Killing 数的 Killing 旋量的 Hermite 双线性型中获得这些形式；(5) 在 S^2×S^1 上给出具有非平凡 H-flux 类与秩二锚映射的整体狄拉克结构的显式计算，并处理 S^3 上图像狄拉克结构的 Hermite 流括号及其奇 Jacobi 恒等式。 

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 正旋量 Kosmann–Dorfman 作用与内在 Kosmann 导数的精确交织，且与辅助相容联络无关；(2) 内在几何中的 KY/CKY 可积性恒等式，以及由特殊 KY 与特殊闭 CKY 形式构造的 Killing 旋量保持算子；(3) 这些形式可由具虚 Killing 数的 Killing 旋量的 Hermite 双线性型生成；(4) 在 S^2×S^1 上，旋联络与对称算子显式给出，Killing 旋量空间为横向族 C^∞(S^1, C^2)；(5) 内在 Killing 截面通过一个 Lie 代数表示作用于旋量空间；(6) 在 S^3 的图像狄拉克结构上，Hermite 流括号的奇 Jacobi 恒等式相差一个相位，加入单个偶相位生成元即得到 Lie 超代数。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了广义等距、隐藏的旋量对称性与通量敏感的 Lie 代数胚几何之间的系统性联系：度规狄拉克结构携带内在黎曼 Lie 代数胚几何，其上的 KY/CKY 恒等式与 Killing 旋量保持算子不依赖辅助联络，且对 H-flux 敏感。具体例子表明 Killing 旋量空间呈现横向族结构，且奇对称性在扭曲下可能产生相位偏差，需引入偶相位生成元方可闭合为 Lie 超代数，为后续研究通量背景下的广义等距与旋量对称性提供了统一的几何工具。

Abstract: We develop a spinorial symmetry framework for metric Dirac structures in $H$-twisted exact Courant algebroids. A generalized metric equips a Dirac structure with an intrinsic Riemannian Lie-algebroid geometry and identifies it isometrically with the positive subbundle of the generalized tangent bundle. For sections preserving the generalized metric, we prove an exact intertwining between the positive-spinor Kosmann--Dorfman action and the intrinsic Kosmann derivative. The comparison is independent of the auxiliary compatible generalized connection. Within the intrinsic geometry we derive Killing--Yano (KY) and conformal Killing--Yano (CKY) integrability identities, construct Killing-spinor-preserving operators from odd special KY and even special closed CKY forms, and obtain these forms from Hermitian bilinears of Killing spinors with imaginary Killing number. We illustrate the framework on a global Dirac structure over $S^2\times S^1$ with nontrivial $H$-flux class and rank-two anchor. Its spin connection and symmetry operators are explicit, and its Killing-spinor space is a transverse family $C^\infty(S^1,\mathbb C^2)$. Finally, intrinsic Killing sections act on Killing spinors by a Lie algebra representation. For a graph Dirac structure over $S^3$, the odd Jacobi identity of the Hermitian-current bracket fails by a phase, and adjoining a single even phase generator yields a Lie superalgebra. These results connect generalized isometries, hidden spinorial symmetries, and flux-sensitive Lie-algebroid geometry.

</details>


### [119] [Swing Surfaces in AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15854)
*Jacqueline Caminiti,Sabrina Pasterski*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究AdS₃/CFT₂在平坦极限下的全息纠缠熵，澄清了Ryu-Takayanagi曲面与平直空间全息中"摆动曲面"(swing surfaces)的关系，并为摆动曲面构造了信息论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究平坦极限下全息纠缠熵如何定义与解释，特别是厘清传统RT公式为何在平坦极限下失效，以及摆动曲面（swing surfaces）所对应的信息论含义。

Method: 作者研究了AdS₃/CFT₂的平坦极限，证明普通RT公式在该极限下无法良定义并将其与态-算符映射的微妙之处相关联；随后构造摆动方案的AdS₃提升，并通过两条互补路径给出信息论解释：一是证明摆动曲面长度计算一类纯CFT态中近乎零（null）边界区间的互信息，其引力解释涉及AdS₃体深处的散射过程；二是将摆动曲面的信息论解释约化为黑洞内视界的信息论解释这一更一般的问题。

Result: 证明了普通RT公式不存在良定义的平直空间极限；给出了摆动曲面的AdS₃提升构造；证明摆动曲面长度给出特定纯CFT态中近零边界区间的互信息，且引力图像涉及体深处散射；并将摆动曲面信息论解释与黑洞内视界的信息论解释联系起来。

Conclusion: 摆动曲面可以通过提升到AdS₃并在引力侧（体深处散射）与CFT侧（近零边界区间的互信息）获得信息论解释，从而将平直空间全息中的摆动曲面问题纳入更广泛的全息纠缠熵与黑洞内视界信息论框架。

Abstract: We study holographic entanglement entropy in the flat limit of AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$, and clarify the relation between Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces and the swing surfaces proposed for flat-space holography. We show that the ordinary RT formula does not admit a well-defined flat-space limit and connect this to subtleties of the state-operator map in the flat limit. We then construct an AdS$_3$ uplift of the swing prescription and develop its corresponding information-theoretic interpretation through two complementary approaches. First, we show that the swing surface length computes the mutual information between suitably chosen nearly null boundary intervals in a special class of pure CFT states. The gravity interpretation of this result involves scattering processes which take place deep inside of the AdS$_3$ bulk. Second, we reduce the problem of finding an information-theoretic interpretation for swing surfaces to the more general problem of finding an information-theoretic interpretation of black hole inner horizons in holography.

</details>


### [120] [Finite cyclic monodromy in BMS3 gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16823)
*Evangelos Melas*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维渐近平坦BMS引力在含绕数的双曲Virasoro轨道上的诱导数据，并探讨形式上光滑等变截面在全局Lorentz轨道上的限制。作者利用关联Hill算子的developing映射重新推导出其稳定子，证明在旋量双覆盖中稳定子是一条线与偶数阶循环群的直积，因而循环因子的特征构成独立于连续参数的离散诱导标签。进一步导出类时投影动量下Poincaré螺旋度阶梯、构造局部平坦绕数BMS几何、证明光滑实周期近视界流模型排除这些绕数轨道，并得到有限单值解析轨道泛函的选择规则与闭式表达式，但未构造全无限维轨道上的酉实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清三维渐近平坦BMS引力中双曲Virasoro轨道（含绕数、无常数超动量）的诱导表示结构，特别是稳定子的离散循环因子带来的独立诱导标签，它不同于大质量BMS粒子中由自旋固定的情形，从而为量子引力中循环扇区的解释提供数学基础。

Method: 利用关联Hill算子的developing映射重新推导轨道稳定子；在旋量双覆盖下分解稳定子为线与偶数阶循环群的直积；将形式上光滑等变截面限制到全局Lorentz轨道；在有限旋转对称点得到有限循环等变条件，并以独立定义的酉全局分支实现相应有限差分方程；在类时投影动量情形构造局部平坦绕数BMS几何并分析光滑实周期近视界流模型及轨道泛函。

Result: 稳定子（在旋量双覆盖中）为一条线与偶数阶循环群的直积，循环因子特征为独立离散诱导标签；有限旋转对称点处截面限制给出有限循环等变条件，酉全局分支给出有限差分方程；导出类时投影动量下的Poincaré螺旋度阶梯；证明投影静止能量非轨道不变量；构造出具有双曲Lorentz全纯的局部平坦绕数BMS几何；证明光滑实周期近视界流模型排除绕数轨道；得到有限单值解析轨道泛函的选择规则与闭式表达式。

Conclusion: 绕数双曲Virasoro轨道上的诱导结构清晰显示离散循环标签独立于连续标签，与大质量BMS粒子情形不同；但全无限维轨道上的酉实现尚未构造，循环扇区在量子引力中的解释仍属推测。

Abstract: We investigate inducing data for three dimensional asymptotically flat BMS gravity on hyperbolic Virasoro orbits with winding, which contain no constant supermomentum, and the restriction of formal smooth equivariant sections to global Lorentz orbits. Using the developing map of the associated Hill operator we rederive their stabilizer and show that, in the spin double cover, it is the direct product of a line with a cyclic group of even order. The character of the cyclic factor is therefore a discrete inducing label independent of the continuous one, unlike for the massive BMS particles, where it is fixed by the spin. At points with the corresponding finite rotational symmetry, restriction of formal smooth equivariant sections to a global Lorentz orbit yields the finite cyclic equivariance condition, while an independently defined unitary global branch realizes the associated finite difference equation. At points with timelike projected momentum we derive the resulting ladder of Poincare helicities, show that the projected rest energy is not an orbit invariant, construct locally flat winding BMS geometries with hyperbolic Lorentz holonomy, prove that the smooth real periodic near horizon current model excludes these winding orbits, and obtain selection rules and closed form expressions for finite monodromy resolved orbital functionals. A unitary realization on the full infinite dimensional orbit is not constructed, and the interpretation of the cyclic sectors in quantum gravity remains conjectural.

</details>


### [121] [$α'^2$-corrections to the bosonic string from an action-level double copy map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15862)
*Eric Lescano,Agustina Pereyra Grau,Jesús A. Rodríguez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过构造一个双重复制（double copy）框架，在作用量层面描述了玻色弦低能有效作用中高阶导数修正（Riem² 与 Riem³）及相应 Kalb–Ramond 耦合，并证明其唯一解为左右对称的 (YM+F³)⊗(YM+F³) 形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色弦的低能有效作用包含 Riem² 和 Riem³ 形式的高阶导数修正以及与之关联的 Kalb–Ramond 耦合，而此前缺乏对这些修正的作用量级别的双重复制描述。作者希望确立：何种高阶导数规范理论拉氏量以及何种色–运动学（color-kinematics）替换规则的变形，能够正确复现这些弦论修正。

Method: 作者在两个层面构造双重复制。其一是引入一个高阶导数规范理论拉氏量，在场重定义意义下由单个 F³ 耦合完全控制；其二是对色–运动学替换规则进行变形，即将结构常数替换为相应的三动量张量。作者证明，在标准替换规则下没有任何高阶导数规范理论算符能产生 Riem³ 顶点，并求解规范理论耦合与映射变形的匹配条件，得到唯一的二重根解，使三次作用量为左右对称的 (YM+F³)⊗(YM+F³) 形式。最终在横-无迹规范下、场重定义范围内与弦论结果进行比较。

Result: 构造出的映射无需额外输入即精确复现了玻色弦在 α′ 阶的三次 Riem H² 耦合、证明了 α′² 阶不存在六导数三次 Kalb–Ramond 耦合，以及不存在对 Kalb–Ramond 场为奇次的相互作用。匹配条件唯一确定为二重根，使作用量呈左右对称结构。

Conclusion: 玻色弦低能有效作用中的高阶导数修正可通过一个受单一 F³ 耦合控制的高阶导数规范理论拉氏量，结合结构常数到三动量张量的映射变形，在作用量层面实现双重复制构造。该构造的唯一解为 (YM+F³)⊗(YM+F³)，并能自动复现直至三次阶的弦论修正结构。

Abstract: The low-energy effective action of the bosonic string contains higher-derivative corrections of the form Riem$^2$ and Riem$^3$ to the Einstein--Hilbert action, together with the associated Kalb--Ramond couplings. We construct an action-level double copy description of these corrections up to cubic order in the fields. Two ingredients are required: a higher-derivative gauge-theory Lagrangian, which up to field redefinitions is controlled by a single $F^3$ coupling, and a deformation of the color-to-kinematics replacement of the structure constants by the corresponding three-momentum tensor. We show that no higher-derivative gauge-theory operator can produce the Riem$^3$ vertex with the standard replacement rule, and that the matching conditions for the gauge-theory coupling and the deformation of the map admit a unique solution, a double root, which renders the cubic action left--right symmetric, of the form $(\mathrm{YM}+F^3)\otimes(\mathrm{YM}+F^3)$. Without further input, the resulting map reproduces the cubic $\mathrm{Riem}\,H^2$ coupling of the bosonic string at order $α'$, the absence of six-derivative cubic Kalb--Ramond couplings at order $α'^2$, and the absence of interactions odd in the Kalb--Ramond field. The comparison is performed in transverse-traceless gauge, up to field redefinitions.

</details>


### [122] [Infinite spin superparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17006)
*Eugenia Boffo,Martin Cederwall*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从高阶导数超粒子作用量出发，在3、4、6、10维超闵可夫斯基背景上得到超庞加莱代数的无穷自旋表示，并通过超扭子变换推导出一次量子化谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构造无穷自旋（infinite spin/连续自旋）表示，这类表示在传统量子场论中较难处理，作者希望借助高阶导数超粒子模型和超扭子方法来系统获得超对称版本的无穷自旋表示。

Method: 在3、4、6、10维超闵可夫斯基背景上构造高阶导数超粒子作用量，从中得到超庞加莱代数的无穷自旋表示；随后执行超扭子变换(super-twistor transform)，并利用该变换推导一次量子化(first-quantised)能谱。

Result: 成功在3、4、6、10维上从高阶导数超粒子作用量获得了超庞加莱代数的无穷自旋表示；超扭子变换使得可以进一步推导出对应的一次量子化谱。

Conclusion: 高阶导数超粒子作用量结合超扭子变换为构造超庞加莱代数的无穷自旋表示及其一次量子化谱提供了有效途径，且该方法在3、4、6、10维均成立。

Abstract: We obtain infinite spin representations of the super-Poincaré algebra from a higher-derivative superparticle action on a super-Minkowski background in 3, 4, 6 and 10 dimensions. Super-twistor transforms are performed, and used to deduce first-quantised spectra.

</details>


### [123] [Cosmological correlators and kinematic flow on complex Hartle-Hawking saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15868)
*Vikramaditya Mondal,Shinji Mukohyama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究正宇宙学常数闭宇宙中洛伦兹引力路径积分由复鞍点（如 Hartle-Hawking 鞍点）主导时，如何在不显式追踪体时间演化的前提下计算宇宙学关联函数，并考察其在运动学变量平移（运动学流）下的响应。针对具有三次相互作用的共形耦合标量检验场，作者将波函数系数写成离散谱和，其被加项含爱因斯坦静态宇宙中的对应系数，并可类比平直时空的 polytopes 方法得到。进一步把这些系数（依赖末态超曲面、由大小固定的相位）等同于 Srivastava-Daoust 多变量超几何函数，它们满足递推关系使求和坍缩为有理函数。对一、二点 Feynman-Witten 树图作者导出运动学变量上的差分方程，其封闭于有限个和，给出离散运动学流；变换矩阵为上三角且元素有理，并满足使流与路径无关的一致性条件。最后得到二、三、四点宇宙学关联函数，并在晚时共形边界上导出四点函数的显式差分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正宇宙学常数闭宇宙中复鞍点（Hartle-Hawking 鞍点）主导下的洛伦兹引力路径积分，探索如何在不显式追踪体时间演化的前提下计算宇宙学关联函数，并理解其在运动学变量平移下的"运动学流"行为。以往平直 FLRW 情形波函数系数由平移运动学上的积分给出，而在三球面上 Hartle-Hawking 背景具有离散谱，需要将积分推广为离散和，从而建立离散运动学流的框架。

Method: 以共形耦合标量检验场带三次相互作用为具体模型，将波函数系数写成离散谱和，其被加项借用爱因斯坦静态宇宙中的对应系数（类比平直时空的 polytopes 程序）。将依赖末态超曲面的系数（通过其大小固定的相位）等同于 Srivastava-Daoust 多变量超几何函数，利用其对任意数目站点成立的递推关系使求和坍缩为有理函数。对一、二点 Feynman-Witten 树图，在运动学变量上建立封闭于有限个和的差分方程，构造离散运动学流；变换矩阵为上三角且元素有理，并验证满足路径无关的一致性条件。最后据此得到二、三、四点关联函数，并在晚时共形边界导出四点函数的差分方程。

Result: 得出波函数系数可表为离散谱和形式，区别于平直 FLRW 情形对平移运动学的积分。识别出这些系数为 Srivastava-Daoust 多变量超几何函数，满足任意站点数的递推关系并坍缩为有理函数。对一、二点树图导出离散运动学流，其变换矩阵上三角、元素有理且满足路径无关的一致性条件。最终给出二、三、四点宇宙学关联函数，并在晚时共形边界上得到四点函数的显式差分方程。

Conclusion: 在 Hartle-Hawking 复鞍点背景下，宇宙学关联函数可由离散谱和描述，波函数系数对应 Srivastava-Daoust 超几何函数并通过递推关系有理化；运动学变量的平移由离散运动学流刻画，其变换矩阵上三角且满足路径无关性。该框架无需显式追踪体时间演化即可计算二、三、四点关联函数，并在晚时共形边界给出四点函数的差分方程，为闭宇宙复鞍点上的宇宙学关联函数计算提供了系统性方法。

Abstract: The Lorentzian gravitational path integral over homogeneous and isotropic metrics in a closed universe with positive cosmological constant is dominated by complex saddle points, such as the Hartle-Hawking saddles. We ask how to compute cosmological correlators on such complex backgrounds, and how they respond to the shifts of the kinematic variables---the kinematic flow---without tracking the bulk time evolution explicitly. We, specifically, study a conformally coupled scalar test field with a cubic interaction around these saddles. We find that the wave function coefficients can be written as discrete spectral sums with the summands containing the corresponding coefficients in the Einstein static universe, which are obtainable in analogy with the polytopes program for flat spacetime. Whereas the wave function coefficients of the flat FLRW case are given by integrals over shifted kinematics, the discrete spectrum on the three-sphere for Hartle-Hawking saddles produces sums. We identify the coefficients, which depend on the final hypersurface through a phase fixed by its size, with Srivastava-Daoust multi-variable hypergeometric functions. They satisfy recursion relations for any number of sites, under which the sums collapse into rational functions. For the one- and two-site Feynman-Witten tree diagrams, we derive difference equations in the kinematic variables which close on a finite set of sums, giving a discrete kinematic flow. The transformation matrices are upper triangular with rational elements, and satisfy a consistency condition which makes the flow path independent. We then obtain the two-, three- and four-point cosmological correlators. At the late-time conformal boundary, we derive an explicit difference equation for the four-point function.

</details>


### [124] [Twisting IKKT with branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17270)
*Fabian Hahner,Natalie M. Paquette*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在BV形式下对IKKT矩阵模型进行超对称扭转分类，发现有限N下仅有两种扭转，而在无穷秩且引入非对易D膜背景时新增五类，共实现IIB超引力的全部扭转超对称等价类；并讨论了与BCOV理论的开闭弦对偶关系及Spin(7)扭转下配分函数的局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受IKKT矩阵模型与IIB弦理论猜想性关系的驱动，旨在系统分类矩阵模型的超对称扭转，厘清其与IIB超引力扭转分类的对应与差异，并为后续研究非对易背景上的世界体理论及局域化性质奠定基础。

Method: 采用BV形式处理IKKT矩阵模型，允许动力学与运动学超荷；对有限N与无穷秩情形分别分类，利用与非对易D膜背景相关的IIB超代数高阶中心扩张研究轨道分层差异；借助经典弦场论的开闭弦对偶将所得扭转联系到BCOV理论的形变，并用BV形式重构Spin(7)扭转下配分函数的局域化。

Result: 有限N下仅存在两种扭转（作者先前工作已研究）；无穷秩下额外发现五类扭转，需非对易IIB超引力D膜背景；七类扭转实现IIB超引力平坦空间中每一类扭转超对称等价类的代表，但轨道分层形式不同；该差异源于IIB超代数在BV处理中出现的高阶中心扩张。

Conclusion: 完整分类了IKKT矩阵模型的超对称扭转并揭示其与IIB超引力的深层联系：非对易背景上的展开给出A膜与B膜乘积上的世界体理论，包括涌现D3膜上的四维BF理论；所有扭转均可归结为BCOV理论的形变；Spin(7)扭转可通过无穷小反场修正的BV形式直接重建Moore-Nekrasov-Shatashvili局域化。

Abstract: Motivated by the conjectural relationship between the IKKT matrix model and type IIB string theory, we classify supersymmetric twists of the matrix model, allowing for both dynamical and kinematical supercharges. Beyond the two twists available at any finite $N$, which we studied in our previous work, we find five additional classes at infinite rank which require non-commuting backgrounds associated with D-branes in IIB supergravity. Together, these twists realize representatives of every equivalence class of twisting supercharges in flat-space type IIB supergravity recently classified by the first author; however, the precise form of the orbit stratification differs. This difference is related to a higher central extension of the IIB superalgebra that appears in our treatment of the IKKT model in the BV formalism. Expanding around the non-commutative backgrounds gives worldvolume theories on products of A- and B-branes, including four-dimensional BF theory on an emergent D3-brane. We relate all these twists to deformations of BCOV theory (the closed string field theory of the topological B-model) using the open-closed duality of classical string field theory. For one of the twists with Spin$(7)$ symmetry, we also show the localization of the IKKT partition function by Moore, Nekrasov, and Shatashvili can be directly reconstructed with the BV formalism, with their auxiliary field completion encoded by quadratic antifield terms. Throughout, we provide self-contained treatments of the formal techniques we employ.

</details>


### [125] [Cosmological Dressing Rules for Massive Scalars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15870)
*Jonathan Gräfe,Matthias Nowinski,Ivo Sachs,Siva Pawan Venna*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出 de Sitter 关联函数的 Schwinger-Keldysh 表述（包括调和分析与 dressed flat space 关联函数两种形式）本质上是同一 Sturm-Liouville 问题的等价谱表示，并由此推导出任意质量标量场的 dressing 规则，其辅助传播子表现为可用平坦空间图论工具处理的 Euler 积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: de Sitter 关联函数的 Schwinger-Keldysh 表述存在多种形式（如调和分析与 dressed flat space 关联函数），它们之间的关系尚不清晰。作者希望揭示这些形式背后统一的数学结构，并提供一种便于实际计算的工具。

Method: 从 Sturm-Liouville 问题出发，将调和分析与 dressed flat space 关联函数两种形式统一为等价的谱表示；基于该谱表示推导任意质量标量场的 dressing 规则，把辅助传播子写成 Euler 积分的形式，使其可直接用平坦空间费曼图方法处理。

Result: 证明了 de Sitter 关联函数的不同 Schwinger-Keldysh 表述是同一 Sturm-Liouville 问题的等价谱表示；得到了适用于任意质量标量场的 dressing 规则，其辅助传播子为 Euler 积分形式，可接入平坦空间图论计算工具。

Conclusion: 通过谱表示的统一视角，de Sitter 空间中的标量场关联函数可以被系统地转换为平坦空间中的图论问题，为宇宙学关联函数的计算提供了一种更实用且统一的框架。

Abstract: The Schwinger-Keldysh formulation of de Sitter correlation functions comes in different guises including harmonic analysis and dressed flat space correlators. We observe that these are equivalent spectral representations of the same Sturm-Liouville problem. Starting from the latter we derive dressing rules for scalar fields of arbitrary mass with auxiliary propagators in the form of Euler integrals that are accessible to flat space diagrammatic tools.

</details>


### [126] [Subsystem Flow: Quantum Error Correction, Petz Recovery, and Ergodic Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15887)
*Yidong Chen,Nima Lashkari,Kwing Lam Leung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究量子纠错与局部多体系统中子代数包含结构之间的关系，通过定义"子系统流"来度量系统偏离纠错(模自治)状态的程度，并引入正包含父哈密顿量、Petz恢复映射与耗散半群等工具，刻画模轨道生成的代数结构，并猜想可用父哈密顿量的谱定义量子混沌的局域谱测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错的目标是通过编码保护逻辑信息免受物理子系统擦除；而在局部多体系统中，自然给出了子代数包含 N⊂M 及参考态 |Ω⟩。作者希望回答：该包含是否承认一个保持参考态的码，使得 N 在擦除补子空间时受到保护，这等价于 N 在参考态的模动力学下是否保持不变(模自治)。由此出发，需要系统刻画偏离自治状态时的动力学与代数结构。

Method: 作者定义"子系统流"来度量偏离模自治的程度：偏离自治时，无穷小流量化相对熵损失；历史代数与持久核描述模轨道生成与保留的可观测量。通过子系统流的解析延拓得到 Petz 恢复映射和一簇正包含父哈密顿量。进一步，利用 L²-核性质证明父哈密顿量谱的离散性，并通过重复 Petz 恢复映射定义耗散半群，其不动点代数为模持久核，且在 L²-核假设下谱具有能隙。

Result: 一般而言，用这些父哈密顿量演化可使 N 离开 M，某些情形下甚至能生成整个 M∨M'。具有公共循环且分离的向量的包含生成一切；因子的标准包含(如 QFT 中嵌套子区域)是遍历的。L²-核性质蕴含父哈密顿量谱离散，从而耗散半群谱在 L²-核假设下具有能隙。

Conclusion: 作者建立了子系统包含、模动力学与量子纠错之间的统一框架。包含的遍历性(模轨道生成一切且不保留任何量)刻画了非纠错极限；父哈密顿量与 Petz 恢复映射提供了可计算的谱结构。作者进一步猜想，包含父哈密顿量的本征值与本征向量可用于定义量子混沌的局域谱测度。

Abstract: In quantum error correction (QEC), the goal is to engineer an encoding that protects logical information against the erasure of a physical subsystem. In local many-body quantum systems, by contrast, nature provides a subsystem inclusion $\mN\subset \mM$ in a fixed reference state $\ketΩ$. Asking whether the inclusion admits a reference-preserving code that protects subsystem $\mN$ against erasure of the complement is equivalent to asking whether $\mN$ is preserved under the modular dynamics of $\mM$ in this state (modular autonomy). We define the subsystem flow, which measures the departure from this error correction regime. Away from autonomy, the infinitesimal flow quantifies relative-entropy loss, while the history algebra and persistent core describe which observables the modular orbit generates and retains. We call an inclusion ergodic when modular orbits generate everything and retain nothing. Analytic continuation of subsystem flow yields Petz recovery maps and a family of positive inclusion parent Hamiltonians. We show that, in general, evolving with these parent Hamiltonians allows $\mN$ to leave $\mM$, and in some cases, it can generate the entire $\mM\vee\mM'$. Inclusions with a common cyclic and separating vector generate everything, and standard inclusions of factors, such as those of nested subregions in QFT, are ergodic. Moreover, the $L^2$-nuclearity property implies that the spectrum of parent Hamiltonians is discrete. We define a dissipative semigroup by repeating the Petz recovery map, whose fixed-point algebra is the modular persistent core and whose spectrum is gapped under the assumption of $L^2$-nuclearity. We conjecture that the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of inclusion parent Hamiltonians can be used to define local spectral measures of quantum chaos.

</details>


### [127] [Exact Infrared Triangle in Gravity with Long-Range Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15921)
*Sangmin Choi,Ameya Kadhe,Prahar Mitra,Andrea Puhm*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明，在存在长程引力作用时，无质量物质耦合的爱因斯坦引力中，新的超转动荷守恒等价于经典对数软引力子定理的无质量极限，并给出位移记忆尾部公式，同时计算出对已知超转动荷的高阶修正（对引力耦合精确），对应Cachazo-Strominger次领头软引力子定理的1圈精确修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作研究了引力耦合到有质量物质的红外普适性，但对于长程相互作用存在时的无质量物质情形尚未有定性不同的理解；作者希望将超转动荷守恒、软引力子定理与位移记忆之间的联系推广到无质量极限，并揭示长程引力"穿着"（dressing）的重求和性质。

Method: 在爱因斯坦引力与无质量物质耦合的框架下，引入长程相互作用，构造新的超转动荷并证明其守恒性；将该守恒律与经典对数软引力子定理的无质量极限建立等价关系；推导位移记忆尾部（tail）的公式，并计算对已知超转动荷的高阶修正。所有结果对引力耦合常数精确，并体现长程引力穿着可重求和。

Result: 证明了新超转动荷守恒等价于经典对数软引力子定理的无质量极限；提出了位移记忆尾部（tail）的公式；发现长程引力穿着可重求和；得到了对已知超转动荷的高阶修正，该修正对引力耦合精确，对应Cachazo-Strominger次领头软引力子定理的1圈精确修正。

Conclusion: 这些结果以定性不同的方式扩展了早期关于引力耦合有质量物质的工作，且对引力耦合常数精确，揭示了长程引力穿着可重求和，为无质量物质情形下的红外普适性、软定理与记忆效应之间的三角关系提供了新见解。

Abstract: Einstein gravity coupled to massless matter exhibits universality in the infrared. In the presence of long-range interactions we show that a novel superrotation charge conservation is equivalent to the massless limit of the classical logarithmic soft graviton theorem, and we propose a formula for the corresponding tail to the displacement memory. These results, which extend earlier work on gravity coupled to massive matter in a qualitatively different way, are exact in the gravitational coupling and reveal that long-range gravitational dressings resum. We also compute higher-order corrections to the known superrotation charge which are exact in the gravitational coupling and would correspond to a 1-loop exact correction to the Cachazo-Strominger subleading soft graviton theorem.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场如何改变夸克-胶子物质中的耗散几乎无法由第一性原理约束，因为实时间输运无法直接由格点模拟获得。本工作旨在利用顶下的 MQGP 启发的 M/弦理论对 QCD 类热等离子体的构造，给出对纵向体粘滞与剪切粘滞比随磁场斜率符号的预测，弥补该领域第一性原理约束的缺失。

Result: 预测纵向体粘滞与剪切粘滞比 ζ∥/η∥ 对磁场的斜率在温度跨越 T0（退禁闭赝临界温度）前后两次变号，具体位于 T1<T0<T2；较低的变号温度 T1 依赖于π介子气体速率不等式条件。动力学理论恒等式再现了同一模式，晶格声速数据亦与此一致；VQCD 在退禁闭相对高温支持负斜率，但其斜率交叉在现有 ζ∥/s 与 η∥/s 数据图中无法分辨。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种归一化不敏感的符号预测——纵向体粘滞与剪切粘滞比随磁场的斜率在温度上两次反转，分别位于退禁闭赝临界温度之下和之上；该预测由多种独立途径（弦理论构造、动力学理论、晶格数据、VQCD）相互印证，为磁场下夸克-胶子物质输运性质提供了新的可检验的理论指引。

Abstract: How a magnetic field alters dissipation in quark--gluon matter is almost unconstrained from first principles, since real-time transport is not directly accessible to lattice simulation. Using a top-down MQGP-inspired M/string-theory construction of a QCD-like thermal plasma, together with a kinetic-theory identity within a specified single-mode quasiparticle truncation and a compatibility construction using available lattice equation-of-state data, we propose a normalization-insensitive sign prediction that the field slope of the longitudinal bulk-to-shear viscosity ratio ζ_\parallel/η_\parallel reverses sign twice with temperature---below and above the deconfinement pseudo-critical temperature T_0, at T_1<T_0<T_2 (the lower reversal T_1 conditional on a stated pion-gas rate inequality). A kinetic-theory identity with phase-appropriate carriers, a quark--gluon plasma above T_0 and a chiral pion gas below, reproduces the same pattern, and lattice speed-of-sound data can be shown to be consistent as well. The bottom-up VQCD in the deconfined phase supports a negative slope for large temperatures; the slope crossover is not resolvable from the available ζ_\parallel/s, η_\parallel/s plots.

### [129] [Imaging the two-sided black hole interior with four-point correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15933)
