TL;DR: 本文提出一种用波包涂抹的边界四点函数来探测双侧 AdS 黑洞视界后局部物理的方法：高频波包沿测地线传播并在体内点局部散射，使关联函数因子化为类平空间散射振幅，且能对黑洞内部图像（如零激波与物质壳层）成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统边界关联函数难以直接探测 AdS 黑洞视界后的局部物理。作者希望构造一个可观测量，使得边界算符能够瞄准并探测视界内的具体体内点，从而成像黑洞内部结构。

Method: 提出用波包涂抹的边界四点函数，并采用非平凡算符时序；其中一个态依赖算符插入被移动到热圆的一半位置。高频下，瞄准体内点的边界波包沿测地线传播并在该点局部散射，使关联函数因子化为类平空间的散射振幅。

Result: 该可观测量可成像黑洞内部：当体内点穿过零激波或物质壳层时，观测信号出现跳变；同时验证了高频极限下关联函数退化为类平空间散射振幅。

Conclusion: 通过波包涂抹的四点函数和特殊算符时序，边界可观测量能够局部探测并成像 AdS 黑洞视界后的内部结构与非平凡态特征。

Abstract: We propose a boundary four-point function smeared with wavepackets, which probes local physics behind the horizon of a two-sided AdS black hole. At high frequencies, boundary wavepackets aimed at an interior bulk point travel along geodesics and scatter locally about the bulk point, factorizing the correlator into a flat-space like scattering amplitude. To obtain this configuration, we utilize a non-trivial time ordering of operators, and also use one state-dependent operator insertion moved halfway across the thermal circle. An interesting property of this observable is that it also probes features of nontrivial states: null shocks and matter shells behind the horizon show up as jumps in the observed signal as the bulk point crosses them, thereby imaging the black hole interior.

</details>


### [130] [Self-Force and Bremsstrahlung in Finite Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15993)
*Kyungsun Lee,Piljin Yi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究非线性电动力学（以 Born–Infeld 理论为原型）中运动点电荷的自力与伴随的轫致辐射，其中自场与自能均为有限值。作者分别独立计算局部自力与全局辐射耗散，证明二者自洽；非线性虽在渐近辐射场中消失，但其残余效应通过运动电荷的有效多极矩存续，使结果偏离麦克斯韦理论，并在附录讨论了 Born–Infeld ModMax 推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麦克斯韦理论中运动点电荷的自力已被包括多位著名物理学家在内的众多研究探讨过。在回顾 Coleman 对麦克斯韦情形的清晰论述之后，作者希望把同一问题推广到以 Born–Infeld 理论为原型的非线性电动力学中——该理论因自场与自能有限，避免了点电荷自能的发散困难，从而为自力问题提供更自洽的理论框架。

Method: 作者以 Born–Infeld 非线性电动力学为原型：回顾 Coleman 对麦克斯韦自力的论述作为基准；分别独立地计算运动的点电荷所受的局部自力（local self-force）与全局辐射耗散（global radiative dissipation）；并考察渐近辐射场中非线性效应关闭后，其残余效应如何通过运动电荷的有效多极矩（effective multipole moments）体现；最后在附录中将分析推广到 Born–Infeld ModMax 理论。

Result: 局部自力与全局辐射耗散两种独立计算结果相互一致（mutually consistent）；尽管非线性在渐近辐射场区域关闭，其残余效应仍通过运动电荷的有效多极矩存续，导致结果偏离麦克斯韦理论；文中还详细讨论了文献中自力两种不同表现形式如何进入该计算。

Conclusion: 在 Born–Infeld 等具有有限自场与自能的非线性电动力学中，运动点电荷的自力与轫致辐射可被一致地推导，且非线性通过有效多极矩留下可观测的残余效应，使结果区别于麦克斯韦理论；附录进一步表明该框架可推广至 Born–Infeld ModMax。

Abstract: We study the self-force and the accompanying Bremsstrahlung for a moving point charge in nonlinear electrodynamics with finite self-field and self-energy. The self-force in the Maxwell theory has been the subject of many studies, often by renowned physicists. After reviewing Coleman's lucid treatise for Maxwell among them, we wish to explore the same question in the Born--Infeld theory, our prototype. We independently compute the local self-force and the global radiative dissipation, and show how the two are mutually consistent. Despite the nonlinearity turning off for the asymptotic radiative field, its residual effect survives via the effective multipole moments of a moving charge, allowing deviation from Maxwell. We close with a detailed discussion about two different manifestations of the self-force in the literature and how these enter our computation. One of the appendices explores Born--Infeld ModMax as a generalization.

</details>


### [131] [Silent Heavy Fields: An Exclusion Rule for the Cosmological Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16029)
*Shuntaro Aoki,Zhehan Qin,Masahide Yamaguchi,Yuhang Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了宇宙对撞机信号消失的排除规则，涵盖两类由de Sitter不变重区通过两点函数或单圈多边形与单一外场耦合的图，并用含化学势的玩具模型给出三角形圈图的非消失领头信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙对撞机利用原初关联函数中的振荡信号探测暴涨期间的重场，但重场可能在时间上持续演化而在关联函数中保持“沉默”，形成探测盲区。需要系统性地界定哪些情形下对撞机信号必然消失，以避免对已有近似结果的误读。

Method: 作者推导了一个排除规则：对由两点函数传递的重区交换图，先用谱分解再通过场重定义证明信号消失；随后利用振荡贡献的匹配，将该结论推广到单圈多边形图上；最后在一个由化学势启发的玩具模型中计算三角形圈图的领头信号作为非消失例证。

Result: 证明了当de Sitter不变的重区在某一个顶点与单一外场协变耦合时，两类图的宇宙对撞机信号为零；此前落入这些类别的过程的近似信号必须重新审视；并首次给出三角形圈图在化学势玩具模型中的领头非消失信号。

Conclusion: 宇宙对撞机存在由排除规则刻画的盲区，凡符合该规则的过程其振荡信号消失，相关旧结果需修正；三角形圈图等反例说明盲区之外仍可获得新信号，为后续探测重场提供更准确的判别依据。

Abstract: The cosmological collider uses oscillatory signatures in primordial correlators to probe heavy fields during inflation. This probe has blind spots, as heavy fields can keep ticking in time while remaining silent in the correlators. In this letter, we derive an exclusion rule under which cosmological collider signals vanish. The rule covers two classes of diagrams where a de Sitter invariant heavy sector couples covariantly to a single external field at one vertex. We first prove it for exchanges mediated by the two-point function of the heavy sector to all loop orders, using a spectral decomposition followed by a field redefinition. The result then extends to one-loop polygons by matching their oscillatory contributions onto those of the first class. The approximate signals of previously studied processes that fall into these classes must therefore be revisited. As a nonvanishing example, we derive the leading signal of a triangle loop for the first time in a toy model motivated by chemical potentials.

</details>


### [132] [Anti-periodic Gravitino and Dirac Effective Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16047)
*Karim Benakli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究在保留 $U(1)_R$ 对称性（禁止 Majorana 引力微子质量但允许 Dirac 质量）的框架下，如何用 $N=1$ 超空间中的有效算符描述引力微子的 Dirac 质量，并通过五维最小超引力在 $S^1/\mathbb Z_2$ 紧化下的具体计算验证这一构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准超引力中引入 $U(1)_R$ 对称性会禁止引力微子的 Majorana 质量项，但 Dirac 质量仍然被允许。作者希望类比已知的 Dirac gaugino 质量机制，在 $N=1$ 超空间中为自旋 $3/2$ 引力微子构造相应的 Dirac 质量有效算符，从而为理解这类无 Majorana 质量的超对称破缺场景提供理论工具。

Method: 作者利用规范不变的引力微子曲率超场（gravitino curvature superfields），通过一个含逆 d'Alembertian 的非局域核耦合，构造出自旋 $3/2$ 双线性算符。随后在 $S^1/\mathbb Z_2$ 紧化的五维最小超引力中检验该构造：引力微子由 Scherk-Schwarz 扭转诱导反周期边界条件，物理 Kaluza-Klein Dirac 质量表现为端点局域源对应的曲率超场两点函数的极点。作者将物理模与规范模分离，把传播子与一个 $16\times16$ 矩阵表示的二次费米子核相关联，并将其矩阵元与 $N=1$ 超空间中两个不变量（含旋量收缩与归一化）的费米子分量进行匹配，且该匹配在具有非零 radion 辅助场的固定背景上完成。

Result: 物理 Kaluza-Klein Dirac 质量确实表现为曲率超场两点函数的极点；$16\times16$ 二次费米子核的矩阵元成功匹配了两个 $N=1$ 超空间不变量的费米子分量（包括旋量收缩和归一化），验证了非局域超空间算符描述的 Dirac 引力微子质量方案在五维紧化背景下的正确性。

Conclusion: 用含逆 d'Alembertian 的非局域核耦合规范不变引力微子曲率超场，能够在 $N=1$ 超空间中有效描述保留 $U(1)_R$ 对称性时的 Dirac 引力微子质量，该构造在五维 $S^1/\mathbb Z_2$ 紧化的最小超引力中通过明确的算符匹配得到验证，为后续研究无 Majorana 质量的超对称模型提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Introducing a preserved $U(1)_R$ symmetry forbids gravitini Majorana masses while allowing a Dirac mass. We investigate how a Dirac mass for gravitini can be described by an effective operator in $N=1$ superspace, in analogy with the well-known Dirac gaugino mass. We find that the corresponding spin-$3/2$ bilinear operator can be expressed in terms of gauge-invariant gravitino curvature superfields coupled through a nonlocal kernel involving an inverse d'Alembertian. We study this construction in minimal five-dimensional supergravity compactified on $S^1/\mathbb Z_2$. The gravitino satisfies anti-periodic boundary conditions induced by a Scherk-Schwarz twist. The physical Kaluza-Klein Dirac masses then appear as poles in the two-point functions of the curvature superfields associated with sources localized at the endpoints. Separation of physical and gauge modes relates these propagators to a quadratic fermionic kernel represented by a $16\times16$ matrix. Its elements match the fermionic components of two invariants written in $N=1$ superspace, including their spinor contractions and normalization. This matching is carried out on a fixed background with a nonzero radion auxiliary field.

</details>


### [133] [Holographic Counterterms from Restricted Weyl Symmetry in Even-Dimensional Einstein-AdS Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16187)
*Giorgos Anastasiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种基于无穷小体Weyl变换的对称性重整化方法，用于偶数维Einstein-AdS引力；通过要求Einstein-Hilbert密度在受限Weyl变换下变化为零（模有限项），确定体协变完备形式，给出任意偶数维的闭式解，并验证其与全息重整化结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶数维Einstein-AdS引力的重整化通常依赖具体维度的构造，缺乏系统且可推广到任意偶数维的统一方法。作者希望借助保持渐近场方程不变的体Weyl对称性，从对称性原理直接确定重整化项的完备形式，从而给出一个维度无关、闭式且与共形不变量环境构造相一致的重整化方案。

Method: 作者考虑保形场方程渐近成立的无穷小体Weyl变换（参数为Σ(X)），要求Einstein-Hilbert密度在受限Weyl变换下的变分在去掉径向截断后积分有限，从而导出两类固定耦合的补偿项：Euler密度和一族协变全导数。所得递推关系可用移位Laplacian因式分解求解，得到任意偶数维的闭式完备形式；在Einstein背景上与ambient构造的共形不变量匹配，并建立重整化体积恒等式以保证壳上有限性。

Result: 该方案在四、六、八维复现已知的共形重整化作用量，并给出十维涉及至□³的显式完备形式。应用于具有非共形平直Einstein视界的八维和十维AdS黑洞时，得到与全息重整化一致的有限壳上作用量。

Conclusion: 基于体Weyl对称性的重整化方法为任意偶数维Einstein-AdS引力提供了统一、闭式且壳上有限的完备化方案，几何上自然且与ambient共形不变量和全息重整化相吻合，为高维重整化提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We develop a symmetry-based approach to renormalization for even-dimensional Einstein-AdS gravity based on infinitesimal bulk Weyl transformations that preserve its field equations asymptotically. We determine a bulk-covariant completion of the Einstein-Hilbert density by requiring its variation under restricted Weyl transformations with parameter $Σ(X)$ to vanish modulo terms whose integrals remain finite as the radial cutoff is removed. This requirement induces two types of compensating terms with fixed couplings: the Euler density and a hierarchy of covariant total derivatives. The resulting recursive relations admit a factorized solution in terms of shifted Laplacians, yielding a closed-form completion in arbitrary even bulk dimensions. On Einstein backgrounds, these expressions agree, after matching normalizations, with those obtained through the ambient construction of conformal invariants. The associated renormalized-volume identity establishes on-shell finiteness for Euclidean AlAdS Einstein geometries in arbitrary even dimensions. The prescription reproduces the known conformally renormalized actions in four, six and eight dimensions and provides an explicit ten-dimensional completion involving powers of the Laplacian up to $\Box^3$. Applications to eight- and ten-dimensional AdS black holes with non-conformally-flat Einstein horizons yield finite on-shell actions in agreement with Holographic Renormalization.

</details>


### [134] [Discrete anomaly inflow from SymTFT of $U(1)$ global symmetry: the modified Villain resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16696)
*Napat Poovuttikul,K. Sapmee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用修正 Villain 形式构造了带异常系数 k 的 U(1) 全局对称性的格点 SymTFT，其中奇数 k 所需的自旋结构由格点 Majorana 费米子实现。作者证明这些 Majorana 费米子恰位于对称性线交汇处，即两个 U(1) 元素融合绕圆周之处。由此推导出体与带隙边界上的 R- 与 F-符号，并证明奇数 k 时边界 F-符号无需通过玻色影子即满足 Gu-Wen 关系。限制到 Z₂ 子群可重现 mod-4 反常相位与费米子叠加规则，并确定哪些 Z_d 子群可被规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的：从连续对称性的反常流入直接推导出超出群上同调范围的全局反常的junction数据（R/F-符号等），而无需依赖玻色影子（bosonic shadow）等间接手段。为此需要显式构造一个能承载奇数 k 所需自旋结构的格点 SymTFT。

Method: 方法：采用修正 Villain（modified Villain）表述构造 U(1) 全局对称性（反常系数 k）的格点 SymTFT；用格点 Majorana 费米子显式实现奇数 k 所需的自旋结构。证明 Majorana 费米子位于对称性线（symmetry lines）的交汇处（即两个 U(1) 元素融合绕圆周的点）。从线（lines）与交汇（junctions）的格点代数出发，推导体（bulk）和带隙边界（gapped boundary）上的 R- 与 F- 融合符号。进一步将对称限制到 Z₂ 子群并计算映射环面（mapping torus）相位，以及检验费米子叠加规则。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 构造出显式格点 SymTFT，奇数 k 的自旋结构由 Majorana 费米子实现；(2) Majorana 费米子恰位于对称线交汇处；(3) 从格点代数推导出体与边界上的 R- 与 F- 符号；(4) 奇数 k 时边界 F- 符号直接满足 Gu-Wen 关系，无需玻色影子；(5) 对 Z₂ ⊂ U(1) 重现 mod-4 反常相位与费米子叠加规则；(6) 确定可被规范的子群 Z_d ⊂ U(1) 及规范后理论的对称性。

Conclusion: 作者成功构造了带自旋结构的 U(1) 格点 SymTFT，并通过Majorana交汇费米子直接得到满足 Gu-Wen 关系的 F-符号，避免了玻色影子。该工作被视为从连续对称性反常流入推导超出群上同调的全局反常 junction 数据的第一步。

Abstract: We construct a lattice SymTFT for a $U(1)$ global symmetry with anomaly coefficient $k$ using the modified Villain formulation, in which the spin structure required for odd $k$ is realised explicitly by lattice Majorana fermions. We demonstrate that the same Majorana fermions sit at the junctions of the symmetry lines, exactly where the fusion of two $U(1)$ elements wraps around the circle. From the lattice algebra of lines and junctions we derive the $R$- and $F$-symbols in the bulk and on the gapped boundary, and show that for odd $k$ the boundary $F$-symbol obeys the Gu-Wen relation for the $U(1)$ symmetry defect, without passing through a bosonic shadow. Restricting to $\mathbb{Z}_2\subset U(1)$, we reproduce the mod-4 anomalous phase from a mapping torus and the fermionic stacking rule. As a simple application of this SymTFT, we also determine which subgroups $\mathbb{Z}_d\subset U(1)$ can be gauged and the symmetry of the resulting theories. This is a first step towards deriving, from the anomaly inflow of a continuous symmetry, the junction data of global anomalies beyond group cohomology.

</details>


### [135] [Multiparticle residues of open-string tachyon amplitudes from DDF sewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16280)
*Dripto Biswas,Qu Cao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过缝接 DDF 顶点并对所有物理中间态求和，将临界开玻色弦彩色排序快子盘振幅的最大同时留数化为高斯收缩，并给出与 Grunsky 算子及高阶 Koba–Nielsen 顶形式相联系的形式体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于直接对弦振幅的极点留数进行严格求和，避免依赖完整世界面积分或传统级数展开，从而在保持局部坐标与缝接规范一致的前提下，系统化地重建并推广 Veneziano 与更高点 Koba–Nielsen 振幅。

Method: 作者缝接三次 DDF 顶点，使对所有物理中间态的求和化为任意平面三次树上的高斯收缩；每个内部能级确定一个精确的有限模截断；留数由缝接变量中行列式前因子乘以源二次型指数经系数抽取得到。四点情形借助三次局部映射与单玻色子能动张量 Ward 恒等式做全阶重求和，恢复 Veneziano 极点系数；并对五点双留数和六点梳形、雪花形给出显式矩阵。

Result: 得到的行列式即横玻色子的真空缝接因子；四点重求和重现 Veneziano 极点系数，五点与六点构造在低阶与独立的 Koba–Nielsen 展开核对一致；规范选取使得顶点与缝接度量间的局部坐标标度相消。

Conclusion: 该构造将快子盘振幅的最大同时留数完全确定于行列式与二次型指数结构，并建立了算子、坐标映射与测度恒等式，以连接 Grunsky 算子与全高阶 Koba–Nielsen 顶形式。

Abstract: We compute the maximal simultaneous residues of color-ordered tachyon disk amplitudes in the critical open bosonic string by summing all physical intermediate states. Sewing cubic Del Giudice--Di Vecchia--Fubini (DDF) vertices reduces this sum to Gaussian contractions for any planar cubic tree, with the internal levels specified independently. Each internal level fixes an exact finite mode cutoff. The residue then follows by coefficient extraction in the sewing variables from a determinant prefactor times the exponential of a quadratic form in the sources. With consistent sewing conventions, the local-coordinate scales cancel between the vertices and the sewing metric. At four points, cubic local maps and a one-boson stress-tensor Ward identity give an all-order resummation that recovers the Veneziano pole coefficients. The same construction gives explicit matrices for the five-point double residue and the six-point comb and snowflake, with low-level checks against independent Koba--Nielsen expansions. The determinant is the vacuum sewing factor of the transverse bosons. We formulate the operator, coordinate-map and measure identities needed to relate this construction to Grunsky operators and the full higher-point Koba--Nielsen top forms.

</details>


### [136] [De Sitter Gravity and Emergent Time from Fusion Matrix Product States and SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17045)
*Shinsei Ryu,Yu-ki Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个与二维德西特（dS₂）引力对偶的量子力学模型——一个由矩阵乘积态定义的量子信道，其最简单部分是一个具有涌现 SL(2,ℝ) 对称性的随机游走，该对称性对应 dS 未来边界的对称性，并给出 dS JT 引力的盘振幅，从而为二维 dS/CFT 对应提供具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为二维德西特引力构建一个具体的量子力学对偶模型，以落实 dS/CFT 对应（尤其是此前相对欠缺的 dS 一方的具体实现），并检验其与 dS₂ 中自由无质量标量场 Bunch-Davies 真空的两点函数及边界对称性是否一致。

Method: 作者构造一个量子信道，它由“每步融合一个自旋”的矩阵乘积态（MPS）定义，并与无弦交叉的 Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型的一个极限相关。其最简单的组成部分是一个具有涌现 SL(2,ℝ) 对称性的随机游走；该对称性被认定为 dS₂ 未来边界的对称性。该构造选定了一个时间变量，使随机游走服从 Schwarzian 作用量，从而得到 dS 的 Jackiw-Teitelboim（JT）引力的盘振幅。作为检验，作者在 dS₂ 固定背景下讨论了自由无质量标量场。

Result: 该模型选出的时间变量使随机游走服从 Schwarzian 作用量，并成功给出 dS JT 引力的盘振幅；其涌现的 SL(2,ℝ) 对称性与 dS₂ 未来边界对称性一致。在 dS₂ 固定背景下，自由无质量标量场 Bunch-Davies 真空的两点函数与模型结果一致，对称性也相互匹配。

Conclusion: 作者为二维德西特引力提供了一个具体的 dS/CFT 对应模型：通过量子信道（MPS）及其随机游走核心，涌现出边界 SL(2,ℝ) 对称性与 Schwarzian 动力学，复现了 dS JT 引力盘振幅，并与 dS₂ 中自由标量场 Bunch-Davies 真空的两点函数相吻合，从而支撑了该对偶构造的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a quantum-mechanical model dual to two-dimensional de Sitter (dS) gravity. It is a quantum channel, defined by a matrix product state that fuses one spin at each step, and is related to a limit of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model without chord crossings. Its simplest part is a random walk with an emergent $SL(2,\mathbb R)$ symmetry, which we identify with the symmetry of the future boundary of dS. It selects a time variable in which the walk obeys the Schwarzian action and yields the disk amplitude of dS Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity. As the simplest case, we consider free massless scalar fields in the fixed background of dS$_2$. We find the agreement of the two-point function in the Bunch-Davies vacuum and the matching of symmetry. Our construction provides a concrete model for the dS/CFT correspondence for two-dimensional de Sitter gravity.

</details>


### [137] [Thermodynamics within and across AdS Gauss--Bonnet holographic CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16364)
*Limin Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究五维AdS Gauss–Bonnet引力中单一全息CFT内部及不同反常系数CFT之间的热竞争，推导出与无量纲耦合共轭的功项，并利用共同边界时钟在固定外源下比较带电球鞍点，揭示耦合的跳跃切换及广义Clapeyron关系等临界现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息CFT在不同反常系数间的热力学竞争与相变行为，特别是在五维AdS Gauss–Bonnet引力框架下，如何比较具有不同耦合常数（反常系数）的CFT的热力学稳定性及相结构。

Method: 从边界第一定律推导与无量纲耦合共轭的功项；采用共同边界时钟（单位流逝柱面时钟与零型D真空能）在固定外源下比较带电球鞍点；利用固定半径下的严格凹性将全局比较约化为耦合区间两端点；通过改变外源驱动两端点CFT之间的一阶切换，并借助广义Clapeyron关系联系共存线斜率与熵差。

Result: 首选耦合在共存线上发生跳变；当Einstein端点本身处于小-大共存时，第三个鞍点加入竞争并在跨理论边界上产生拐点；外部控制量的补偿变化在有限能量移动下保持鞍点序不变；在单一CFT内确定了中性Hawking–Page线及带电小-大黑洞共存面；临界电荷在有限耦合处趋于零，熵隙以1/2指数闭合，热容在临界电荷温度扫描中以2/3指数发散。

Conclusion: 在五维AdS Gauss–Bonnet引力中，通过共同边界时钟和固定外源的比较框架，可系统刻画CFT间及CFT内部的热力学竞争与临界行为，明确耦合切换的相变结构及其与标度指数的关系。

Abstract: We study thermal competition within individual holographic conformal field theories (CFTs) and across theories with different anomaly coefficients in five-dimensional AdS Gauss--Bonnet gravity. We derive the work term conjugate to the dimensionless coupling from the boundary first law and use a common boundary clock to compare charged spherical saddles at fixed external source. Varying this source drives a first-order switch between the CFTs at the endpoints of the chosen coupling interval. In the unit-lapse cylinder clock with the zero-type-D vacuum energy, strict concavity at fixed radius reduces the global comparison to these two endpoints. The preferred coupling jumps across the coexistence line, whose slope is related to the entropy difference by a generalized Clapeyron relation. Where the Einstein endpoint itself undergoes small--large coexistence, a third saddle joins the competition and produces a kink in the cross-theory boundary. A compensating change of the external control preserves saddle ordering under finite energy shifts. Within fixed CFTs, we determine the neutral Hawking--Page line and the charged small--large black hole coexistence surface. The critical charge tends to zero at a finite coupling. The entropy gap closes with exponent one half, and the heat capacity diverges with exponent two thirds along a temperature sweep at the critical charge.

</details>


### [138] [Matching lattice and continuum chiral determinants at finite volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16544)
*Zhengwen Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于平坦四维环面上的一个 SU(5) 代，Craig 的规范不变 overlap 测度与 Hsu-Witten 的重构 Weyl 行列式这两种格点手征规范理论权重，在连续极限下沿光滑带隙路径一致，只需固定归一化并扣除一个局域 Yang-Mills 抵消项；并给出数值与解析验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点手征规范理论面临费米子权重如何良定义的核心困难，主要有两条进路：Craig 的规范不变 overlap 测度，以及 Hsu-Witten 的在重构联络上取连续 Weyl 行列式（相位由五维 η-不变量固定）。需要澄清二者在连续极限下是否等价，以及等价所需的条件（归一化、抵消项、带隙假设）。

Method: 作者在平坦四环面上取一个 SU(5) 代，沿光滑带隙路径比较两种权重；固定归一化并扣除一个系数被确定的局域 Yang-Mills 抵消项。对有界光滑族，仅需一致 Dirac 带隙（不要求真空展开收敛），给出对数变分的显式一致收敛速率。对 Craig 插值证明重构界，把 Hsu 的小场比较推广到有限振幅；对 Hsu 带平坦颈圈的反射正性重构，相位变分仍匹配，但二次抵消项的对数系数被一个可计算的、依赖剖面的因子增强。在真空展开发散的 hypercharge 背景下，对输入规范场做数值比较，并解析导出模量的对数修正系数。

Result: 两种权重在连续极限下沿光滑带隙路径一致（固定归一化并扣除局域 Yang-Mills 抵消项后）。一致 Dirac 带隙即可保证匹配，无需真空展开收敛；得到对数变分的显式一致收敛速率。Craig 插值的重构界推广到有限振幅。Hsu 反射正性重构下相位变分仍匹配，但二次项对数系数被剖面依赖因子增强。数值显示 hypercharge 背景下相位二次收敛，模量带对数修正，其系数由自由 overlap 真空极化解析给出。

Conclusion: Craig 测度与 Hsu-Witten 权重在连续极限下经适当归一化和局域抵消后等价，所需的 Dirac 带隙条件弱于真空展开收敛；并可定量刻画对数修正系数，说明两种格点手征规范理论构造的一致性及其修正结构。

Abstract: Recent formulations of lattice chiral gauge theories define a complex fermion weight either through Craig's gauge-invariant overlap measure or, following Hsu and Witten, through a continuum Weyl determinant on a reconstructed gauge connection, with its phase fixed by the five-dimensional $η$-invariant. For one $SU(5)$ generation on a flat four-torus, we prove that their relative weights agree in the continuum limit along smooth gapped paths, after fixing a normalization and subtracting a local Yang-Mills counterterm, whose coefficient we determine. On bounded smooth families a uniform Dirac gap suffices: the vacuum expansion need not converge. We obtain explicit uniform convergence rates for the logarithmic variations and, for Craig's interpolation, prove the reconstruction bounds that the matching requires, extending Hsu's small-field comparison to finite amplitude. For Hsu's reflection-positive reconstruction with flat collars, the phase variations still match, while the logarithmic coefficient of the quadratic counterterm is enhanced by a calculable, profile-dependent factor. In a hypercharge background on which the vacuum expansion diverges, numerical comparisons at the input gauge field support quadratic convergence of the phase, whereas the modulus acquires a logarithmic correction to quadratic scaling, whose coefficient follows analytically from the free overlap vacuum polarization.

</details>


### [139] [Accelerating solitons in Rindler space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16568)
*Huiquan Li,Jianyong Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维闵可夫斯基时空中可积的相对论非线性场论在 Rindler（加速参考系）空间中的性质，发现其仍保持可积性，并具体分析了 sine-Gordon 孤子在 Rindler 空间中的动力学与能量行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究加速观测者视角下可积非线性场论的行为，理解闵可夫斯基时空中完整孤子被 Rindler 视界截断后所呈现的新物理现象。

Method: 将二维闵可夫斯基时空中可积的相对论非线性场论（以 sine-Gordon 模型为例）变换到 Rindler 空间，解析研究经典 SG 孤子解的性质，并分析其能量流入流出视界的行为。

Result: 1) Rindler 空间中理论仍可积；2) 每个 Rindler 楔形中的加速观测者只能看见米氏时空中完整孤子的一部分，该部分自身构成携带部分拓扑荷的孤子；3) 孤子解可解释为从视界入射后减速、停在某点再回落、向视界加速至光速；4) 能量可流入和流出 Rindler 视界，高能孤子从视界获得能量，低能孤子则损失能量。

Conclusion: 可积相对论非线性场论在 Rindler 空间保持可积；经典 sine-Gordon 孤子在该空间呈现部分拓扑荷、视界附近的往返运动，以及与 Rindler 视界之间的能量交换行为。

Abstract: Relativistic nonlinear field theories that are integrable in two-dimensional Minkowski spacetime remain integrable in Rindler space. We investigate the properties of classical sine-Gordon (SG) solitons in Rindler space. Accelerating observers in each of the two Rindler wedges can see only a portion of the full soliton in Minkowski spacetime; this portion itself forms a soliton carrying a partial topological charge. The soliton solution admits the following interpretation: a soliton incoming from the horizon decelerates to stop at some point, then falls back and accelerates to the speed of light toward the horizon. Energy can flow into and out of the Rindler horizon. The higher energy soliton gains energy from the horizon while the lower one loses energy.

</details>


### [140] [Is Spin Slow in Hot QCD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16684)
*Zi-Hao Liu,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 通过完整的领先阶碰撞算符，本文证明热QCD等离子体中轻夸克自旋的阻尼速率高达输运速率量级 g^4T ln(1/g)，而非此前预期的缓慢弛豫，因此自旋无法作为独立流体力学变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前观点认为，若要将自旋提升为流体力学变量，需依赖质量压低的自旋翻转过程，从而预期自旋弛豫很慢。本文旨在检验这一假设是否在QCD等离子体中成立。

Method: 采用完整的领先阶碰撞算符进行分析，并利用空间自旋锁定于动量的特性，考虑螺旋度守恒的软、小角度散射对自旋的旋转效应。

Result: 轻夸克自旋以输运速率 g^4T ln(1/g) 阻尼，几乎与矢量流一样快；阻尼涉及多个动力学模式而非单一弛豫时间；整体旋转仅贡献可逆的进动；更强的耦合进一步不利于慢自旋模式的存在。

Conclusion: 由于自旋阻尼速率接近输运速率，且强耦合下更不利于慢自旋模式，热QCD等离子体中轻夸克自旋不应被视为缓慢弛豫的流体力学变量。

Abstract: Promoting spin to a hydrodynamic variable requires slow relaxation, as mass-suppressed helicity flips in hot QCD suggest. Using the full leading-order collision operator, we show instead that light-quark spin damps at the transport rate $g^4T\ln(1/g)$, nearly as fast as vector currents. Since spatial spin locks to momentum, helicity-conserving soft, small-angle scatterings readily rotate it. Damping involves multiple kinetic modes beyond a single relaxation time, global rotation adds only reversible precession, and stronger coupling further disfavors a slow spin mode in quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [141] [Gauge Theory Bootstrap with varying $N_c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16824)
*Rafael Cordoba,Yifei He,Martin Kruczenski,Derek Ping,Jignesh Mohanty,Emil Alegria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用规范理论 Bootstrap 方法研究随颜色数 N_c 变化的渐近自由规范理论，在 N_c=3 至 12 范围内提取散射长度、有效力程、低能常数及 σ、ρ、f_2 共振，考察其 N_c 依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过微调 N_c 这一微观参数，直接检验规范理论 Bootstrap 方法描述渐近自由规范理论低能强耦合动力学的能力，并理解共振态性质对颜色数的依赖。

Method: 采用规范理论 Bootstrap 方法；N_c 通过求和规则和渐近形状因子进入计算，从而可调节微观参数并研究相应低能动力学；对 3≤N_c≤12 提取散射长度、有效力程和低能常数 l̄_i，并获得 σ、ρ、f_2 共振质量与宽度。

Result: ρ 和 f_2 质量对 N_c 依赖很弱，宽度随 N_c 增大而减小，符合常规 q̄q 介子的 1/N_c 标度；σ 介子的质量和宽度均随 N_c 增大而增加，与先前的幺正化 χPT 分析一致。

Conclusion: 这些结果为规范理论 Bootstrap 方法研究渐近自由规范理论提供了进一步检验，验证了其对不同颜色数下低能动力学和共振性质描述的有效性。

Abstract: We use the Gauge Theory Bootstrap to study asymptotically free gauge theories with a varying number of colors $N_c$. The parameter $N_c$ enters the computation through the sum rules and asymptotic form factors, allowing us to tune this microscopic parameter directly and examine the corresponding low-energy strongly coupled dynamics. For $3\le N_c\le 12$, we extract the scattering lengths, effective ranges, and low-energy constants $\bar{l}_i$, and study their $N_c$ dependence. We also obtain the $σ$, $ρ$, and $f_2$ resonances for the same range of $N_c$. The $ρ$ and $f_2$ masses depend only weakly on $N_c$, and their widths decrease as $N_c$ increases, consistent with the $1/N_c$ scaling expected for conventional $q\bar{q}$ mesons. In contrast, the $σ$ meson mass and width both increase with $N_c$, in agreement with a previous unitarized $χ$PT analysis. These results provide a further test of the Gauge Theory Bootstrap for studying asymptotically free gauge theories.

</details>


### [142] [Holographic viscous transport in scalar-coupled Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16936)
*Tao Ma,Song He,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在五维 Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet 全息模型中，直接比较了由边界推迟应力张量关联函数（Kubo 公式）提取的剪切与体粘滞系数和由黑洞视界处守恒流/熵产生公式得到的视界公式，检验了标量依赖耦合下 Eling-Oz 公式的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前视界公式（如基于视界守恒流或熵产生的方法）大多只在常数 Gauss-Bonnet 耦合或特定近似下得到验证，缺乏对标量依赖耦合情形下直接边界计算的基准对比，尤其需要检验 Eling-Oz 公式的 1/(4π) 前置因子在 η/s 偏离该值时是否仍成立。

Method: 作者在五维 Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet 模型中，将模型校准到零重子密度下的格点 QCD 状态方程；通过加入曲率与标量抵消项得到紫外有限的推迟应力张量关联函数，并在不利用视界守恒律或熵产生公式的前提下、完整保留 Gauss-Bonnet 耦合 α 依赖地求解涨落方程，用 Kubo 公式提取剪切与体粘滞系数；再与视界守恒流计算（常数耦合及一阶 α 截断）以及 Eling-Oz 公式进行比较。

Result: 常数耦合下，视界守恒流计算与边界 Kubo 结果在剪切和体两个通道完全一致。标量依赖耦合下，两种方法都发现 η/s 存在极小值；体通道中，一阶 α 截断的守恒流计算得到的 ζ/s 峰值高度较低而峰值温度几乎不变。对两种耦合，未修正的 Eling-Oz 公式 ζ/s=(4π)^(-1)(∂φ_h/∂ln s)^2（φ_h 为视界处标量值）均精确再现边界的 ζ/s 结果，即使 Gauss-Bonnet 相互作用使 η/s 偏离 1/(4π)，该前置因子依然保持不变。

Conclusion: 这些结果为视界处方提供了直接的边界基准，支持了在超越常数耦合情形下未修正的 Eling-Oz 公式依然有效，并说明 Gauss-Bonnet 项对 η/s 的修正并不改变 ζ/s 公式中的 1/(4π) 前置因子。

Abstract: We test horizon formulas for the shear and bulk viscosities of a holographic plasma against viscosities extracted directly from retarded stress-tensor correlators. We work in a five-dimensional Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet model calibrated to the lattice QCD equation of state at zero baryon density. UV-finite renormalized correlators are obtained by including the required curvature and scalar counterterms, and the fluctuation equations are solved while retaining the full dependence on the Gauss-Bonnet coupling $α$, without using a horizon conservation law or an entropy-production formula. For constant coupling, the horizon conserved-current calculation reproduces the boundary Kubo results in both channels. For the scalar-dependent coupling studied here, both methods find a minimum in the shear-viscosity-to-entropy-density ratio $η/s$. In the bulk channel, the conserved-current calculation truncated at first order in $α$ yields a lower peak height in $ζ/s$, with the peak temperature nearly unchanged relative to the boundary result. For both couplings, the unmodified Eling-Oz (EO) formula, $ζ/s=(4π)^{-1}(\partialφ_h/\partial\ln s)^2$, reproduces the boundary result for $ζ/s$, where $φ_h$ is the horizon value of the scalar. The prefactor $1/(4π)$ is thus retained even when the Gauss-Bonnet interaction shifts $η/s$ from this value. These results provide a direct boundary benchmark for horizon prescriptions and support the unmodified EO formula beyond constant coupling.

</details>

Conclusion: 协变 Wigner–Weyl 微积分及其动量空间拓扑不变量为理解非均匀费米子体系的量子反常与拓扑输运提供了统一且可严格化的框架，新结果消除了若干技术假设并建立了反常、费米面不变量与输运效应之间的精确对应。

Abstract: We review, systematize and extend the covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus for fermions in non-Abelian gauge fields and its applications to systems inhomogeneous in coordinate space with nontrivial momentum-space topology: the non-Abelian chiral separation and quantum Hall effects, their stability under interactions, the Fermi-surface invariant of inhomogeneous systems and the non-elliptic models that carry it, with their equilibrium chiral magnetic effect, the factorization of the chiral anomaly into the instanton number and the momentum-space invariant $N_3$, and the noncommutative-geometric form of the invariants. Previous results are brought to common conventions, with hypotheses stated and notions defined. New results: the characterization of the operators whose covariant symbol is gauge-field independent and the ordering independence of the integrated anomaly; the anomaly factorization on a closed four-manifold without the homotopy hypothesis, the factorization into instanton number and $N_3$ holding exactly when the untwisted index vanishes; the Luttinger--Ward identity behind the renormalized-velocity current and the continuum conditions replacing the compactness of the Brillouin zone in the non-renormalization theorems; the identification of the Moyal $N_3$ of the chiral separation effect with the fibre degree of the principal symbol; the residue evaluation of the Fermi-surface invariant of the non-elliptic models, in the continuum and on a lattice, and the equilibrium current of the conserved charge along the magnetic field that it implies -- a chiral magnetic effect permitted by the non-Hamiltonian form of these models -- which the model with spherical Fermi surfaces trades for a chiral separation effect and a single-Weyl anomaly, and a modification of it restores at the price of the anomaly; and the index-pairing form of the chiral separation invariant.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞的潮汐响应（Love数）是理解引力波观测和高维引力理论的关键量，但现有研究主要局限于静态、球对称黑洞。作者旨在将标量场Love数的分析推广到任意维度、带有旋转的Myers-Perry黑洞，厘清潮汐响应中对几何细节敏感与不敏感的部分。

Method: 作者将标量场的潮汐响应分解为两个常数：一个编码潜在的logarithmic running（对数跑动）的参数，一个为Love数。利用波动方程的渐近展开，在任意维度下显式计算Myers-Perry黑洞的跑动参数；对于依赖视界边界条件的Love数，则采用若干不同的近似方法（multiple distinct approximations）进行求解。

Result: 证明了描述对数跑动的参数对视界结构不敏感，并给出了任意维度Myers-Perry黑洞下该参数的显式结果；Love数则依赖于视界处的边界条件细节，通过多种近似方案得到了相应数值或解析估计。

Conclusion: 标量场在Myers-Perry黑洞中的潮汐响应可清晰地分为“普适”的对数跑动部分与“依赖视界细节”的Love数部分。该分解为高维旋转黑洞的潮汐物理提供了统一框架，并指出了精确确定Love数所需的边界条件敏感性。

Abstract: We analyze Love numbers for scalar fields in the Myers-Perry geometry in arbitrary dimensions. The tidal responses of black holes are specified by two constants which encode a potential logarithmic running and the Love number. We argue that the parameter specifying the running is not sensitive to the structure of the horizon, and we explicitly compute this parameter for Myers-Perry black holes in arbitrary dimensions by utilizing the asymptotic expansion of the wave equation. The Love numbers rely on the details of the boundary conditions at the horizon, and we compute them using several distinct approximations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在理解大 N 转级数在有限 N 下确定全息可观测量的极限：e^{-N} 瞬子（通常的引力瞬子）之后的 e^{-N^2} 渐近（来自 D 膜瞬子，即黑洞阈值以上的新型引力鞍点）能否被纳入重整化群框架，以及这些 D 膜瞬子数据是否对转级数的重求和构成解析性阻碍，从而限制其精度。

Result: 发现在黑洞相中，D 膜瞬子和的增长导致大 N 转级数重求和存在解析性阻碍，这一阻碍设定了全息可观测量的精度上界；在 1/16-BPS 指标中，推导出的热 AdS 与黑洞之间交换 D 膜瞬子数目的对偶关系成功重现了包裹黑洞的 D3 膜作用量，从而构成对该框架的一致性与自洽性检验。

Conclusion: 大 N 转级数对有限 N 全息可观测量的确定精度受 D 膜瞬子数据的本质限制；该框架有望推广到一般情形，作者并指出其与双锥虫洞（double-cone wormhole）之间的联系。

Abstract: We propose that the large $N$ transseries of a holographic observable above the black hole threshold admits a precision bound, a limit on how precisely the transseries can determine the observable at finite $N$, set by the data of its D-brane instanton sectors. In doing so, we develop methods in large $N$ resurgence, based on the work of Écalle, that capture the $e^{-N^2}$ asymptotics of gravitational saddles beyond the $e^{-N}$ instantons. Under an assumption on the growth of the D-brane instanton sums that we expect in the black hole phase, we find an analytic obstruction to the resummation of the large $N$ transseries. This obstruction sets the precision bound. As our main example and consistency check, we study the $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS index of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills and its black hole. We derive relations between the D-brane instanton data on thermal AdS and on the black hole, in the form of a duality that exchanges the numbers of D-brane instantons on the two backgrounds. In the index, these relations reproduce the actions of D3-branes wrapped on the black hole. We conclude with our expectations for generic situations and their relation to the double-cone wormhole.

</details>

Result: 该弦的所有态——一个携带十六个超荷的无穷塔——均是无质量的。中性玻色子部分的作用量可对偶为一个三次自对偶作用量；带电态的耦合可解释为相关李超代数的结构常数。由此提出经典目标理论是该超代数的自对偶规范理论，其中弦的每一个态都是一个规范场。

Conclusion: 作者提出该理论的经典目标空间是一个自对偶规范理论，其规范代数由左移子物理态构成的李超代数给出；弦的每个态都是规范场，引力也包含在该规范代数之中。这将是闭弦所有模式的一个纯三次场论。

Abstract: We study an $N=(2,1)$ heterotic string with right-movers on $\mathbb R^{2,2}$ and left-movers of signature $(5,5)$, obtained by adjoining the lattice $\mathrm{II}_{3,3}$. Unlike the standard $N=(2,1)$ strings it requires no null current, and its target space remains four-dimensional. All of its states, an infinite tower with sixteen supercharges, are massless. For the neutral bosons, the Kutasov-Martinec determinant action is dual to a cubic action for a self-dual metric coupled to self-dual Maxwell fields, and we complete it with fermions using a single superfield. For the charged states we give evidence that the couplings are the structure constants of a Lie superalgebra formed by the physical states of the left-movers. We are thus led to propose that the classical target theory is a self-dual gauge theory for this superalgebra: every state of the string is a gauge field, and gravity is part of the gauge algebra. This would be a purely cubic field theory of all the modes of a closed string.

Abstract: We numerically solve for the ground state of the $SU(2)$ BFSS matrix model for M-theory. This state corresponds to the zero-energy bound state of two D0-branes or, by the BFSS conjecture, to the eleven-dimensional graviton with two units of momentum along the M-theory circle. The wavefunction exhibits a strongly coupled core, as well as an asymptotic power-law tail where the D0-branes are well separated. The coefficient of this power-law decay is the three-graviton coupling, previously computed by Herderschee and Maldacena, which our numerical solution reproduces with high accuracy. We also show that this coupling is protected by supersymmetry in the matrix model, and derive its value from the non-renormalized one-loop interaction of the two D0-branes. Lastly, we provide a simple representation-theoretic argument regarding the recently proposed excited bound states of the model.

</details>


### [148] [Decoupling Dynamics: A Tale of Two Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17327)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SymTFT 在有限对称性的规范下保持不变，但在连续对称性的动力学规范下会改变，缺乏对其变化的完整量子层面理解；作者希望将这种变化追溯到同一个高维动力学规范理论的不同拓扑极限，从而解释规范化如何改变边界条件并选择不同 SymTFT，并厘清其中的拓扑缺陷、荷与反常如何存留、电流携带的动力学信息如何被丢弃。

Method: 作者提出一个 (d+1) 维动力学全息规范理论，使其在不同拓扑极限下分别给出连续阿贝尔对称规范前后的两种 SymTFT。规范化改变该动力学理论的边界条件，从而选择相应拓扑极限。通过结合体全息理论的合适极限与"光子截断"（photon truncation），在希尔伯特空间与算符层面给出 SymTFT 的完全量子定义，并系统研究非反常与反常示例，对 (2+1) 维体理论给出完整量子分析，进而处理四维边界理论的混合轴矢-矢量反常情形。

Result: 作者证明规范前后的两个不同 SymTFT 是同一 (d+1) 维动力学全息规范理论的两个不同拓扑极限，规范化等价于改变该理论边界条件并选择另一极限。完整量子分析表明拓扑对称缺陷、荷和反常在该构造下存留，而电流所承载的动力学信息被光子截断丢弃。对 (2+1) 维体给出完整量子分析；对具有混合轴矢-矢量反常的四维边界理论，成功恢复矢量对称规范后所余不可逆手征对称的 SymTFT。

Conclusion: 连续对称动力学规范导致的 SymTFT 变化可统一理解为高维动力学全息规范理论的不同拓扑极限，由边界条件选择。该框架为 SymTFT 提供了严格的量子定义，并清晰区分了存留的拓扑信息与被丢弃的动力学信息，可推广到非反常与反常情形，包括不可逆对称性。

Abstract: The symmetries of a $d$-dimensional QFT are known to be captured by a topological theory in one dimension higher, the Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT), which is invariant under gauging finite symmetries of the QFT, but not under dynamical gauging of continuous symmetries. We show that the distinct SymTFTs of a $d$-dimensional QFT before and after dynamical gauging of a continuous Abelian symmetry arise as different topological limits of the same dynamical holographic gauge theory in $(d+1)$-dimensions. Gauging changes the boundary condition of this dynamical theory and thereby selects a different topological limit, explaining why the associated SymTFT changes. We give a fully quantum definition of these SymTFTs at the level of Hilbert spaces and operators by combining suitable limits of the bulk holographic theory with photon truncation. This construction clarifies how topological symmetry defects, charges, and anomalies survive while the dynamical information carried by currents is discarded. We study non-anomalous and anomalous examples, providing a complete quantum analysis for $(2+1)$-dimensional bulks. For a four-dimensional boundary theory with a mixed axial-vector anomaly, we recover the SymTFT of the non-invertible chiral symmetry that remains after gauging the vector symmetry.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Little-Parks Oscillations and Parity Effects in Mesoscopic Superconducting Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14597)
*Iogann Tolbatov,Luca Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在介观准一维环中，利用带扭曲边界条件的Ginzburg-Landau展开，从弱耦合BCS极限到中间耦合区间解析研究了Little-Parks效应，推导出跨耦合强度的T_c(Φ)闭合表达式，并将LP响应分解为离散拓扑宇称项与连续动能项两个物理来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Little-Parks效应中超导转变温度T_c随磁通Φ的周期振荡在从弱耦合到中间耦合范围内的物理机制，特别是区分拓扑宇称干涉与超流连续质心动能两种贡献，并理解耦合增强、相干长度收缩时宇称调制的指数抑制。

Method: 采用带扭曲边界条件的Ginzburg-Landau展开，同时纳入离散有限尺寸拓扑宇称干涉和由超流诱导的连续质心动能位移；在GL框架下推导跨不同相互作用强度的T_c(Φ)闭合表达式；并以相干长度比ξ/R和1/R²标度刻画两类贡献。

Result: 得到T_c(Φ)的闭合表达式，表明LP响应由两部分构成：由ξ/R支配的离散拓扑宇称效应和按1/R²标度的连续动能项。随耦合增强、空间相干长度收缩（ξ≪R），配对对全局边界约束几何失明，离散宇称调制指数抑制；但凝缩体仍保持依赖磁通的宏观质心动量，使T_c维持由超流动能主导的代数型LP调制。

Conclusion: Little-Parks效应在耦合增强过程中从离散拓扑宇称调制过渡到由宏观超流动能主导的代数调制；文章还讨论了在强耦合极限附近相位涨落占主导之前，平均场GL有效性的失效边界。

Abstract: We analytically investigate the Little-Parks (LP) effect, the periodic oscillation of the superconducting transition temperature $T_c$ driven by an applied magnetic flux $Φ$, from the weak-coupling Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) limit to the intermediate-coupling regime in a mesoscopic, quasi-one-dimensional ring. Utilizing a Ginzburg-Landau (GL) expansion with twisted boundary conditions, we correctly incorporate both the discrete finite-size topological parity interference and the continuous center-of-mass (CM) kinetic energy shift induced by the supercurrent. We derive a closed-form expression for $T_c(Φ)$ valid within the GL framework across variable interaction strengths. We demonstrate that the LP response comprises two distinct physical contributions: a discrete topological parity effect governed by the correlation length ratio $ξ/R$, and a continuous kinetic energy term scaling as $1/R^2$. As the coupling increases and the spatial correlation length of the pairs contracts ($ξ\ll R$), the constituent pairs become geometrically blind to the global boundary constraint, leading to an exponential suppression of the discrete parity modulation. However, the requirement that the condensate acquires a flux-dependent macroscopic CM momentum persists. This ensures that the critical temperature $T_c$ maintains an algebraic LP modulation driven predominantly by the macroscopic kinetic energy of the supercurrent. We bound our theoretical approach by discussing the breakdown of mean-field GL validity before phase fluctuations dominate near the strong-coupling limit.

</details>


### [150] [Symmetry-Enabled Rolling of Composite Bosons: A Route to Superconductivity from Strong Repulsion with $T_c$ Proportional to the Electron Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14615)
*Zhiyu Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究强库仑排斥体系中的超导机制，提出“滚动”（rolling）对称性原理，使三电子-一空穴（3e1h）复合玻色子在强束缚下仍能轻质量移动，从而获得仅受电子带宽限制的超导转变温度 Tc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强库仑排斥体系中已发现超导，作者探讨排斥能否产生 Tc 仅受电子带宽限制的超导。库仑排斥通过将电子束缚到空穴上可产生配对，但通常形成的复合粒子在晶格上只能通过高阶跃迁移动，中间态代价约为束缚能，导致强束缚下复合体变重、超流刚度与 Tc 受限，Tc 远低于带宽。作者希望打破“强束缚—重质量—低 Tc”这一权衡。

Method: 作者利用对称性绕开上述权衡：在满足特定对称性要求的晶格上，复合体可通过单粒子跃迁移动而不经历高能中间态，作者称之为“滚动”。推导出滚动的判据，并据此构造出全部十四种支持滚动的极简晶格模型；证明可滚动的 3e1h 复合体确实能在基态稳定存在；进一步提出在双栅极双层结构中实现该超导，并用精确数值方法验证。

Result: 在满足对称性条件的晶格中，滚动使复合体的质量即使束缚无限强也保持有限，从而可在受控区域实现与电子带宽成正比的 Tc；构造出十四种极简滚动晶格模型；数值计算表明在双栅极双层结构中 Tc 可达到电子跳跃能的百分之几。

Conclusion: 滚动性可作为在强排斥电子体系中寻找与设计超导的指导原则，所构造的极简模型可作为原型，为高温超导的探索提供了新思路。

Abstract: Motivated by superconductors discovered in systems with a strong Coulomb repulsion, we ask whether such a repulsion can give rise to superconductivity with a transition temperature $T_c$ bounded only by the electron bandwidth. Coulomb repulsion can produce pairing as it binds electrons to holes. If each hole binds three electrons into a three-electron-one-hole (3e1h) composite, the condensation of these bosonic composites can give rise to superconductivity. However, the resulting $T_c$ falls far below the electron bandwidth in the controlled regime where the binding is much stronger than the carriers' kinetic energy. This is because, on a lattice, a tightly bound composite usually has to move through a higher-order process where the intermediate state costs an energy of order the binding energy, so that the strong binding makes the composite heavy, limiting the superfluid stiffness and hence $T_c$. Here, we show that symmetry offers a way around this tradeoff. In lattices satisfying certain symmetry requirements, a composite can move by single-particle hops without any costly intermediate state, a motion we call rolling. Such rolling keeps the mass of the composite finite even for an infinitely strong binding, so that a $T_c$ proportional to the electron bandwidth can be achieved in the controlled regime. We derive a criterion for rolling and apply it to construct all fourteen minimal lattice models that support such rolling. We show that rollable 3e1h composites can indeed be stabilized in the ground state. We then propose to realize such superconductivity in a dual-gate bilayer, and use exact numerics to show that $T_c$ can reach a few percent of the electron hopping. These results establish rollability as a guiding principle in the search for and design of superconductors in strongly repelling electron systems, with the minimal models constructed here as prototypes.

</details>


### [151] [Nitride crystalline barrier epitaxial SIS Josephson junctions by MBE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14968)
*Manas Verma,Anand Ithepalli,Amit Rohan Rajapurohita,Eegene Clara Chung,David A. Garcia-Wetten,Naomi Pieczulewski,Danqing Wang,John G. Wright,Archit Shah,Jimy Encomendero,Rishabh Singh,Chandrashekar Savant,Hangwang Yang,Anthony Hyatt,Jaehong Choi,Thomas Varghese,Kushagra Aggarwal,Paul B. Welander,Apurva Mehta,David Schuster,Valla Fatemi,Gregory D. Fuchs,Michael C. Hamilton,Hong X. Tang,David A. Muller,Huili Grace Xing,Debdeep Jena*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了首次通过MBE（分子束外延）成功制备出具有晶体AlN隧穿势垒的NbN/AlN/NbN超导体-绝缘体-超导体（SIS）约瑟夫森结，实现了相干约瑟夫森隧穿，为超导量子电路与有源电子/光子器件的单片集成开辟了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有晶体隧穿势垒的SIS约瑟夫森结一直难以通过MOCVD或MBE等外延技术实现，而这些技术正是制造晶体管、激光器等当今电子和光子系统的核心手段。因此，突破关键材料瓶颈、用MBE实现晶体势垒约瑟夫森结，是推动超导器件与成熟半导体/光电子平台集成的前提。

Method: 采用分子束外延（MBE）技术外延生长NbN/AlN/NbN三层结构，其中AlN势垒厚度约为4.5 nm，形成晶体氮化物势垒的SIS约瑟夫森结。通过电流-电压特性、临界电流密度、IcRn乘积、超导能隙电压及其随温度演化，以及磁场下Fraunhofer电流调制等测量手段对器件进行表征。

Result: 尽管AlN势垒厚度约4.5 nm，NbN/AlN/NbN三层结构仍表现出相干约瑟夫森隧穿，具体参数为：临界电流密度约17 A/cm²，IcRn乘积约2.5 mV，超导能隙电压约3.7 mV，且能隙随温度的演化遵循BCS-like行为。磁滞的电流-电压特性和磁场下的Fraunhofer电流调制证实了欠阻尼（underdamped）SIS约瑟夫森行为。

Conclusion: 通过MBE成功实现晶体势垒约瑟夫森结，为采用合金和超晶格电极及隧穿势垒的丰富材料体系打开了大门，并有望实现超导量子电路与HEMT、激光器等有源电子和光子器件的单片集成。

Abstract: Superconductor-insulator-superconductor Josephson junctions with crystalline tunnel barriers have remained elusive by epitaxial techniques such as MOCVD or MBE that produce the junction materials and devices powering today's electronic and photonic systems like transistors and lasers. Here, we overcome the key materials hurdle to realize the first functional nitride crystalline barrier Josephson junctions by MBE. Despite an AlN barrier thickness of approximately 4.5 nm, the NbN/AlN/NbN trilayers exhibit coherent Josephson tunneling with critical current densities of approximately 17 A/cm2, an IcRn product of approximately 2.5 mV, and a superconducting gap voltage of approximately 3.7 mV with a BCS-like gap evolution with temperature. Hysteretic current-voltage characteristics and Fraunhofer current modulation with magnetic field establish underdamped SIS Josephson behavior. The realization of crystalline barrier Josephson junctions by MBE opens the doors to a richer materials palette with alloys and superlattice electrodes and tunnel barriers, as well as potential monolithic integration of superconducting quantum circuits with active electronic and photonic devices such as HEMTs and lasers.

</details>


### [152] [Interplay of Pair Density Wave and Uniform Superconductivity on the Kagome Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15354)
*Ruiqing Fu,Xianxin Wu,Ziqiang Wang,Sen Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在一个单轨道 t-W-J 模型下研究了 kagome 晶格上配对密度波（PDW）与均匀超导的相互作用，发现均匀超导分量会重塑 PDW 的幅值、相位结构与准粒子拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索 kagome 晶格中均匀超导与配对密度波（PDW）之间的竞争与共存机制。在 p 型 van Hove 填充附近，位点吸引（W）利于零动量 BCS 配对，而最近邻键吸引（J）因 kagome 几何与子格干涉自然诱导有限动量配对，从而提供了研究两者相互作用的理想平台。

Method: 采用单轨道 t-W-J 模型，在 2×2 超胞中进行自洽平均场计算，并结合 Ginzburg–Landau 理论分析对称性允许的耦合项，研究均匀超导分量如何重组三个 F1 PDW 分量的幅值与相位结构。

Result: 均匀超导分量会重组三个 F1 PDW 分量的幅值和相位结构，产生纯键配对极限下不存在的超导态；Ginzburg–Landau 分析确认了可促成该重组的对称性允许耦合。所得态表现出独特的电荷调制和准粒子谱，且手性 PDW 随位点吸引增强发生拓扑转变。

Conclusion: 均匀超导会重塑 PDW 态的序参量景观和准粒子拓扑，揭示了 kagome 体系中均匀配对与有限动量配对耦合的物理图像。

Abstract: We study the interplay between pair density wave (PDW) and uniform superconductivity on the kagome lattice within a single-orbital $t$-$W$-$J$ model near the $p$-type van Hove filling, where $W$ and $J$ denote effective pairing attractions on lattice sites and nearest-neighbor bonds, respectively. While onsite attraction favors zero-momentum pairing through the conventional BCS instability, nearest-neighbor attraction naturally promotes finite-momentum pairing due to the kagome geometry and sublattice interference. The model therefore provides a framework for exploring the competition and coexistence between uniform superconductivity and PDW order. Self-consistent mean-field calculations in a $2\times2$ supercell reveal that the uniform component reorganizes both the amplitudes and phase structure of the three $F_1$ PDW components, giving rise to superconducting states absent in the pure bond-pairing limit. A Ginzburg--Landau analysis identifies symmetry-allowed couplings that can favor this reorganization. The resulting states exhibit distinct charge modulations and quasiparticle spectra, while the chiral PDW undergoes topological transitions as the onsite attraction increases. Our results demonstrate how uniform superconductivity reshapes the order-parameter landscape and quasiparticle topology of a PDW state.

</details>


### [153] [Machine-accelerated extraction of experimental superconducting parameters for physical insight and prediction across thousands of materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15627)
*Mihir Manium,Benjamin Geisler,Peter J. Hirschfeld,Amalia I. Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: AutoSuperCon 是一个包含8个超导参数、覆盖6793种超导体（20354条记录）的大型数据库，数据由大语言模型从超过47000篇arXiv预印本中提取，并与NIMS SuperCon参考数据集高度一致（R²=0.96）。该数据库揭示了超导物理规律，并支持训练出高性能的超导临界温度与上临界场预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导材料有望推动核聚变、无损耗电子器件等下一代技术，但机器学习预测超导材料时，除临界温度外缺乏经过验证的实验训练数据。因此需要构建一个覆盖多参数、规模大且经过验证的超导实验数据库，以支撑物理规律发现与数据驱动的材料筛选。

Method: 作者构建了AutoSuperCon数据库：利用大语言模型（LLM）从超过47000篇arXiv预印本中自动提取8个超导参数，共得到6793种超导体的20354条记录；用NIMS SuperCon等参考数据集对提取结果进行交叉验证（临界温度R²=0.96）。基于该数据库，作者分析了常规超导体、铜氧化物等不同体系的能隙比、上临界场与泡利极限的关系、临界温度与超流密度的上限关系；并采用基于成分的预测器结合迁移学习训练临界温度（R²_log=0.83）和上临界场（R²_log=0.69）的模型。

Result: 分析显示：能隙比中位数从常规超导体的3.6升至铜氧化物超导体的6.4；28%的上临界场超过泡利极限；临界温度存在一个与超流密度成正比的上限。基于成分的机器学习模型对临界温度和上临界场分别达到R²_log=0.83和0.69，迁移学习进一步提升了性能；在已知超导家族内，仅凭成分即可同时筛选这两项参数。

Conclusion: AutoSuperCon数据库为数千种超导体提供了可靠的多参数实验数据，既揭示了超导物理规律（如能隙比分布、泡利极限超越比例、Tc与超流密度的上限关系），又使得仅凭成分即可在已知超导家族内高效筛选候选材料，从而推动数据驱动的超导材料发现。

Abstract: New superconductors could unlock next-generation technologies from nuclear fusion to lossless electronics. Machine learning can predict candidate superconductors but lacks validated experimental training data beyond the critical temperature. Here we present AutoSuperCon, a database of eight superconducting parameters, containing 20354 entries for 6793 superconductors. The data were extracted from over 47000 arXiv preprints using a large language model and validated against reference datasets ($R^2 = 0.96$ with NIMS SuperCon). Using AutoSuperCon, we show that median gap ratios rise from 3.6 in conventional superconductors to 6.4 in cuprates, 28% of upper critical fields exceed the Pauli limit, and critical temperatures lie below a ceiling proportional to the superfluid density. Further, we train composition-based predictors for the critical temperature ($R^2_{\log}=0.83$) and the upper critical field ($R^2_{\log}=0.69$), boosted by transfer learning. Within known superconducting families, composition alone is sufficient to screen candidates for both parameters. AutoSuperCon thus enables physical insight and data-driven discovery across thousands of superconductors.

</details>


### [154] [Band geometry, Anderson's theorem, and vortex nucleation in unconventional 2D superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15872)
*Vladislav Poliakov,Inti Sodemann Villadiego,T. Senthil*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究无序对具有非平庸能带几何的二维材料中超导的影响。研究表明，Anderson定理和Abrikosov-Gorkov无序致拆对理论需由正常态Bloch函数结构修正；将此结果应用于扭转MoTe2发现，其观测超导的BCS配对机制与实测正常态剩余电阻率存在张力。此外，对于手性时间反演破缺超导体，BCS机制下弱无序不会诱导涡旋玻璃相，这与任意子流体形成的超导形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受二维材料中手性超导体的近期发现启发，作者希望理解在具有非平庸能带几何的材料中，无序如何影响超导电性，尤其是考察传统Anderson定理与Abrikosov-Gorkov理论是否需要修正，以及无序能否在时间反演破缺的手性超导体中诱导涡旋相。

Method: 作者从正常态Bloch函数的结构出发，重新推导并修正Anderson定理和Abrikosov-Gorkov无序诱导拆对的描述；随后将这些理论结果应用于扭转MoTe2，并结合实验测量的正常态剩余电阻率检验其BCS配对机制；最后分析随机势无序能否在手性时间反演破缺超导体中成核超导涡旋。

Result: 研究表明：正常态Bloch函数会修正Anderson定理与Abrikosov-Gorkov理论；应用于扭转MoTe2显示，BCS配对机制与实测正常态剩余电阻率之间存在张力；在通常BCS机制稳定的超导体中，弱无序不会诱导涡旋玻璃相，这与任意子流体形成的超导行为形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 在非平庸能带几何材料中，无序对超导的影响需考虑正常态波函数结构的修正；扭转MoTe2中的超导若由BCS机制解释与实验电阻率数据矛盾，暗示可能存在其他配对机制；同时也说明仅BCS机制下无序不足以产生涡旋玻璃相，这为区分BCS超导与任意子流体超导提供了判据。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent discovery of chiral superconductors in 2d materials, we study the effects of disorder on superconductivity in materials with non-trivial band geometry. First, we show how Anderson's theorem and the conventional Abrikosov-Gorkov description of disorder-induced pair breaking are modified by the structure of the normal-state Bloch functions. We apply these results to twisted MoTe2 and show that a BCS pairing mechanism for the observed superconductor is in tension with the measured normal state residual resistivity. Second, we study whether random potential disorder can nucleate superconducting vortices in a chiral time- reversal-breaking superconductor. We show that, for a superconductor stabilized by the usual BCS mechanism, weak disorder does not induce a vortex glass phase, in contrast to superconductivity formed from an anyon fluid.

</details>


### [155] [Evidence of anisotropic surface state residual loss in UTe$_2$ amid isotropic low-energy excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16196)
*Arthur Carlton-Jones,Jared Z. Dans,Gicela Saucedo Salas,Shanta R. Saha,Johnpierre Paglione,Nicholas P. Butch,Steven M. Anlage*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过微波谐振腔微扰技术测量UTe₂单晶的复合表面阻抗，发现穿透深度与表面电阻在低温下均呈现二次、各向同性的幂律温度依赖，而剩余损耗则表现出明显的各向异性，该结果与弱散射下单对点节点的假设不符，并探讨了拓扑超导的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非常规超导体UTe₂的配对机制及其可能的拓扑性质，通过其对外加微波的感应通道（穿透深度）和电阻通道（表面电阻）的影响来刻画超导基态的低能激发。

Method: 采用谐振腔微扰技术，在微波频率下测量多种UTe₂单晶的复合表面阻抗，使感应通道与电阻通道具有可比性并可同等处理；通过分析大量谐振模式的复合电动力学行为，获得激发出基态的方向依赖性。

Result: 低温下穿透深度与表面电阻表现出相似的幂律温度依赖，且在多个模式聚合后均呈二次型且各向同性；而剩余损耗则呈现明显且系统的各向异性。该幂律结果与弱散射条件下费米面上单对点节点的图景不一致。

Conclusion: 各向同性的二次幂律温度依赖与各向异性剩余损耗并存的现象，提示UTe₂可能具有拓扑超导电性，而非简单的单对点节点配对图像。

Abstract: We explore the pairing mechanism and possible topological properties of the unconventional superconductor UTe$_2$ through their influence on both the inductive and resistive channels using resonant cavity perturbation techniques. We measure a composite surface impedance for a variety of UTe$_2$ single crystals at microwave frequencies, where the inductive and resistive channels are comparable and can be treated on equal footing. By studying the composite electrodynamics for a large number of resonant modes, we are able to provide a comprehensive picture of the directional dependence of the excitations out of the ground state. We find similar power-law temperature dependencies at low-temperature for both the penetration depth and surface resistance, which in aggregate across modes, are quadratic and isotropic. On the other hand, we find a distinct and systematic anisotropy of the residual loss. The power-law results are inconsistent with a single pair of point nodes on the Fermi surface under weak scattering. We discuss the possibility of topological superconductivity as an interpretation of these isotropic power-laws in conjunction with the anisotropic residual loss.

</details>


### [156] [Quantum geometry drives orbital paramagnetism in superconducting fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16387)
*Zi-Ting Sun,Ying-Ming Xie,Chunli Huang,Naoto Nagaosa*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用高斯涨落理论研究了转变温度以上的多分量超导体，发现涨落可产生顺磁响应而非传统的抗磁响应；顺磁项源于不同序参量分量间的干涉，与库珀对的量子几何相关，并在时间反演对称的kagome超导模型中占主导，而当轨道时间反演对称性破缺时抗磁性恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，超导体在转变温度以上由涨落诱导的轨道磁化率总是抗磁的（Meissner效应），并在接近转变时发散。本文旨在探索多分量超导体中是否存在超出这一常规图像的新机制，尤其是涨落能否产生顺磁响应，以及该响应如何反映库珀对的内部结构（量子几何）。

Method: 作者采用高斯涨落理论，研究转变温度以上的通用多分量超导体。将磁化响应分解为两项竞争贡献：库珀对质心运动的常规抗磁项，以及不同序参量分量间干涉产生的顺磁项。后者通过库珀对的量子几何（描述其内部分量如何随动量变化）来刻画。随后将该理论应用于具有两个手性配对模式的kagome超导体模型，分别考察时间反演对称保持与破缺（如弱环流序）两种情形。

Result: 计算表明，除常规抗磁项外，多分量序参量间的干涉会产生一个与量子几何相关的顺磁贡献。在时间反演对称保持的母态下，顺磁项可超过抗磁项，产生净的涨落顺磁性。当轨道时间反演对称性破缺（如形成弱环流序）时，两个手性模式的简并被解除，冷却过程中低能模式主导涨落，体系回归单分量行为，抗磁性重新占优。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种超导体中涨落顺磁性的新机制，并证明轨道磁响应可用于探测库珀对的量子几何。时间反演对称破缺导致的模式简并解除能够调控涨落的磁响应从顺磁转变为抗磁，为理解多分量超导体的磁性质提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Superconductors expel magnetic fields through the Meissner effect. Even slightly above the superconducting transition temperature, electrons can briefly form Cooper pairs, leading to an orbital diamagnetic susceptibility that diverges as the transition is approached. In this work, we use Gaussian fluctuation theory to study a generic multicomponent superconductor above its transition temperature and show that fluctuations can instead produce a paramagnetic response. We identify two competing contributions: the usual diamagnetic contribution from the center-of-mass motion of Cooper pairs and a paramagnetic contribution from interference between different components of the superconducting order parameter. The latter is related to pair quantum geometry, which describes how the internal components of the Cooper pair change with momentum. We then apply our theory to a model of a kagome superconductor with two chiral pairing modes. When the parent state preserves time-reversal symmetry, the paramagnetic contribution can outweigh the conventional diamagnetic contribution, producing net fluctuation paramagnetism. However, when the orbital time-reversal symmetry is broken, e.g., by forming a weak loop-current order, the degeneracy between the two chiral modes is then lifted. Upon cooling toward the superconducting transition, the lower-energy mode dominates the fluctuations, and diamagnetism wins as single-component physics takes over. Our results provide a mechanism for fluctuation paramagnetism in superconductors and show that the orbital magnetic response can probe the quantum geometry of Cooper pairs.

</details>


### [157] [Noise-Protected Superconducting Qudits from Higher-Harmonic Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16563)
*Hamed Vakili*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出利用约瑟夫森结电流-相位关系的二次谐波，在由三个约瑟夫森结组成的超导回路中实现强抗弛豫的qutrit（三能级量子位），并指出二次谐波的符号决定了具体设计方案，可用交变磁约瑟夫森结作为候选平台，由栅极电压调控该符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qudit比qubit能存储更多信息，但同qubit一样受噪声影响，须在容错量子计算前克服其噪声敏感性。作者希望利用二次谐波设计对弛豫（relaxation）有强保护、主要受退相干（dephasing）限制的超导qutrit。

Method: 利用电流-相位关系的二次谐波，在含三个约瑟夫森结的超导回路中构造受保护的qutrit。二次谐波为正时，采用结间有微小不对称的简单回路即可得到可调谐、对称保护（symmetry-protected）的qutrit；二次谐波为负时，通过加入微小对称破缺项（如弱约瑟夫森二极管效应）实现。并通过微观计算证明栅极电压可调节二次谐波的符号，识别交变磁（altermagnetic）约瑟夫森结为候选平台。

Result: 两种设计方案存在不同权衡：方正二次谐波方案具有精确的电荷选择定则；负二次谐波方案几乎不受电荷噪声影响，且其准粒子错误可被纠正。两者都要求较大的二次谐波，交变磁约瑟夫森结可作为候选平台。微观计算表明栅极电压选择这些结中二次谐波的符号，但栅极电压噪声随之成为退相干的主要来源。

Conclusion: 二次谐波的符号可作为设计受保护超导qudit的设计原则；具体选择哪种方案取决于所使用的材料。

Abstract: Qudits, which generalize qubits to $d$ levels, store more information per circuit element than qubits. As with qubits, however, their sensitivity to noise must be overcome before they can be used for fault-tolerant quantum computing. Here we show that, by exploiting the second harmonic of the current--phase relation, a superconducting loop of three Josephson junctions can host qutrits strongly protected against relaxation, while dephasing limits their coherence. When the second harmonic is positive, a simple loop with a small asymmetry between the junctions provides a tunable, symmetry-protected qutrit. When it is negative, a protected qutrit can be realized by adding a small symmetry-breaking term, such as a weak Josephson diode effect. The two designs involve different trade-offs: the first has an exact charge selection rule, whereas the second is nearly insensitive to charge noise and its quasiparticle errors can be corrected. The preferable choice therefore depends on the materials used. Both require a sizable second harmonic to begin with, and we identify altermagnetic Josephson junctions as a candidate platform. Finally, microscopic calculations show that a gate voltage selects the sign of the second harmonic in these junctions, although gate-voltage noise then becomes the leading source of dephasing. These findings establish the sign of the second harmonic as a design principle for protected superconducting qudits.

</details>


### [158] [Sign changing pairing in single layer FeSe/SrTiO3 revealed by nonmagnetic impurity bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16679)
*Huimin Zhang,Zhuozhi Ge,Michael Weinert,Lian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用扫描隧道显微镜/谱学（STM/S）研究了分子束外延生长的单层FeSe/SrTiO₃，发现Fe空位缺陷诱导的隙内束缚态具有电子-空穴空间不对称性（空穴态二重对称、电子态四重对称），且在外磁场下无能量移动或劈裂，支持符号反转（sign-changing）配对序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层FeSe/SrTiO₃高温超导的配对对称性究竟是常规的符号保持s波还是非常规的符号反转对称，一直是争论焦点，需要实验直接探明其序参量结构。

Method: 采用分子束外延（MBE）生长单层FeSe/SrTiO₃，并用扫描隧道显微镜/谱学（STM/S）探测Fe面内非磁性Fe空位缺陷诱导的隙内束缚态，同时施加外磁场观察其响应。

Result: 观测到非磁性Fe空位缺陷产生的稳健隙内束缚态，其空间分布呈现强烈的电子-空穴不对称性：空穴态具有二重对称性，电子态具有四重对称性；这些束缚态在外加磁场下不出现能量移动或劈裂。

Conclusion: 束缚态在磁场下无移动或劈裂与符号反转的序参量一致，为单层FeSe/SrTiO₃的非传统符号反转配对提供了重要实验证据。

Abstract: The discovery of high-temperature superconductivity in single layer FeSe epitaxially grown on SrTiO3(001) substrates has instigated extensive debate over whether its pairing symmetry is conventional sign-preserving s-wave or unconventional sign-changing. Here we probe the pairing state for single layer FeSe/SrTiO3 grown by molecular beam epitaxy using scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy. We observe robust in-gap bound states induced by non-magnetic Fe-vacancy defects within the Fe-plane, which exhibit strong spatial electron-hole asymmetry with two-fold symmetry in hole states and four-fold in electron states. The bound states exhibit no energy shift or splitting under an applied magnetic field, consistent with a sign-changing order parameter.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

### [159] [High-Fidelity In Silico Dataset of Ordered Binary and Ternary MXenes with Predictive Synthesis Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14687)
*Ahmed H. Biby,Charles B. Musgrave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文利用 r$^2$SCAN 泛函的 DFT 计算构建了 1,040 种有序二元/三元剥离态 MXene 的高保真数据集，计算其形成能、分解吉布斯能等热力学量，揭示 MXene 在平衡条件下均不可得，但高温、高压或特定碳化学势下部分可稳定合成，为数据驱动的 MXene 发现奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MXene 的结构与组分空间极其庞大，且缺乏考虑合成条件的可靠稳定性描述符，导致新型 MXene 的靶向合成十分困难，亟需系统性的热力学稳定性数据与合成策略指导。

Method: 采用 r$^2$SCAN 泛函的密度泛函理论（DFT），构建包含 1,040 种有序二元和三元无支撑剥离态 MXene 的数据集，涵盖单过渡金属 MXene、面内有序双过渡金属 MXene、面内有序金属-双空位 MXene 和面外有序双过渡金属 MXene。对每种结构计算形成吉布斯能、分解吉布斯能、分解产物、层对称群及凸包上方能量，并考察高温（500–2000 K）、高压（10 GPa）及碳化学势窗口下的稳定性。

Result: 结果显示所有 MXene 在平衡条件下均热力学不可达；其中 115 种（以碳化物为主）在高温（500–2000 K）下趋于热力学稳定，23 种在 10 GPa 高压下稳定；此外，Mn–Fe–C 化学空间中的 3 种 MXene 在实验可行的碳化学势范围内可稳定存在。研究还揭示了 MXene 空间内的稳定性趋势。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可合成候选物及其对应合成条件，弥合了 MXene 发现中数据驱动方法的关键缺口，并为发展机器学习稳定性描述符及未来自主实验工作流奠定了基础。

Abstract: Despite their promise, the targeted synthesis of novel MXenes remains challenging due to their vast structural and compositional space and the lack of reliable stability descriptors that account for synthesis conditions. To address this gap, we utilized density functional theory (DFT) with the r$^2$SCAN functional to develop a high-fidelity dataset of 1,040 ordered binary and ternary bare delaminated MXenes, including mono-transition metal MXenes, in-plane ordered double-transition metal MXenes, in-plane ordered metal-divacancy MXenes, and out-of-plane ordered double-transition-metal MXenes. For each structure, we calculated formation and decomposition Gibbs energies, decomposition products, layer symmetry groups, and energies above the hull. We find that all MXenes are thermodynamically inaccessible under equilibrium conditions. However, 115 predominantly carbide MXenes become thermodynamically stabilized at elevated temperatures (500--2000 K), and 23 are stabilized under high pressure (10 GPa). Additionally, three MXenes in the Mn--Fe--C chemical space are stabilized within an experimentally feasible range of carbon chemical potentials. These findings reveal stability trends across the MXene space and identify synthesis strategies that can guide experimental efforts. Our dataset provides a robust foundation for developing machine-learned stability descriptors and for guiding experimental efforts toward novel MXene discovery. By identifying synthesizable candidates along with their synthesis conditions, this work bridges a critical gap in data-driven approaches to MXene discovery and lays the foundation for future autonomous experimental workflows.

</details>


### [160] [Improving Nonequilibrium Thermal Conduction in CaF2 by Breaking the Phonon Bottleneck Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14957)
*Mingde Qu,Minghuan Song,Yuxing Wang,Wudi Wang,Chenbo Zhang,Jiamin Quan,Jun Xu,Zhongwei Zhang,Jie Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出通过选择性激发横声学（TA）声子来打破CaF2中由纵向光学（LO）声子输运/弛豫低效所导致的非平衡声子瓶颈，从而改善激光光学材料的散热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CaF2中热电子能量快速传递给弱传播的LO声子，形成非平衡声子瓶颈，限制高效散热；需要寻找打破瓶颈、提升热导的策略。

Method: 结合电子-声子散射计算、声子玻尔兹曼输运分析与非平衡分子动力学模拟，识别优先接收热载流子能量的LO声子；借助三声子散射相空间分析，选择性激发TA声子以加速LO弛豫并促进能量向载热声学模式转移。

Result: 持续LO声子激发使热导率下降约12%；在此基础上额外激发TA声子可使热导率相比LO瓶颈受限状态提高约21%，并超过未激发时的数值。

Conclusion: 调控声子支间能量再分配可抵消声子瓶颈引起的热量积聚，为通过选择性控制声子布居实现激光光学材料的主动热管理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Heat dissipation in laser optical materials depends not only on their thermal conductivity but also on how absorbed energy is redistributed among various phonon modes. In calcium fluoride (CaF2), rapid energy transfer from hot electrons to weakly propagating longitudinal optical (LO) phonons can create a nonequilibrium phonon bottleneck effect that limits efficient heat removal. Here, we propose selective phonon excitation as an effective strategy to break this bottleneck. Combining electron-phonon scattering calculations, phonon Boltzmann transport analysis, and nonequilibrium molecular dynamics simulations, we identify LO phonons as the primary modes that preferentially receive energy from hot carriers, leading to energy accumulation due to their inefficient transport and relaxation. Guided by three-phonon scattering phase-space analysis, selective excitation of transverse acoustic (TA) modes can accelerate LO relaxation and promote energy transfer toward heat-carrying acoustic modes. While sustained LO-phonon excitation reduces the thermal conductivity by approximately 12%, additional TA-mode excitation improves heat conduction by approximately 21% compared to the state limited by the LO-phonon bottleneck, surpassing the unexcited value. These findings demonstrate that controlling interbranch energy redistribution can counteract phonon-bottleneck-induced heat accumulation and reveal a working route for active thermal management in laser optical materials through selective control of phonon populations.

</details>


### [161] [Reversal of the Anomalous Hall Effect by Directional In-Plane Electric Field in Strained Bilayer MoO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14982)
*Wenpeng Wang,hang Shi,Xiangru Kong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了应变双层MoO中，通过面内电场方向调控谷选择性自旋劈裂与反常霍尔电导可逆翻转的现象，并揭示了AC1堆叠下应变驱动的丰富物性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状磁性材料因同时具备可调的谷与自旋自由度，被视为实现多功能自旋电子器件的理想平台。然而，如何在不依赖外部磁场的情况下，实现对单谷自旋劈裂的选择性操控以及反常霍尔响应的可逆翻转，仍是一个亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: 作者以应变双层MoO为研究对象，重点考察其AC1与AC2两种堆叠构型。通过引入双轴应变诱导Dirac型能带交叉，并施加沿a、b方向的面内电场，结合第一性原理计算分析谷简并度、Berry曲率分布及反常霍尔电导的变化。

Result: 研究发现，在能量与动力学均稳定的AC2堆叠中，沿a方向电场选择性解除X谷的自旋简并而保持Y谷简并，沿b方向则效果相反，实现了单谷自旋劈裂的选择性切换。该切换伴随符号相反的Berry曲率重分布，导致反常霍尔电导可逆翻转。AC1堆叠虽不具备该效应，但展现出金属-拓扑绝缘体转变、量子化自旋霍尔电导、谷极化及压磁效应等丰富的应变驱动现象。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了应变与电场协同调控谷选择性自旋劈裂及反常霍尔响应的机制，表明双层MoO是构建多功能自旋电子与谷电子器件的极具前景的平台。

Abstract: Layered magnetic materials with tunable valley and spin degrees of freedom hold promise for multifunctional spintronic devices. Here, we demonstrate that the orientation of an in-plane electric field enables selective switching of single-valley spin splitting and reversal of the anomalous Hall conductivity in strained bilayer MoO. In particular, electric fields along the a and b directions selectively lift the spin degeneracy at the X and Y valleys, respectively, while preserving it at the other valley. This valley-selective switching is accompanied by a redistribution of Berry curvature with opposite signs, giving rise to a reversible anomalous Hall response. These effects are realized in the energetically and dynamically stable AC2 stacking configuration, where biaxial strain induces Dirac-like band crossings that enable subsequent electric-field control. Meanwhile, the AC1 stacking configuration, which is also energetically and dynamically stable, exhibits rich strain-driven phenomena, including a transition from metal to topological insulator with quantized spin Hall conductivity, as well as valley polarization and piezomagnetism. Our findings reveal a combined effect of strain and electric fields for controlling valley-selective spin splitting and anomalous Hall responses, highlighting bilayer MoO as a promising platform for multifunctional spintronic and valleytronic devices.

</details>


### [162] [Depth determination from core-loss 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15037)
*Michael Deimetry*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用动量分辨的芯损失四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D-STEM）数据来确定掺杂原子三维深度分布的方法，并通过贝叶斯框架在极低芯损失计数下实现纳米至亚纳米深度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越样品投影显微图、确定掺杂原子的三维分布对材料表征与开发至关重要；传统STEM图像为二维投影，无法直接给出掺杂原子沿深度方向的位置。

Method: 在Born近似下，对探针位置做傅里叶变换，芯损失4D-STEM数据会产生干涉条纹，其频率编码了掺杂原子相对焦平面的距离；针对实际实验的两大挑战——动力学散射降低条纹衬度（可通过偏离主晶带轴采集部分缓解）、芯损失剂量惩罚性高——采用贝叶斯框架进行深度推断。

Result: 模拟表明，仅需100至1000个芯损失计数（视信背比而定）即可实现纳米至亚纳米级深度精度。

Conclusion: 结合动量分辨芯损失4D-STEM与贝叶斯推断，可在极低剂量下实现对掺杂原子三维深度分布的高精度测定，为材料表征提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Moving beyond specimen-projected micrographs in scanning transmission electron microscopy to determine the three-dimensional distribution of dopants is of vital importance for materials characterization and development. We present an approach for dopant depth determination using momentum-resolved core-loss four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy data. Within the Born approximation, Fourier transformation over probe position reveals fringes whose frequency encodes the dopant's distance from the focal plane. Two principal challenges arise in extending this approach to realistic experimental conditions. First, dynamical scattering degrades fringe contrast which can be partially mitigated by acquiring off major zone axes. Second, because core-loss doses are punishing, we use a Bayesian framework which, in simulation, achieves nanometre to sub-nanometre depth precision from only 100 to 1000 core-loss counts, depending on the signal-to-background.

</details>


### [163] [Defective $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15060)
*Jorge Vega Bazantes,Timo Lebeda,Zhiqiang Mao,Jianwei Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论结合meta-GGA泛函，研究了块体α-In₂Se₃的电子结构及中性点缺陷（空位与反位缺陷）在富Se和富In生长条件下的形成能，发现中间层Se空位具有异常低的形成能，并揭示了其稳定性源于邻近In原子的局部弛豫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-In₂Se₃是一种具有铁电应用前景的二维范德华材料，但原生缺陷会显著影响其结构和电子性质，因此需要系统研究其缺陷行为。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论，使用先进的meta广义梯度近似（meta-GGA）交换关联泛函，计算块体α-In₂Se₃的电子结构，并在富Se和富In两种化学条件下研究中性质点缺陷（空位与反位缺陷）的形成能。

Result: 计算复现了原始材料的实验结构参数；发现中间层Se空位在两种化学环境下均表现出异常低的形成能。结构分析表明，邻近的四面体配位In原子沿三重轴发生显著的局部弛豫，配位数由4降为3时仍保持C₃ᵥ对称性，为该缺陷的显著稳定性提供了微观解释。

Conclusion: 中间层Se空位因其邻近In原子的对称性保持型局部弛豫而具有热力学稳定性，是α-In₂Se₃中一种重要的原生缺陷，对理解该材料的缺陷物理及铁电应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ is a two-dimensional van der Waals material with promising applications involving ferroelectricity. However, the presence of native defects can significantly influence its structural and electronic properties. In this work, we employ density functional theory with a state-of-the-art meta-generalized gradient approximation (meta-GGA) exchange-correlation functional to investigate the electronic structure of bulk $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ and the formation of neutral point defects, including vacancies and antisites, under Se-rich and In-rich growth conditions. Our calculations reproduce the experimental structural parameters of the pristine material and reveal that the Se vacancy in the middle layer exhibits an unexpectedly low formation energy under both chemical environments. Structural analysis shows that the neighboring tetrahedrally coordinated In atom undergoes a substantial local relaxation along the three-fold axis, which preserves its $C_{3v}$ site symmetry even as its coordination is reduced from four to three, providing a microscopic explanation for the remarkable stability of this defect.

</details>


### [164] [Brute-force molecular dynamics simulation of aging in amorphous Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15664)
*Elisa Petroni,Dario Baratella,Omar Abou El Kheir,Andrea Redaelli,Marco Bernasconi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文利用机器学习原子间势，在略低于玻璃转变温度下对非晶Ge₂Sb₂Te₅进行约100 ns的老化分子动力学模拟，发现老化过程中轴向键的Peierls畸变增强，并据此关联到DFT计算中电子带隙的展宽；同时观察到原子迁移率显著下降和键寿命增加，但未发现同极键的减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硫系相变合金（如Ge₂Sb₂Te₅）的非晶相会随时间发生老化，导致电阻升高，损害相变存储器件的工作性能。由于老化过程涉及的时间尺度远超常规从头算分子动力学的可及范围，需要借助机器学习势才能在接近玻璃转变温度下、于纳秒尺度上直接模拟老化相关的结构弛豫并阐明其微观机制。

Method: 作者采用机器学习原子间势进行分子动力学模拟：先在400 K下平衡24 ns，随后在略低于玻璃转变温度的温度下进行约100 ns的老化模拟。通过分析模拟轨迹中的结构演化（如轴向键的Peierls畸变、同极键、原子迁移率、键寿命），并结合密度泛函理论（DFT）对较小的老化非晶模型计算电子带隙，将结构变化与电子性质联系起来。

Result: 老化过程中观察到轴向键的Peierls畸变增强，这是该类合金的典型结构特征，并与DFT在更小老化非晶模型中计算得到的电子带隙展宽相关联。同时，原子迁移率明显下降、键寿命增加。但模拟中未观察到同极键的减少——尽管理想老化玻璃中同极键应消失，其消除需要在更长的时间尺度上才能实现。

Conclusion: 通过机器学习势实现的长时间尺度模拟揭示了非晶Ge₂Sb₂Te₅老化初期的结构弛豫机制：Peierls畸变增强导致带隙展宽，解释了电阻升高现象，同时伴随原子迁移率降低与键寿命增长。同极键的消除尚未发生，表明完全老化需要远超100 ns的时间尺度，这为理解相变存储器中非晶相的老化行为提供了微观依据。

Abstract: Chalcogenide alloys such as the prototypical Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ compound are used in phase change electronic memories due to their ability to undergo a fast and reversible transition between the amorphous and crystalline phases upon heating. The amorphous phase is subject to aging that leads to an increase in the electrical resistance, which is detrimental to the operation of the devices. Using a machine learning interatomic potential, we have investigated the structural relaxations responsible for aging in amorphous Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ in molecular dynamics simulations at temperatures slightly below the glass transition. The aging simulation, lasting about 100 ns, follows an equilibration of 24 ns at 400 K. During aging we observed an enhancement of the Peierls distortion in axial bonds which is a typical feature of these alloys that we relate to the widening of the electronic band gap computed within Density Functional Theory in smaller aged amorphous models. We also observed a marked reduction of the atomic mobility with an increase of the bond lifetimes during aging. However, we do not see a reduction in homopolar bonds, which are expected to be absent in the ideal aged glass and which should be removed on a much longer time scale.

</details>


### [165] [High-Throughput Surface Screening Reveals Energetically Competitive Low-Symmetry Facets in Wurtzite Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15138)
*Gustavo Madrigal,Cibrán López,Jordi Llorca,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一套"自动表面生成 + 机器学习势筛选 + 密度泛函理论"的组合方法，系统研究了纤锌矿 GaN、LaN 和 ZnO 的 226 个化学计量干净表面模型（Miller 指数 |h|,|k|,|l|≤3）。研究发现高指数低对称棱柱面在表面形成能上与传统的 m 面、a 面具有竞争力，但相似的表面能并不对应相似的能带对齐，不同晶面的电离势和电子亲和势差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤锌矿半导体的电子器件、传感和太阳能燃料转换性能都由其表面和界面决定。此前第一性原理研究主要集中在高对称性的低指数晶面，原因是模拟低对称高指数表面的计算成本高且建模复杂。然而，表面形成能本质上由平面原子密度和断裂键强度决定，这并不排除高 Miller 指数表面在能量上具有竞争力。因此需要系统探索高指数晶面的稳定性与电子结构。

Method: 作者将表面模型自动生成、机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potential）快速筛选和高精度密度泛函理论（DFT）计算三者结合，对纤锌矿 GaN、LaN、ZnO 的 226 个干净、化学计量的表面模型进行了系统研究。这些模型覆盖所有满足 |h|,|k|,|l|≤3 的 Miller 取向及其不等价终止面，并进一步采用杂化泛函（hybrid-functional）计算评估真空参考下的能带边缘位置（电离势与电子亲和势）。

Result: 研究发现，低对称高指数棱柱面在能量上可与传统的 m 面和 a 面相竞争：十五个最低能量的非极性表面中有九个是高指数面，三种化合物各自最优的五个候选表面的形成能仅比最小表面能高 2.5%–5.3%。但杂化泛函计算显示，这种能量的接近并不等同于电子能带对齐的相似。在 GaN 中，两个最稳定表面的形成能仅相差 0.08 J/m²，但真空参考的能带边缘位置相差约 0.8 eV；在 LaN 中，近乎简并的 m 面和 a 面的能带边缘位置相差 0.16 eV。这表明形成能相近的纤锌矿晶面可能具有迥异的电离势和电子亲和势。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了对纤锌矿实际可能出现的各类晶面进行稳定性和能带对齐的联合、系统评估，并绘制了候选表面图谱，为相关纳米结构和异质界面的理性设计提供了依据。研究成果揭示了单纯依赖表面形成能筛选晶面是不够的，必须同时考虑各晶面的真空能级对齐特性。

Abstract: Surfaces and interfaces govern the performance of wurtzite semiconductors in electronics, sensing, and solar-fuel conversion. To date, first-principles studies have focused mostly on high-symmetry low-index facets, owing to the computational cost and complexity of modelling low-symmetry high-index surfaces. In practice, however, surface formation energy is determined by the planar atomic density and the strength of the bonds severed upon cleavage, neither of which inherently excludes high-Miller-index surfaces from being energetically competitive. Here, we combine automated surface generation, machine-learning interatomic potential screening, and density functional theory to explore 226 clean, stoichiometric surface models of wurtzite GaN, LaN, and ZnO, spanning all Miller orientations with $|h|,|k|,|l| \le 3$ and their inequivalent terminations. We find that low-symmetry high-index prismatic facets are energetically competitive with respect to the conventional $m$- and $a$-planes: nine of the fifteen lowest-energy nonpolar surfaces are high-index, and the five best candidates of each compound lie within only 2.5-5.3% of the minimum surface energy. Hybrid-functional calculations reveal, however, that this energetic proximity does not translate into similar electronic alignment. In GaN, the two most stable surfaces differ by just 0.08 J m$^{-2}$ in formation energy but by almost 0.8 eV in vacuum-referenced band-edge positions, whereas in LaN the nearly degenerate $m$- and $a$-planes are separated by 0.16 eV. Wurtzite facets with similar formation energies may therefore exhibit very different ionization potentials and electron affinities. Our results enable the joint, systematic assessment of stability and band alignment across the facets that can actually occur in wurtzites, and provide a map of candidate surfaces for the rational design of related nanostructure and heterointerfaces.

</details>


### [166] [Probing Unknown Luttinger-Like Parameters and Band Structure in BAs: A 24-Band k.p and DFT Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15186)
*Mohsen Jemai,Nouha Mastour,Omar Zitouni,Tarek Barhoumi,Said Ridene*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用24带k.p理论和密度泛函理论（DFT）计算并分析了砷化硼（BAs）p型导带（价带）的电子能带结构及其未知的Luttinger类参数，结果表明24带k.p模型能高效、准确地复现BAs的电子性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BAs半导体材料的部分Luttinger类能带参数未知，且需要验证24带k.p模型能否准确复现其电子结构，同时评估其相对于DFT的计算效率，以期为后续复杂纳米结构和量子阱建模提供可靠高效的理论工具。

Method: 作者采用24带k.p模型和DFT两种方法计算BAs的电子能带结构。DFT计算使用CP2K代码中实现的高斯基组与平面波基组，并将24带k.p模型的能带结构及关键带参数与DFT结果和已有实验数据进行对比；同时对24带k.p哈密顿量参数进行调节，并将有效质量和带参数与已发表的理论研究进行校验。

Result: 结果表明，在所研究的能量范围内，24带k.p模型能准确复现BAs的电子能带结构和关键能带参数，并与DFT及已有实验结果吻合；调节后的有效质量和带参数也得到先前理论研究的验证。

Conclusion: 24带k.p方法在计算上比DFT更高效，且精度可靠，适合用于基于BAs半导体的复杂纳米结构和量子阱的建模。

Abstract: In this work, the electronic band structure and the unknown Luttinger-like parameters associated with the p-type conduction bands (CBs) of boron arsenide (BAs) are calculated and analyzed using both 24-band $k.p$ and density functional (DFT) theories. For the DFT study, Gaussian and plane-wave basis sets implemented in the CP2K code were used. Comparison with DFT and available experimental results shows that the 24-band k.p model accurately reproduces the known electronic properties of BAs. These results demonstrate that the 24-band k.p model accurately reproduces the electronic band structure and key band parameters over the investigated energy range. In addition, the 24-band $k.p$ Hamiltonian parameters are adjusted and we show that the effective masses and the band parameters are validated against previously published theoretical studies. These findings suggest that the $k.p$ method is computationally more efficient than DFT, making it suitable for modeling complex nanostructures and quantum wells based on BAs semiconductor.

</details>


### [167] [Metric random tilings: exact entropy and the diffraction of counted disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16730)
*Xinyan Wu,Qingqing Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出"度量随机镶嵌"（metric random tilings）概念，研究拓扑锁定、边长在精确面闭合条件下波动的周期性网络，并通过计数引擎枚举开普勒的11种均匀网络，量化其度量熵，展示度量无序对衍射的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机镶嵌理论描述晶体与准晶体的热力学时，计数的是"排列"而非"几何"，度量仍由化学设计固定、由衍射测量。作者希望把"度量（几何本身）"纳入精确计数范式，研究拓扑锁定但边长可波动的周期性网络中的度量无序。

Method: 引入度量随机镶嵌：拓扑锁定、边长在精确面闭合约束下波动的周期性网络；构建专门的计数引擎枚举开普勒的11种均匀网络，并将其分为零熵与正度量熵两类；对蜂窝网络（最密载体）分析所有边长值域，通过显式构造验证熵，并用Debye–Waller定律分析Bragg强度的相干分数随值域的变化。

Result: 11种均匀网络被划分为7个零熵网络和4个正度量熵载体。在蜂窝网络上，所有边长值域均携带广延熵，其中算术类达到每瓦片 s=0.544 kB（拟合带0.53–0.58），另两类约0.2。平移增大值域时熵严格不变，而固定斑块尺寸下Bragg强度的相干分数从9×10⁻⁴变到0.58，遵循振幅流的Debye–Waller定律。

Conclusion: 度量无序进入了精确计数范式，可在真实材料中通过被"度量光晕"包裹的Bragg斑点加以识别；度量随机镶嵌为理解拓扑锁定网络中几何涨落的热力学与衍射特性提供了新框架。

Abstract: Tiling mathematics has twice become the language of a state of matter: crystals, then quasicrystals, whose thermodynamics are described by the random tiling of fixed shapes.
